Zhao,M. -Y. Zhuang*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文讨论了当前星系形成理论框架的局限性，强调需要高红移宇宙的专门观测来解决五个关键开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管近年来取得了显著进展，但目前仍缺乏一个全面且可预测的星系形成理论框架。

Method: 基于宇宙学初始条件的数值模拟，结合亚格子模型和近似方法。

Result: 当前模型无法先验地准确预测星系的关键性质，如结构、大小、恒星形成历史、化学丰度模式或超大质量黑洞质量。

Conclusion: 星系形成的理论进展从根本上依赖于观测数据，并通过模型预测与实证结果的迭代检验来推动。

Abstract: Although significant progress has been made over recent decades, a comprehensive and predictive framework for galaxy formation remains elusive. Starting from cosmological initial conditions, current models cannot a priori accurately predict key galaxy properties such as their structure, size, star formation histories, chemical abundance patterns, or supermassive black hole mass. This challenge stems from the vast range of spatial scales involved, as numerical simulations must simultaneously account for sub-AU processes nearby stars and black holes in addition to Mpc-scale cosmological gas inflows/outflows and environmental effects. Consequently, simulations must rely on subgrid models and approximations. As a result, theoretical progress in galaxy formation remains fundamentally driven by observational data and the iterative testing of model predictions against empirical results. Here we highlight five key open problems in theoretical galaxy formation that require dedicated observations of the high-redshift Universe, beyond what can currently be achieved through GO programs.

</details>


### [2] [DMAJOR: A JWST Legacy Survey of Protoplanetary Disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10103)
*Benjamin Pope,Yinuo Han,Sean Andrews,Kateryna Andrych,Phil Armitage,William Balmer,Myriam Benisty,Faustine Cantalloube,Max Charles,Ilse Cleeves,Ian Czekala,Louis Desdoigts,Ruobing Dong,Rodrigo Ferrer-Chavez,Katherine Follette,Christian Ginski,Jonah Goldfine,Hayden Greer,Cassandra Hall,Sasha Hinkley,Haochang Jiang,Devika Kamath,Jens Kammerer,Matthew Kenworthy,Milo Langker,Kellen Lawson,Nienke van der Marel,Sebastián Marino,Maksym Mohorian,Christophe Pinte,Daniel Price,Laurent Pueyo,Shrishmoy Ray,Nour Skaf,Floris van der Tak,Peter Tuthill,Miguel Vioque,Jason Wang,Shangjia Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: DMAJOR是一项利用JWST开展的红外成像巡天计划，旨在对不同银河系环境中的原行星盘进行高分辨率、高稳定性观测，以揭示其三维结构、小颗粒与冰的分布、散射光结构与大尺度环境的关系，并发现新伴星。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解原行星盘在不同星系环境下的结构演化及其与行星形成的关系，弥补地面观测在灵敏度和分辨率上的不足。

Method: 利用JWST的高稳定性、高灵敏度和高分辨率，结合时间序列重访策略探测变异性，并采用基于模型的数据分析、遥测数据与相互校准提升数据质量、降低望远镜开销。

Result: 构建一个统一、基准性的原行星盘样本，实现对小颗粒与冰的空间分布、散射光结构与环境关联性的直接测绘，并识别新的伴星系统。

Conclusion: DMAJOR将为原行星盘的结构、动力学与环境依赖性提供关键观测约束，推动行星形成理论的发展。

Abstract: We propose an infrared imaging survey of resolved protoplanetary disks spanning a range of galactic environments, directly mapping the 3D structure of planet-forming disks across the IR. DMAJOR (Disk Motions and Architectures JWST Observational Reference survey), our legacy survey, will deliver a benchmark sample to reveal structure and connection to environment. The exquisite stability of JWST allows us to probe for variability with a time-scheduled revisit strategy, while its sensitivity and resolution open a window on disks unobservable from the ground. A uniform sample will reveal how small grains and ice are distributed in protoplanetary disks, how scattered light structures relate to large-scale environmental features, and identify new companions. To achieve this, DMAJOR takes advantage of model-based data analysis using telemetry and mutual calibration to enhance processed data products while minimizing telescope overheads.

</details>


### [3] [Satellite Streaks in HETDEX: Spectra, Orbital Identification, and Survey Impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10301)
*Erin Mentuch Cooper,Karl Gebhardt,Dustin Davis,Daniel J. Farrow,Barbara G. Castanheira,Gavin Dalton,Gary J. Hill,Stephen C. Hummel,Chenxu Liu,Shiro Mukae,Shun Saito,Donald P. Schneider,Sarah Tuttle*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文构建了HETDEX巡天PDR1数据中的卫星轨迹光谱数据库，分析了527条轨迹的轨道类型、亮度特性及光谱特征，揭示了空间碎片污染趋势及其对天文观测的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着近地轨道卫星数量激增，其轨迹对天文观测（尤其是光谱巡天）造成日益严重的污染，亟需系统性识别、分类与量化影响。

Method: 基于HETDEX PDR1的6778次观测数据，检测并提取卫星轨迹；结合TLE星表进行轨道识别与分类；通过测光与光谱分析，校正曝光时间效应，计算瞬时与平均星等，并归一化比较不同轨道目标亮度。

Result: 发现527条轨迹，其中90.1%可匹配已编目天体；低轨与地球静止轨道目标在曝光平均星等相近（~16.5 mag），但瞬时星等差异达5.6 mag；Starlink轨迹归一化亮度g(550km)=5.95，与VisorSat设计一致；光谱显示为反射太阳光，斜率差异由信噪比主导。

Conclusion: HETDEX数据揭示了卫星轨迹污染的严重性与演化趋势（月污染率超20%），证实其对宽视场光谱巡天构成实质性干扰；同时展示了大口径望远镜对暗弱空间目标的探测能力，并为后续观测策略与减缓措施提供关键实证依据。

Abstract: We present a spectroscopic database and analysis of satellite streaks in Public Data Release 1 (PDR1) from the Hobby-Eberly Telescope Dark Energy Experiment (HETDEX), covering 6778 observations and $\sim$90 deg$^2$ of sky from January 2017 to July 2024. We detect 527 streaks across 492 observations, a contamination fraction of 7.3% that rises to monthly values above 20% by 2023--2024, although the streaks mask only 0.09% of the survey area. Of the 527 streaks, 475 (90.1%) are identified with a cataloged object using archival two-line elements: 258 (54%) lie in low or Earth orbit and 217 (46%) are geostationary or highly elliptical. Only 35% were launched since 2010; the rest are spent rocket bodies, debris, and older spacecraft. This reflects HETDEX's sensitivity to faint objects and its 2017--2024 observing baseline. The Hobby-Eberly Telescope's large 10\,m aperture and HETDEX's $\sim$6\,minute exposures result in similar observed magnitudes for low Earth orbit (LEO) and geostationary Earth orbit (GEO) objects (median $g_{\rm AB}=16.2\pm1.1$ for LEO, $16.7\pm1.1$ for GEO). However, once transit time is corrected for, instantaneous magnitudes differ widely: $g_{\rm inst}=7.4$ for LEO versus $g_{\rm inst}=13.0$ for GEO objects, a difference of 5.6\,mag, while the exposure-averaged magnitudes differ by only 0.5 mag. Normalized to 550 km, the 34 Starlink streaks give $g(550 {\rm km})=5.95$, consistent with the mean $V(550\,{\rm km})=5.92\pm0.04$ reported for VisorSat-design spacecraft. The spectra are consistent with reflected sunlight, with modest, signal-to-noise-driven differences in slope. The catalog, spectra, and code are archived at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22962479.

</details>


### [4] [Foreshortening of Polarizer Angles in Wide Field Polarizing Imagers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11159)
*Ritesh Patel,J. Marcus Hughes,C. E. DeForest,Daniel B. Seaton,Chris Lowder,Matthew J. West*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文分析了PUNCH任务中宽视场成像仪（WFI）因大视场导致的偏振片角度投影畸变问题，量化了其对线偏振测量精度的影响，并讨论了该效应在广域偏振成像技术中的普遍意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PUNCH任务需精确测量日冕与日球层的线偏振，但其WFI的90度超大视场导致偏振片角度在像面上发生显著投影畸变，偏离标称值，影响偏振测量精度。

Method: 采用简单的几何建模方法，估算WFI视场内不同位置像素所感知的偏振片有效角度变化，并分析该变化对偏振亮度测量的影响及光学畸变对角度位置的进一步偏移。

Result: 发现±60度和0度偏振片的有效角度在视场内分别变化±2.5度和±3.5度；该畸变会系统性影响偏振亮度计算，且光学畸变会进一步改变畸变角度的空间分布。

Conclusion: 大视场偏振成像中偏振片角度的投影畸变不可忽略，必须在数据处理中建模校正；该问题对类似IMAX 3D等广域偏振技术也具重要启示。

Abstract: The Polarimeter to Unify Corona and Heliosphere (PUNCH) is a very wide field polarizing imager covering a 90 degree diameter region of the sky, stitched together from four individual fields of view (FOVs), three of which are supported by Wide Field Imagers (WFIs) with 40 degree diameter individual FOVs. The science objective requires precise measurement of linear polarization. The WFI FOV is sufficiently wide that polarizer angles are foreshortened by projection effects relative to the line of sight of each pixel in the instrument, deviating from their nominal angular positions of -60, 0, and +60 degree relative to the instrument frame of reference. These angles are foreshortened by the very wide WFI field of view, changing their effective angle across the image plane - an effect that is analogous to similar challenges of polarimetric right/left channel separation in large-format 3D cinema, such as the IMAX format. Using a simple geometrical approach we estimated the perceived polarizer angle in the WFI FOV will vary by +/-2.5 degree for +/-60 degree and +/-3.5 degree for 0 degree polarizer angles. We present the effect of this deviation on the polarized brightness to be recorded by WFI, along with how optical distortion will shift the location of foreshortened angles. We also discuss the implications of this effect on upcoming technologies based on polarimetry across in wide fields.

</details>


### [5] [Particle Swarm Optimization Strategies for Lensed Gravitational Waves: Parameter Estimation, Source Classification, and Accelerated Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12135)
*Weiqing Yu,Meng Zhang,Xiaobo Zou,Xiao Guo*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出使用粒子群优化（PSO）算法进行强引力波透镜信号的搜索、参数估计与分类，并结合高斯混合先验提升贝叶斯推断效率，显著提高计算速度与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强引力波透镜效应引入额外参数，使搜索问题具有高维性、多模态性和强非线性，传统方法面临挑战。

Method: 采用粒子群优化（PSO）算法，在四探测器相干网络中开展透镜引力波信号的搜索、参数估计与三分类（透镜/无透镜/噪声）；结合多数投票与通量比阈值构建分类流程；并提出PSO_GAUSS_MIX方法，利用PSO结果构造高斯混合先验用于嵌套采样。

Result: PSO能有效恢复啁啾质量、质量比、天区位置及透镜参数（2-PN匹配情形），波形失配时出现质量偏差；三分类准确率达86.19%；PSO_GAUSS_MIX在2-PN情形下实现1.38倍加速且保持后验精度。

Conclusion: PSO是一种高效、鲁棒的透镜引力波搜索优化器，亦可作为高质量先验构造工具，为未来引力波天文学中的强透镜分析提供新范式。

Abstract: The detection and parameter estimation of lensed gravitational wave (GW) signals are crucial for astrophysics and cosmology. Strong lensing introduces extra parameters, rendering the search high-dimensional, multi-modal, and strongly nonlinear. We apply Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO) to the search, parameter estimation, and classification of lensed compact binary coalescence GWs within a four-detector coherent network. Using the restricted 2-PN waveform as a template and a model-independent double-image lens parameterization, we assess PSO on both model-matched 2-PN signals and template-mismatched IMRPhenomD signals. Results show effective recovery of chirp mass, mass ratio, sky location, and lens parameters in the 2-PN case, with mass biases appearing under waveform mismatch. A three-class classification pipeline combining 12 PSO runs, majority voting, and a flux ratio threshold achieves 86.19% accuracy in distinguishing lensed GWs, unlensed GWs, and noise. We further propose PSO_GAUSS_MIX method, which constructs a Gaussian mixture prior from PSO estimates for nested sampling, yielding speedups of 1.38x in the restricted 2-PN cases while preserving posterior accuracy. These results establish PSO as an efficient optimizer for lensed GW searches and an effective prior constructor for Bayesian parameter estimation.

</details>

*Anton Neisius,Élodie Choquet,Vincent Chambouleyron,Arthur Vigan*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MITHiC实验平台的升级进展，包括新增可变形镜、Zernike波前传感器（ZELDA）以及与现有SLM和Lyot日冕仪的协同使用，以提升高对比度成像能力，服务于系外行星直接探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高对比度成像受恒星亮度和波前误差限制，需通过日冕仪和波前控制提升对比度，以满足当前实验平台及未来如Roman空间望远镜等任务需求。

Method: 在MITHiC实验平台上集成新型可变形镜（DM）、Zernike波前传感器（ZELDA），并与现有经典Lyot日冕仪和空间光调制器（SLM）协同工作，实现相位/振幅联合调控与低阶像差校正。

Result: 完成了MITHiC平台升级并获得初步实验结果，验证了其作为先进高对比度成像技术研发平台的可行性与灵活性。

Conclusion: 升级后的MITHiC平台为发展下一代高对比度成像技术提供了重要实验支撑，有助于推动系外行星直接探测技术进步。

Abstract: High-contrast imaging for the direct detection of exoplanets is limited by stellar brightness and by wavefront errors. Coronagraphy and wavefront control reduce these effects and enable contrast levels compatible with current testbeds and future missions such as the Roman Space Telescope. Within this framework, the MITHiC testbed at the Laboratoire d'Astrophysique de Marseille is being upgraded with a new deformable mirror used to control the wavefront. It complements the existing setup, which includes a classic Lyot coronagraph and a spatial light modulator (SLM) enabling phase and amplitude control to optimize the dark-hole region. We also implement the Zernike wavefront sensor (ZELDA) to correct low-order aberrations. This contribution presents recent progress on the MITHiC upgrade and initial results, highlighting its role as a versatile platform for developing advanced high-contrast imaging techniques.

</details>


### [7] [Ångström Exponent Calculation Using Wide-Field Stellar Photometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12704)
*Shefali Negi,Jan Ebr,Sergey Karpov,Jiří Eliášek*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文扩展了FRAM望远镜的气溶胶Angstrom指数测量方法，由单滤光片（B波段）升级为BVR三波段联合分析，利用Gaia DR3合成测光数据；发现多滤光片垂直气溶胶光学深度（VAOD）存在非物理的滤光片依赖排序问题，导致负指数，提出截距校正法以获得合理结果；在CTAO不同台址获得具有地理与气象特征的Angstrom指数分布，并与AERONET观测定性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升FRAM望远镜对气溶胶Angstrom指数的测量精度与可靠性，克服单波段分析的局限性，并实现与标准测光系统（Johnson-Cousins）的直接兼容。

Method: 基于FRAM望远镜在B（440 nm）、V（540 nm）、R（620 nm）三波段的宽视场恒星测光数据，采用Gaia DR3合成测光（无需通带转换），反演各台址的垂直气溶胶光学深度（VAOD），并计算B–V、V–R、B–R滤光对的Angstrom指数；针对VAOD异常排序问题引入经验性截距校正。

Result: 发现多滤光片VAOD存在系统性非物理排序，导致负Angstrom指数；截距校正后获得合理指数：南半球Paranal台址（CTAO-S0/S1）呈单峰分布（α≈0.41–0.84），北半球La Palma台址（CTAO-N）呈双峰分布（α≈0.2对应沙尘事件，α≈1.3对应洁净背景）；与共同时段AERONET观测定性一致。

Conclusion: Gaia DR3合成测光支持FRAM多波段气溶胶监测，但原始VAOD存在未明系统偏差；截距校正是一种有效实用方案；FRAM可稳健区分不同气溶胶类型（如沙尘与背景气溶胶），具备在CTA等大型天文台址开展长期气溶胶表征的潜力。

Abstract: The FRAM (F/Photometric Robotic Atmospheric Monitor) telescopes can measure the aerosol extinction at optical wavelengths using wide-field stellar photometry at the Cherenkov Telescope Array Observatory (CTAO) sites. The Angstrom exponent describes the wavelength dependence of aerosol extinction. In this work we extend the FRAM method from a single $B$-filter (440 nm) analysis using the Tycho-2 catalogue to a simultaneous three-filter analysis in $B$, $V$, and $R$ (440, 540, and 620 nm) using Gaia DR3 synthetic photometry, which provides photometry directly in the Johnson--Cousins system without passband transformations. The exponent is derived from the vertical aerosol optical depth (VAOD) for the B--V, V--R, and B--R filter pairs at three sites over 2021--2022. The multi-filter VAOD shows a systematic filter-dependent ordering that is inconsistent with physical expectation and produces negative exponents. It is not currently possible to determine which filter yields the correct VAOD, and none of the four processing configurations we tested resolves the ordering. We introduce an intercept correction as a practical remedy that yields physically plausible exponents. At the two southern Paranal sites (CTAO-S0 and CTAO-S1) the exponent distributions are unimodal, with mean $α\approx 0.84$ (B--V) and $0.65$ (B--R) at CTAO-S0 and $0.60$ and $0.41$ at CTAO-S1. At CTAO-N (La Palma) the distributions are bimodal, with $α\approx 0.2$ (B--V) during episodic Saharan dust intrusions and $α\approx 1.3$ under clean background conditions. AERONET sun-photometer measurements on the dates common to both instruments show the same two populations, providing qualitative support for the FRAM method.

</details>

### [8] [First detection of the forbidden rotational spectrum of H$_3^+$: JWST/MIRI observations of the shocked gas in HH 168](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10122)
*David A. Neufeld,Joel D. Green,Dan M. Watson,Paule Sonnentrucker*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST/MIRI首次在HH 168中探测到H₃⁺的纯转动谱（由离心畸变导致的禁戒跃迁），发现其发射与热激波H₂共分布，非热激发，温度>2000 K；对比激波模型表明解离型J激波更符合观测，H₃⁺谱线可作为激波中电离状态的新探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H₃⁺是星际离子-分子化学的基石，其振动谱早已被观测，但纯转动谱（仅由离心畸变允许的禁戒跃迁构成）此前从未在实验室或天文源中被探测到，亟需填补这一关键空白。

Abstract: One of the main steps in data reduction for ground-based astronomy is sky subtraction. Abstract: Active galactic nuclei exhibit complex, nonlinear multiwavelength variability that standard cross-correlation techniques, sensitive only to linear covariance, may fail to capture fully. We apply time-lagged Mutual Information (MI) lag estimation, via the Kraskov-Stoegbauer-Grassberger $k$-nearest-neighbour estimator ($k = 5$) with a 150-iteration Random Subset Selection bootstrap, to the light curves of four Seyfert galaxies (Mrk 509, NGC 5548, NGC 4151, and NGC 4593) monitored across eight energy bands by Swift. In the UV/optical regime the MI estimator successfully reproduces the inter-band disc reprocessing lags established by the interpolated cross-correlation function, with performance degrading toward the optical where low variability amplitude produces poorly defined MI peaks. In the X-ray bands, where the physical X-ray/UV coupling is known to be weak, the estimator fails to recover consistent lags across the sample, with the notable exception of NGC 4593. Contextualising these outcomes within the multifractal characterisation of our previous work, we discuss how MI performance relates to the multifractal spectral width $Δα$ of each band, and we propose $Δα$ as a candidate diagnostic for anticipating where MI-based reverberation mapping is likely to succeed or fail in multiband datasets of active galactic nuclei.

Abstract: The transition from atomic hydrogen (HI) to molecular hydrogen (H$_2$) is the first step in the evolution from diffuse interstellar gas to dense star-forming cores. HI narrow self-absorption (HINSA), produced by cold atomic gas mixed with molecular material, provides a direct tracer of the HI-to-H$_2$ transition in molecular clouds. This approach cannot recover the radial HI distribution and yields large errors for young cores whose profiles deviate from a Gaussian shape. Here we present hinspheres, an open-source framework that models the core as onion-like spherically symmetric concentric shells, solves radiative transfer along the line of sight and jointly fits the HI abundance, temperature, and kinematic parameters. The framework recovers all parameters with a mean relative error of 4.6-6.8% on our synthetic data with different ages. A combined resolution-noise grid shows that the median recovery error remains below 16% for cores within 1 kpc observed at FAST resolution with noise levels below 0.2 K. Applying it to the dark cores L1574 and L1544, we reveal radial HI abundance profiles that span about three orders of magnitude, which were missed by the single-Gaussian method. The derived chemical ages are $6.5\times10^6$ and $8.7\times10^6$ yr, respectively. Abstract: We observed 69 molecular cloud regions at 450 and 850 $μ$m with the POL-2 polarimeter on the James Clerk Maxwell Telescope and matched the maps to a common $12.6''$ beam. The median $γ- α$ is $-0.57$ to $-0.45$, with $γ< α$ in 24-27 regions. These ranges span the two extreme placements of $3σ$ limits for polarized intensity below the noise floor. The medians of $γ-α$ in 9 low-mass, 19 high-mass and 7 Central Molecular Zone (CMZ) regions are not ordered low-mass $<$ high-mass $<$ CMZ (Jonckheere-Terpstra $p = 0.43$-$0.70$). A difference among classes is not robustly detected. The Kruskal-Wallis $p = 0.056$-$0.078$ becomes $p = 0.026$-$0.041$ when the Rosette Molecular Cloud alone is excluded, although the high-mass class has the lowest median in every treatment. The classes follow a distance sequence (the beam spans 0.008-0.51 pc), coupling environment and physical scale. Within clouds, the 450 $μ$m polarization fraction decreases with Herschel column density in all 7 fields with sufficient dynamic range. Coefficients from pooled pixels mix trends within clouds, differences between clouds, and the influence of OMC 1. They therefore do not define a single law for individual pixels. The wavelength dependence across sources provides a benchmark for grain alignment. We cannot order it by environment and do not propose a unique mechanism.

Abstract: The Milky Way exhibits a hierarchical structure shaped by mergers, accretion, and internal dynamics. Gaia Data Release 3 (DR3) enables a global view of this structure, but requires scalable, model-agnostic methods to extract multi-scale clustering patterns. We aim to characterise the hierarchical clustering structure of the Milky Way in a model-agnostic way and to assess how the resulting structures relate to existing catalogues. We apply the hierarchical density-based clustering algorithm AstroLink to a locally magnitude-complete subsample of over one billion Gaia DR3 stars with five-dimensional astrometric information. The data are corrected for selection effects and transformed using a distance contraction and feature scaling. We compare the resulting cluster hierarchy to catalogues of open clusters, globular clusters, dwarf galaxies, and stellar streams using custom availability-aware metrics and a bi-directional best-match framework. At a significance threshold of $S = 3.8σ$, AstroLink identifies 7410 statistically significant clusters spanning a wide range of morphologies. Compact systems are often recovered as reciprocal matches with many also exhibiting internal substructure, while extended structures are typically fragmented or partially recovered. A substantial fraction of clusters have no counterpart in the comparison catalogues, these may be new structures but additional physical checks are needed to verify this. A model-agnostic, hierarchical clustering approach can recover the multi-scale structure of the Milky Way at Gaia DR3 scale and provides a unified framework for comparing heterogeneous catalogues and identifying candidate structures.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate possible causal influences in five historical seasons (1989-1993) of NGC 5548 using a version of the Synergistic-Unique-Redundant Decomposition (SURD) method adapted for this analysis, an information-theoretic causal discovery method for time series. We examine 7200 decompositions with common and unequal lags, two or three seasonal flux states, and base delays of 1-30 d. We rank the individual named components within each information family, reporting their amplitudes and simultaneous leakage. In 1989, unique continuum information about the core reaches 0.799 and 0.387 of target entropy for two and three states, with leakage 0.047 and 0.276. In 1992, the red-wing history supplies unique information about the future blue wing in both variable sets and both state counts, with peak target-entropy fractions 0.106-0.166. Distinct joint influences also emerge: for the 1991 core, the two-state S(F,B) and S(F,R) components contribute 0.187 and 0.125 at 20 d with leakage 0.087, although the unique continuum term vanishes. Strong core-wing redundancy identifies candidate inputs for simpler predictive models. Large late-lag leakage, reaching approximately 0.97 in the total set, identifies targets for additional driver measurements and richer temporal histories. The resulting seasonal map of individual, shared and joint influences complements the response delays and velocity structure measured by reverberation mapping, and provides specific causal hypotheses for the evolving broad-line region.

Abstract: The stellar-to-halo mass relation (SHMR), a key prediction of galaxy formation models, peaks at $M_* \sim 3 \times 10^{10}\,M_\odot$. This scale is often attributed to circumgalactic baryonic feedback processes, however, direct observational evidence linking the two remains scarce. The radial Mg II absorption equivalent-width profile, $W_{\rm MgII} \propto D^k$, becomes progressively shallower with increasing stellar mass, following $k = 0.70\log(M_*/M_\odot)-9.12$. Crucially, we find that the $W_{\rm MgII}$ in the CGM scales tightly with the SHMR. The $W_{\rm MgII}$ peaks at the same characteristic mass as the SHMR, coinciding with a transition of the cool CGM from super-virial to sub-virial kinematics. Low-mass halos show excess non-gravitational motions consistent with recycled accretion and feedback-driven circulation, whereas massive halos show halo-centric heating that may maintain star formation quenching. Abstract: We investigate critical relaxation dynamics and their connection to quantum information scrambling in the conformal field theory dual to a Reissner-Nordström anti-de Sitter (RN-AdS) black hole. Abstract: We revisit the thermodynamic implications of the Gauss-Bonnet topological term in four spacetime dimensions. However, standard clustering analysis based on 2-point statistics only capture the Gaussian information of the matter density field, while the analytical models used to match these statistical probe are limited to quasi-linear scales, as they are based on perturbation theory. Therefore, the non-Gaussian and non-linear information of the density field is not exploited. To go beyond these limitations, several alternative clustering methods have been developed that rely on a simulation-based pipeline. In this presentation, we focus on the DESI Bright Galaxy Survey, a magnitude-limited sample of bright galaxies in the redshift range of 0.1 < z < 0.4. We present how accessing the non-linear and non-Gaussian information of the density field allows around 40% improvement on the constraints of some $Λ$CDM parameters. Abstract: Weak gravitational lensing is a powerful cosmological probe that enables direct mapping of the matter distribution and precise constraints on the fundamental parameters governing structure formation in the universe. To address this gap, we present the FAIR Universe Weak Lensing Machine Learning Uncertainty Challenge, one of the NeurIPS 2025 competitions, as a benchmark dataset and evaluation study. Our dataset reflects realistic weak-lensing observations, incorporating multiple systematic biases, and thereby providing a controlled testbed for robust cosmological inference under uncertainties. We summarize the main methodological advances developed by the winners of our challenge, including data augmentation with conditional optimal transport, an AI-agent-driven approach for parameter inference, ensemble learning, Vision Transformer-based architectures, and physics-informed feature integration. By unifying the benchmark dataset and the winning solutions, this work provides a community resource for developing and evaluating machine learning methods for weak lensing cosmology and, more broadly, for scientific inference under systematic uncertainties.

Abstract: We investigate whether Solar System dynamics can provide independent constraints on the present day cosmological deceleration parameter $q_0$, offering a complementary approach to conventional cosmological probes. We show that a $10\%$ dark energy deficiency can suppress the expansion of a representative nonlinear void by approximately $2.7\%$, generating peculiar velocity residuals of order $60$-$100\,\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}}$ across scales of $30$-$50\, Mpc$. We extend the background-level framework of M. Farhang and S. M. S. Movahed [Phys. Rev. Abstract: The microhertz gravitational wave (GW) frequency regime remains one of the least explored regions of the GW spectrum. This is due to the physical limitations of our current and upcoming GW observatories such as LIGO and LISA, which are tuned to dHz and mHz respectively. Proposed experiments targeting the $μ$Hz regime are ambitious, expensive and unlikely to be operational within the next 20 years. Pulsar Timing Arrays (PTAs), with their Galaxy-sized baselines, occupy the other end of the spectrum; the frequencies they are sensitive to are a function of the observing cadence and total dataset timespan. Existing PTA campaigns have cadences and timespans corresponding to the nanohertz regime, however the possibility exists that they can be 'pushed' to detect higher frequencies. This paper investigates the viability of a custom microhertz-sensitive PTA, observing a small number of millisecond pulsars with a daily cadence, over the course of 1 and 2 years respectively. We assume the use of both 100-m and 500-m class radio telescopes for these observations, and an idealised noise model with negligible systematics to determine the most optimistic detection threshold. We compute the sensitivity of our proposed high-cadence PTA configurations to power-law signals and individual GW sources. We find that a 500-m class telescope would likely be able to set new upper limits on individual sources and GW background signals in the $0.1-1~μ$Hz range; while a 100-m class telescope would, in the best case, only match that of the performance of the EPTA second data release in the same range. Finally we consider a possible $μ$Hz PTA consisting of SKA-Mid and DSA observing 24 pulsars; we find this performs on par with the 500-m class while using far less telescope time, presenting a more feasible solution to microhertz-GW pulsar timing.

Abstract: We investigate how baryon acoustic oscillations (BAO) and Type Ia supernovae constrain radial density profiles and acoustic-ruler evolution in Lemaitre-Tolman-Bondi (LTB) cosmology with a cosmological constant. Extending a homogeneous Friedmann-Robertson-Walker analysis requires calculating both distances and unequal radial and transverse stretching of a single initial ruler. Combining transverse BAO with supernovae isolates relative ruler evolution without an absolute calibration, while radial BAO supplies complementary information. We connect this test to exact nonlinear models with a low-density region compensated by surrounding excess matter, varying its depth, width, distance from the observer, and background matter density. Using DESI DR2 BAO and DES-Dovekie supernovae with full covariance and identical calibration freedom, the LTB fit improves $χ^2$ by 8.523 over flat $Λ$CDM for three additional parameters, mainly through the supernovae. DESI strongly rejects the particular deep profile favored by supernovae and angular BAO alone. However, a narrow depression near the observer and a broader, shallower depression farther away fit almost equally well, with $Δχ^2=0.647$. The combined data therefore constrain particular geometries without uniquely reconstructing the density profile or ruler evolution. The inference is conditional on supernova selection and interpreting released BAO summaries through local ruler transport at effective redshifts. Homogeneous simulations show that the fit improvement does not establish a detection of inhomogeneity. Finally, in the local approximation, common LTB ruler evolution shifts angular BAO and the transverse component of three-dimensional BAO together and cannot alone explain their discrepancy.

Abstract: In Inflation without an Inflaton (IWI), second-order de Sitter (dS) gravitons generate primordial curvature as the Coulomb-type potential of their gradient stress. Each pair contributes $(p/k)^2$, yielding exponential enhancement over the contributing e-folds. With exact Bunch-Davies evolution, this solution satisfies all four scalar second-order Einstein equations in pure dS. The retarded transverse-traceless solution instead has a UV kernel of order unity, $(k/p)^2$ below the scalar kernel, and a one-loop correction $Δ^2_{h,2}/Δ^2_h\simeq0.04\,Δ^2_h$. Its source acts before internal freeze-out, but oscillatory cancellations prevent tensor enhancement.
  We evolve the curvature through a smooth exit into radiation domination. Conserved curvature resides on the vacuum-decay hypersurface. A global depletion clock holds each scale on zero-shear time for $N_{\rm w}$ e-folds after horizon exit before local release, giving all scales equal enhancement. Energy-momentum conservation feeds the depleted vacuum energy into a non-vacuum sector, modeled as radiation. Deep in radiation domination, $Δ^2_ζ=c_wΔ_h^4e^{N_{\rm w}}/20$, with clock-dependent $c_w$, $n_s-1=-4ε$, and $n_T=-2ε$. The spectrum is adiabatic and Gaussian; super-horizon $ζ$ is conserved to better than $10^{-4}$, and changing the adiabatic exit fluid alters it by less than $0.2\%$. A direct radiation exit requires $31\lesssim N_{\rm w}\lesssim50$, or $3\times10^{-6}\lesssim r\lesssim0.036$.

Above $k_{\rm end}e^{-N_{\rm w}}$, the scalar spectrum steepens to $k^{-1}$, suppressing $μ$-distortions for sharp release when $N_{\rm w}\gtrsim45$.

TL;DR: 本文通过贝叶斯方法系统研究了非高斯原初扰动产生超大质量黑洞种子的可行性，发现对数正态和截断幂律功率谱增强模型在满足COBE/FIRAS μ-畸变约束下，可成功解释JWST观测到的高红移‘小红点’（LRDs），且局域非高斯参数|f_NL|≈1、峰振幅A≈10⁻³时即可实现；结果还显示正负f_NL均被偏好，但负f_NL的偏好强烈依赖于吸积历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST在z≳4处观测到‘小红点’（LRDs），暗示存在早于标准模型预期的超大质量黑洞（≳10⁶M⊙），挑战了基于星族III残骸吸积与并合的传统黑洞种子形成理论，因而亟需探索如原初黑洞（PBH）等替代理论；而PBH形成所需的原初功率谱增强又受CMB谱畸变（尤其是μ-畸变）严格限制。

Method: 采用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法，对两类非高斯原初扰动模型（对数正态型和截断幂律型）进行首次全面贝叶斯参数分析，联合考虑局域非高斯参数f_NL、功率谱峰振幅A及不同吸积历史（含爱丁顿极限吸积、占空比f_duty等），并施加COBE/FIRAS μ-畸变约束。

Result: 两种模型均可在|f_NL|=O(1)、A=O(10⁻³)且结合标准爱丁顿吸积时，同时满足μ-畸变限制并成功产生LRD对应的SMBH种子；分析发现正、负f_NL均被数据偏好；其中负f_NL的偏好高度依赖吸积占空比——当f_duty≲0.1时该偏好消失。

Conclusion: JWST观测到的高红移LRDs可通过非高斯原初扰动机制形成SMBH种子，且与当前CMB谱畸变观测兼容；未来更灵敏的CMB谱畸变实验（如PIXIE、SuperPIXIE）将对此假说提供关键检验。

Abstract: Recent JWST observations of "Little Red Dots" (LRDs) at $z \gtrsim 4$ suggest the existence of early supermassive black holes (SMBHs) with masses $\gtrsim 10^6\,M_\odot$. Their rapid emergence challenges conventional seeding and growth scenarios based on accretion and mergers of Population III stellar remnants, motivating renewed interest in primordial black holes (PBHs) formed from primordial perturbations. Explaining such massive seeds requires power spectrum enhancements that are constrained by cosmic microwave background (CMB) spectral distortions. In this work we perform the first comprehensive Bayesian exploration of non-Gaussian primordial perturbations capable of producing SMBH seeds while remaining consistent with CMB spectral distortion constraints, and examine the role of accretion history in constraining these models. We perform a suite of Markov chain Monte Carlo analyses of power spectrum enhancements (lognormal and cut-power-law) with varying local non-Gaussianity and accretion histories, satisfying the COBE/FIRAS $μ$-distortion constraint with and without the requirement that the model produces SMBH seeds. We find that both models can seed LRDs and evade $μ$-distortion bounds for $|f_{\rm NL}|= O(1)$ and peak amplitude $A= O(10^{-3})$, when standard Eddington-limited accretion is also incorporated. Interestingly, the results exhibit a preference for both positive and negative values of $f_{\rm NL}$. The preference for negative $f_{\rm NL}$ is strongly dependent on the accretion history, and is removed if the black holes have a small duty cycle, $f_{\rm duty}\lesssim 0.1$. These results demonstrate that the JWST high-$z$ LRD population can be seeded by primordial perturbations while remaining compatible with CMB data, and highlight the importance of next-generation CMB spectral distortion missions to test this hypothesis.

</details>

### [92] [An Information-Theoretic Perspective on Spectral Reconstruction in Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10671)

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Kullback-Leibler散度的信息论框架，用于量化格点关联函数数据对谱重建的约束力，结合高斯平滑与贝叶斯方法评估重建谱结构的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限、含噪的格点关联函数对谱重建的可靠性评估需厘清数据约束与先验假设之间的关系。

Method: 构建基于KL散度的信息论框架，量化先验到后验分布的变化；考虑单能量点谱强度及全谱相关性约束；引入高斯加权平滑以实现能量尺度上的局部信息评估；结合分层贝叶斯重建与后验采样进行验证。

Result: 在三峰模拟谱和近物理质子格点关联函数上验证了该框架；信息度量能解释重建谱特性，并提供半定量准则以判断谱结构受数据约束强度及先验影响下的可靠性。

Conclusion: 所提信息论框架为谱重建结果的可信度评估提供了可解释、可计算的量化依据，有助于区分数据驱动特征与先验引入偏差。

Abstract: Assessing the reliability of spectral reconstruction from finite, noisy lattice correlators requires understanding the constraints supplied by the data relative to prior assumptions. We develop an information-theoretic framework using Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergences to quantify the information supplied by the data through changes from prior to posterior probability distributions. Our analysis accounts for both changes in the probability distributions of spectral strength at individual energies and constraints encoded in correlations across the spectrum. We smear these information measures over neighboring energies using Gaussian weights to obtain a smoothed assessment at a specified energy scale. We demonstrate this framework using a hierarchical Bayesian reconstruction method with posterior sampling, applied to mock correlator data generated from a three-peak spectrum and a near-physical lattice proton correlator. These calculations show that the information measures help explain the properties of reconstructed spectra. They also provide a semiquantitative criterion for assessing how strongly reconstructed spectral structures are constrained by the data and how reliable they are in light of prior effects.

</details>

*Scott Moerschbacher,Frank X. Lee*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文比较了固定抵消模板与协方差优化的广义最小二乘估计器在弱场响应系数提取中的性能，发现固定模板在精度损失极小的情况下更稳健，尤其在模型误设时表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高背景场格点计算中弱场响应系数提取的精度与鲁棒性，避免因模型误设（如忽略高阶响应项）导致系统偏差。

Method: 将响应系数提取建模为磁场强度空间中的线性估计问题，对比固定阶数（O(B⁴)–O(B⁸)）抵消模板与基于协方差优化的广义最小二乘估计器，并利用核子双比值和重子十重态（decuplet）精确可控的相关器进行验证。

Result: 固定模板几乎不损失精度，且对高阶响应项缺失具有强鲁棒性；协方差优化虽提升1–2%统计精度，但其权重对遗漏响应模式的杠杆效应达3–4个数量级，导致矩估计偏移达1.8个边际标准误差；decuplet控制实验显示，固定模板满足理论约束（如μ_{Δ⁺}/μ_{Δ^{++}}=1/2），而协方差拟合严重偏离。

Conclusion: 统计精度提升不等于物理可靠性提升；在模型不确定性存在时，简单、鲁棒的固定模板优于高精度但敏感的协方差优化方法；结果强调需区分精度与鲁棒性，而非直接外推至物理点磁矩。

Abstract: Background-field lattice calculations extract weak-field response coefficients from correlators computed at several field strengths. We treat this extraction as linear estimation in field space and compare fixed cancellation stencils with covariance-optimized, generalized-least-squares estimators of the same coefficient, using archival nucleon double ratios on about 1000 quenched configurations at six quark masses and decuplet correlators for which exact answers follow from the lattice contractions. The fixed $O(B^4)$-$O(B^8)$ stencils are nearly coincident and cost almost nothing in precision. Weight-free null-space tests reject the two-term response model in most nucleon ensembles; whether they reject the three-term model depends on the temporal fits and on the noise of the estimated temporal covariance, which raises the null-contrast error by a factor of 1.7-2.4. With that noise included, covariance optimization gains 1-2% in precision at the five lighter masses (3-30% with two-state time fits, which also lower moments by 3-10%) and nothing on independent data halves, while its weights carry three to four orders of magnitude more leverage on omitted response modes than the fixed stencils and move the moments by up to 1.8 marginal standard errors. The decuplet controls show what such leverage does under misspecification: the first-order responses obey $μ_{Δ^+}/μ_{Δ^{++}}=1/2$ and $μ_{Δ^0}=0$ exactly. The lowest-order estimate misses the first by 10-25 standard errors. The fixed $O(B^4)$-$O(B^8)$ stencils satisfy it within errors in the production windows, whereas the correlated linear fit in $B^2$, with the same constraints as the $O(B^4)$ stencil, misses it by 3-17%; the null-space test rejects that fit's model. The results distinguish statistical precision from robustness to omitted response modes; they are not physical-point magnetic moments.

</details>


### [94] [Flavor decomposition of the nucleon axial and tensor charges from lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10816)
*C. Alexandrou,S. Bacchio,M. Di Carlo,A. Evangelista,C. Iona,F. Pittler,G. Spanoudes*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用四种不同格距的twisted-mass clover-improved费米子组态，计算了核子的轴向荷与张量荷，结果与实验值一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定核子的轴向荷和张量荷对理解强相互作用及检验标准模型至关重要，而格点QCD是目前唯一第一性原理方法。

Method: 采用四组具有相同物理体积（约5.5 fm）但不同格距（0.05–0.08 fm）的twisted-mass clover-improved费米子组态；上下夸克、奇异夸克和粲夸克质量调至近似物理值；同时计算连通与非连通图以获得同位旋矢量与标量荷及其味分解。

Result: 得到同位旋矢量轴向荷g_A^{u-d}=1.263(34)(26)，与实验一致；同位旋标量轴向荷g_A^{u+d}=0.458(16)(1)；同位旋矢量张量荷g_T^{u-d}=0.951(21)(5)；同位旋标量张量荷g_T^{u+d}=0.556(16)(5)。

Conclusion: 该工作在多格距下实现了轴向与张量荷的高精度格点计算，系统误差控制良好，为后续研究核子自旋结构与新物理信号提供了可靠基准。

Abstract: We determine the nucleon axial and tensor charges using four ensembles of twisted-mass clover-improved fermions with approximately equal physical volumes of about 5.5~fm and four lattice spacings, $ a=0.05$~fm, 0.06~fm, 0.07~fm, and 0.08~fm. The mass of the degenerate up and down, strange and charm quarks are tuned to approximately their physical values. We compute both connected and disconnected contributions to determine the isovector and isoscalar charges and their flavour decomposition. For the nucleon isovector axial charge we find $g_A^{u-d}=1.263(34)(26)$, in agreement with the experimental value. For the isoscalar axial charge we find $g_A^{u+d}=0.458(16)(1)$, while for the corresponding tensor charges we find $g_T^{u-d}=0.951(21)(5)$ and $g_T^{u+d}=0.556(16)(5)$.

</details>


### [95] [Evaluating generative samplers for lattice field theory by scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11108)
*Satoshi Iso,Lingxiao Wang*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种尺度分辨的诊断方法，用于评估格点场论中生成式采样器的性能，指出传统有效样本量（ESS）可能无法反映长程观测量的准确性，并通过自由场和φ⁴理论验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于全局有效样本量（ESS）的评估方法可能掩盖长距离观测量的误差，尤其当误差主要分布在短距离模式上时；需发展能区分不同空间尺度误差的新诊断工具。

Method: 提出基于粗粒化模型分布与目标分布之间重叠度的尺度分辨诊断方法；在截断尺度处该重叠决定渐近ESS分数，降低截断尺度可分离长波误差；结合边际Metropolis检验与精确局域更新进行偏差校正。

Result: 在自由场理论中，两个ESS相近的采样器对磁化率分别产生32%偏差与无偏差；在二维φ⁴临界区域，扩散采样器的路径权重ESS分数随体积迅速下降，但磁化低阶矩仍接近目标值；边际Metropolis检验保持O(1)接受率并减小剩余偏差。

Conclusion: 全局ESS不是长程物理量准确性的可靠指标；尺度分辨重叠诊断更有效地揭示误差来源；需独立提供或计算目标边缘分布，避免因权重估计导致的全局重叠失真。

Abstract: Generative samplers for lattice field theory are commonly evaluated by their effective sample size (ESS). We show that a small global ESS need not imply inaccurate long-distance observables: errors spread over many short-distance modes can dominate this diagnostic. We introduce a scale-resolved diagnostic based on the overlap between coarse-grained model and target distributions. At the lattice cutoff, this overlap determines the asymptotic ESS fraction; lower cutoffs isolate long-wavelength errors. In the exactly solvable free theory, two samplers with comparable ESS yield a $32\%$ bias and no bias in the susceptibility, respectively. In two-dimensional $φ^4$ theory near criticality, trained diffusion samplers show the same separation of scales: their path-weight ESS fraction falls rapidly over the volumes studied, while low moments of the magnetization remain close to their target values. Varying the reference parameters substantially changes infrared observables, with little effect on the log-weight variance. Given the target magnetization distribution, a marginal Metropolis test maintains an acceptance rate of order one in our tests. Combined with exact local updates, it reduces the remaining mismatch, although residual bias remains at finite relaxation length. In the free field, uniformly bounded relative errors in the proposal mode variances give a volume-independent bound on the number of updates needed for a prescribed accuracy of the chain's stationary mode variances at fixed correlation length. The target marginal must be supplied or computed separately; estimating it from the samplers' own weights can inherit the poor global overlap.

</details>


### [96] [Renormalized symmetry probes for emergent symmetries and non-chiral lattice fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12142)
*Ting-Wai Chiu*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于研究高温QCD中对称性恢复的RG不变探针方法，特别处理了手征-自旋等涌现对称性在格点正则化下的重整化问题，并应用于物理点Nf=2+1+1 QCD模拟，确认了从轻夸克到粲夸克扇区的手征对称性分层恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温QCD中对称性恢复需RG不变且方案无关的探针；但现有方法在手征对称性破缺正则化（如Wilson型）或涌现对称性（如SU(2)_{CS}）下难以保证，亟需系统性重整化框架。

Method: 提出以\bar{ud}扇区热关联子为基准统一重整各味扇区的热/屏蔽介子关联子；定义对称性比κ_{AB}，其依赖通过比值R_{AB}吸收重整化效应；对精确对称性R_{AB}=1，κ_{AB} RG不变；对涌现对称性，R_{AB}≠1携带抵消后的物理尺度依赖；适用于Wilson、twisted-mass和staggered费米子。

Result: 在N_f=2+1+1物理点域墙费米子模拟中，验证了手征对称性从轻到粲扇区的分层恢复，且该结论不依赖于重整化；\bar{uc}与\bar{sc}扇区具有最宽的chiral-spin窗口和更紧阈值；自由夸克极限下导出κ_{fade}=2/3、κ_{CS}≤1/3。

Conclusion: 所提重整化框架使κ_{AB}成为可靠、普适的对称性恢复探针，尤其凸显含粲夸克的自旋-1介子通道为研究chiral-spin对称性的最优选择。

Abstract: The degeneracy of hadron correlators in symmetry-related channels signals symmetry restoration in high-temperature QCD. RG-invariant probes of this degeneracy need care when the regularization breaks chiral symmetry, or when the symmetry is not exact in the massless action, as for the emergent chiral-spin symmetry $SU(2)_{CS}$. We renormalize the thermal and screening meson correlators of every flavor sector by the thermal correlator of the same channel in the $\bar ud$ sector at a common reference point. The symmetry ratio $κ_{AB}$ then depends on the renormalization only through the ratio $R_{AB}$. For exact symmetries, $R_{AB}=1$ and $κ_{AB}$ is RG invariant and scheme independent in mass-independent schemes. For emergent symmetries, $R_{AB}\neq1$ carries the relative logarithmic cutoff dependence of the two channels, which cancels in $κ_{AB}$, and the residual dependence on the reference scale is physical. The framework applies to Wilson, twisted-mass and staggered fermions, where $R_{AB}$ absorbs the channel-dependent renormalization, and the tensor pair $(T_k,X_k)$ keeps $R_{T_kX_k}=1$ on isotropic Wilson-type lattices. Lattice artifacts are a small correction to the chiral-spin probes $κ_{CS}$ and $κ_{fade}$, but as large as the signal of the exact-symmetry probes. For free quarks, we derive $κ_{fade}=2/3$ for every mass and separation, and $κ_{CS}\le1/3$. We apply the renormalized probes to $N_f=2+1+1$ QCD with optimal domain-wall quarks at the physical point, with $a=0.064$ fm and $T=192$-$1539$ MeV. The hierarchical restoration of the chiral symmetries from the light to the charm sectors is confirmed, and is invariant under renormalization. The $\bar uc$ and $\bar sc$ sectors have the widest chiral-spin windows, also at a tighter threshold. Thus spin-1 mesons with one charm quark are the most attractive channels for the chiral-spin regime.

</details>

*J. A. Crawford,J. Finkenrath,L. Barca*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核子关联函数的计算与分析对理解其能谱和结构至关重要，但存在早期激发态主导和晚期信噪比指数下降两大挑战，导致难以确定合适的基态能量提取时间窗。

Method: 利用蒸馏轮廓调制各拉普拉斯本征模对关联函数的贡献，构造优化的核子算符，提升其与基态的重叠。

Result: 在二点和三点关联函数中实现了对核子激发态的有效压制，并推进了针对核子的优化蒸馏轮廓构建工作。

Conclusion: 该方法为更精确提取核子基态能量提供了新途径，有望提升格点QCD中核子物理的计算精度。

Abstract: The calculation and analysis of nucleon correlation functions is essential for understanding the observed spectra and structure of these ubiquitous hadrons. Two challenges arise in their analysis: at early Euclidean times, they are dominated by excited state contributions and at later Euclidean times, the signal-to-noise ratio degrades exponentially. This leads to a common challenge in identifying a reasonable window on which to extract the ground state energy. By modulating the contribution of each Laplacian eigenmode to the correlator via distillation profiles, we build optimised operators which improve the overlap with the nucleon ground state. In these proceedings, we present our progress on constructing optimised distillation profiles for nucleons and their excited state suppression in two- and three-point correlation functions.

</details>

Abstract: We propose a framework to construct the Symanzik effective Hamiltonian for lattice quantum field theories directly based on dimensional analysis and symmetries in the Hamiltonian formulation. This allows to classify all operators which can contribute at a given order in the spatial lattice spacing. This list can be reduced using the equations of motion and other identities. We exemplify the framework for $φ^4$ and pure gauge theory, obtaining results consistent with earlier publications for the case of classical improvement. It provides the means to develop a non-perturbative improvement programme in the Hamiltonian formulation of lattice quantum field theories in the future.

</details>


### [99] [Unpolarized proton PDF with physical quark masses and large momenta](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12730)
*Xiang Gao,Andrew Hanlon,Jinchen He,Xiangyu Jiang,Swagato Mukherjee,Peter Petreczky,Qi Shi,Yong Zhao*

TL;DR: 本文通过格点量子色动力学（LQCD）计算了质子的同位旋无关（u-d）非极化部分子分布函数（PDF），在物理夸克质量下，采用库仑规范固定下的夸克双线性算符和大动量有效理论框架，结合高统计精度测量与先进重整化方法，实现了对光锥PDF的精确提取，并与唯象PDF在中等x区域达成良好一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算质子的部分子分布函数（PDF）对于理解强子内部结构及检验量子色动力学（QCD）至关重要；传统唯象拟合存在模型依赖性，而格点QCD可提供第一性原理计算，但面临大动量、长距离分离及重整化等挑战，亟需在物理夸克质量下提升精度与可靠性。

Method: 采用(2+1)味费米子组态（a=0.076 fm），在库仑规范下构造夸克双线性算符，结合动量增强的核子插值算符以提升大动量和大夸克分离下的信噪比；使用静态RI/MOM与混合重整化方案处理裸矩阵元；通过NLO微扰匹配加NLL重求和，将准PDF外推至MS-bar方案下的光锥PDF。

Result: 在x ≈ 0.25–0.75区间内，格点计算的u−d PDF与主流唯象PDF（如JAM、CJ15）高度一致；首次在坐标空间虚部实现与唯象PDF NLO匹配结果的定量吻合；不确定性显著降低，且与更精细格距（a=0.06 fm）的先前结果自洽。

Conclusion: 该工作在物理夸克质量下实现了高精度、低不确定度的质子PDF格点计算，验证了大动量有效理论与先进重整化/匹配策略的有效性；结果与唯象分析的一致性为未来格点PDF作为QCD精密基准提供了坚实支撑。

Abstract: We present a lattice quantum chromodynamics (QCD) calculation of the proton isovector ($u-d$) unpolarized parton distribution function (PDF) at physical sea and valence quark masses, using Coulomb-gauge-fixed quark bilinears within the large-momentum effective theory framework. The calculation is performed on a (2+1)-flavor ensemble with a lattice spacing of $a=0.076$ fm and proton momenta up to $2.3$ GeV. We combine kinematically enhanced nucleon interpolating operators, the Coulomb-gauge method, and high-statistics measurements to improve the signal quality at large momenta and quark separations. We use the static regularization-independent momentum subtraction and hybrid schemes to renormalize the bare matrix elements. The light-cone PDF in the $\overline{\rm MS}$ scheme is obtained from the quasi-PDF through next-to-leading-order (NLO) perturbative matching supplemented by next-to-leading-logarithmic renormalization-group resummation. With reduced uncertainties, the resulting PDF is consistent with our previous calculation on a similar lattice ensemble with $a=0.06$~fm and a valence pion mass of 300 MeV. Notably, despite the reduced uncertainties, the agreement with the phenomenological PDF persists over the longitudinal momentum fraction range $0.25\lesssim|x|\lesssim0.75$, where the lattice calculation is expected to be reliable. This agreement is further supported by a comparison in coordinate space, where our short-distance lattice matrix elements are consistent with those obtained from the phenomenological PDF through NLO matching. In particular, we observe close agreement in the imaginary part for the first time. Together, these results represent an encouraging step toward precision benchmarking of lattice QCD calculations of PDFs.

</details>


### [100] [The structure of the pion at large $N_c$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12771)
*Isaac Zhu,Fernando Romero-López,Phiala E. Shanahan*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用Twisted Eguchi-Kawai (TEK)简化模型，在大N_c极限下计算了π介子的夸克动量份额，结果表明在大N_c极限下，π介子的轻锥动量主要由胶子携带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强子内部结构，特别是大N_c极限下夸克与胶子对动量的贡献分配，是检验QCD非微扰性质的重要途径；现有N_c=3下的结果与理论预期存在差异，需在大N_c极限下进行系统研究。

Method: 采用Twisted Eguchi-Kawai (TEK) 简化模型生成纯规范场构型，结合最大扭质量费米子计算π介子关联函数；利用Feynman-Hellmann方法提取裸矩阵元，并通过微扰论重整化因子转换至2 GeV处的MS-bar方案。

Result: 在物理π介子质量及大N_c极限下，得到夸克动量份额为⟨x⟩_q^{MS-bar}(2 GeV) = 0.267(105)。

Conclusion: 该值显著小于N_c=3时的实验与格点QCD结果，表明在大N_c极限下，π介子的轻锥动量主要由胶子承载，而非夸克。

Abstract: We compute the pion quark momentum fraction in the large-$N_c$ limit using the Twisted Eguchi-Kawai (TEK) reduced model. Gauge configurations for different values of the lattice spacing are generated with the pure-gauge TEK action, while the pion correlators are computed for different quark masses with maximally twisted mass fermions. The bare matrix element corresponding to the quark momentum fraction is obtained using the Feynman-Hellmann method, and we use perturbative renormalization factors to connect to the $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ scheme at $2\,\mathrm{GeV}$. A global analysis at several lattice spacings, pion masses, and values of $N_c$ yields $\langle x\rangle_q^{\overline{\mathrm{MS}}}(2\,\mathrm{GeV}) = 0.267(105)$ at the physical pion mass in the large-$N_c$ limit. This value is significantly smaller than existing $N_c=3$ determinations from phenomenology and lattice QCD, indicating that, in the large-$N_c$ limit, the pion's light-cone momentum is carried predominantly by gluons.

</details>


### [101] [A regularization-independent Information Criterion for correlated data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12877)
*Davide Laudicina*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种正则化无关的信息准则RIAIC，用于在相关数据上进行正则化拟合时的模型与数据子集选择，并通过格点QCD数据分析验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准AIC在处理正则化拟合和相关数据时存在正则化依赖性，限制了其在如格点QCD等领域的应用。

Method: 通过将信息准则定义为相关Kullback-Leibler散度中模型依赖部分的估计量，推导出正则化无关的RIAIC，并进一步给出其贝叶斯扩展。

Result: 在格点QCD数据上的测试表明，RIAIC能重现标准相关AIC的子集与模型选择特性，且涨落符合统计不确定性预期。

Conclusion: RIAIC为含协方差正则化的相关数据分析提供了可靠、正则化无关的模型与子集选择工具，拓展了信息准则的应用范围。

Abstract: We generalize the Akaike Information Criterion (AIC) to allow model and data subset selection when regularized fits are performed on correlated data. While the standard generalization to regularized fits is inherently dependent on the type of regularization applied to the data covariance matrix, our definition, the RIAIC, is, on average, regularization-independent. The regularization-independence is achieved by defining the new Information Criterion as an estimator of the model-dependent contribution to the correlated Kullback--Leibler divergence. The Bayesian extension is derived as well. We test the new criterion on lattice QCD data by performing regularized fits and show that it reproduces the selection properties of the standard correlated AIC, with fluctuations which are compatible with the expected statistical uncertainties. These results enable the application of subset and model selection techniques to cases where regularizations are applied to the covariance matrix of the fitted dataset.

</details>

### [102] [Proof of multiloop high-multiplicity integrand reduction coefficients in the 't Hooft-Veltman scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10065)
*Shengli Yi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在't Hooft-Veltman方案下，利用高重数费曼积分被积函数中传播子分母的仿射线性关系，给出了部分分式约化的紧凑行列式系数公式，并通过递归分解与多重割方法提供了两个独立证明；同时分析了该公式在特殊运动学下的行为及奇异系数项如何协同保持有限多项式恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在't Hooft-Veltman方案中，外部动量处于整数维空间，导致高重数费曼积分的传播子分母满足仿射线性关系，这为发展更高效、解析更清晰的部分分式约化方法提供了契机。

Method: 采用两种独立方法证明部分分式约化的行列式系数公式：一是基于递归部分分式分解与关于仿射关系数量的数学归纳法；二是运用多重割方法，结合消去公分母、设互补分母为零以隔离单项，并借助克莱姆法则求解完整系数。

Result: 严格证明了此前提出的紧凑行列式形式的部分分式系数公式；阐明了该公式在分母乘积基退化时的行为，展示了奇异系数项如何组合仍保持有限多项式恒等式。

Conclusion: 该行列式公式具有坚实的数学基础与良好的解析性质，适用于tHV方案下高重数费曼积分的系统化约化，在特殊运动学极限下仍能维持物理一致性。

Abstract: In the 't Hooft-Veltman (tHV) scheme, external momenta lie in a fixed integer-dimensional space, and the propagator denominators of high-multiplicity Feynman integrands obey affine-linear relations. Previous work used these relations to construct a partial-fraction reduction with a compact determinantal formula for its coefficients. We present two independent proofs of this formula. The first proof uses recursive partial-fraction decomposition and induction on the number of affine relations. The second adopts the multiple cut method. After clearing the common denominator, setting the complementary denominators to zero isolates one product, and Cramer's rule gives the complete coefficient. We also discuss the behavior of the formula at special kinematics and give an example in which terms with singular coefficients combine to preserve a finite polynomial identity as the denominator-product basis degenerates.

</details>


### [103] [Phenomenological explorations of a quasi-degenerate $Z'$ boson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10075)
*C. S. Kim,Donghun Lee,Gyeonghyun Min,Sechul Oh,Dibyakrupa Sahoo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对质量接近标准模型Z玻色子的Z'玻色子进行了模型无关的现象学分析，发现其存在会显著影响e⁺e⁻ → f f̄过程的截面和前后不对称性，尤其在Z峰外更明显，并利用轻中微子种类数Nν的测量对其施加约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究质量与标准模型Z玻色子接近的Z'玻色子可能带来的可观测效应，尤其是对e⁺e⁻对撞中轻子/夸克末态的截面和前后不对称性的修正。

Method: 基于树图级计算，开展模型无关的唯象分析，研究准简并Z'对e⁺e⁻ → f f̄（f ≠ e⁻）过程的影响，并结合Nν实验测量给出约束。

Result: 发现准简并Z'对前后不对称性的影响在Z峰以外区域尤为显著；利用LEP等实验对Nν的精确测量，可对该类Z'参数空间施加有效限制。

Conclusion: 即使Z'与Z质量高度接近，其存在仍可通过高精度电弱观测量（如A_FB和N_ν）被探测或排除，凸显了非共振区物理分析的重要性。

Abstract: We do a general model-independent phenomenological analysis of the curious case of a $Z'$ boson with mass close to that of the Standard Model (SM) $Z$ boson. From tree-level calculation alone we show that presence of such a quasi-degenerate or `practically' degenerate $Z'$ boson affects both the cross-section and forward-backward asymmetry of the process $e^+ e^- \to f \bar{f}$ where $f$ (with $f\neq e^-$) can be a lepton or quark. Most importantly the effect on forward-backward asymmetry is found to be more prominent away from the $Z$-pole. We provide constraints on such a $Z'$ from the measurement of $N_ν$, the number of light neutrino species.

</details>


### [104] [Critical Temperature in Nonextensive Hadron Models: A Pressure-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10079)
*M. A. A. Ahmed,H. Zainuddin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非广延统计框架下强相互作用物质的临界温度Tc，通过Tsallis熵导出的压力公式分析了不同q值和模型（MIT、Dual、Fuzzy）对Tc的影响，发现q在0.99到1.002区间内最能给出物理上一致的Tc估计值（约102 MeV）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能核碰撞中，由于内在涨落、记忆效应和长程色相互作用，火球演化可能偏离马尔可夫热化，使得非广延统计描述在近平衡态更适用；而确定强相互作用物质的临界温度Tc对理解强子物质与夸克胶子等离子体相变至关重要。

Method: 基于Tsallis熵构建压力方程，结合三种夸克模型（MIT袋模型、对偶QCD模型、模糊QCD模型），系统分析非广延参数q对压力行为及临界温度Tc的影响，并通过根的存在性、单调性及模型加权比较进行求解与约束。

Result: 当q=1时，压力方程可解析求解，MIT模型给出Tc≈102–106 MeV；q=0对应核液气相变，Tc≈55 MeV；q>1时可能出现多个正根；综合加权分析得复合估计Tc^w≈102 MeV；可靠物理区间为0.99≤q≤1.002。

Conclusion: 非广延参数q与具体强子模型共同决定临界温度Tc的取值，q接近1（但略大于1）且在窄区间[0.99, 1.002]内时，不同模型结果可协调统一，支持约102 MeV作为强子-夸克相变的稳健估计值。

Abstract: The determination of the critical temperature $T_c$ in strongly interacting matter is central to understanding the transition between confined hadronic matter and deconfined quark-gluon degrees of freedom. In high energy nuclear collisions, intrinsic fluctuations, memory effects, and long-range color interactions can drive the fireball evolution away from purely Markovian thermalization, making nonextensive statistical descriptions relevant near equilibrium. We investigate the critical temperature of nonextensive hadron models using a pressure-based formulation derived from Tsallis entropy. Here we show that the qualitative structure of the pressure equation is controlled by both the nonextensive parameter $q$ and the model-dependent bag contribution $B^{\text{model}}$. For $q=0$, the pressure equations are monotonic and admit a unique positive root, whereas for $q=1$ the seventh-order term vanishes and analytic solutions can be obtained. For $q>1$, the negative $T^7$ contribution modifies the pressure behavior and can generate multiple positive roots at finite volume. %Cosmological bounds require $q \approx 1$, meaning $q \simeq 1.001$ in proton-proton phenomenology is a system-dependent fit parameter. For $q\geq1$, $T^{\text{model}}_c$ is $\left(102.3\leq T_c^{\text{MIT}}\leq106.1,\ 86.5\leq T_c^{\text{Dual}}\leq 87.8,\ \text{and} \ 113.1\leq T_c^{\text{Fuzzy}}\leq 121.1\right)$ MeV. At $q=0$, $T^{\text{model}}_c \sim 55$ MeV marks the nuclear liquid-gas transition. A weighted comparison constrained by the MIT bag model at $q=1$ gives $(T_c^{\text{w}}\approx102)\ \text{MeV}$, with Fuzzy and Dual QCD terms entering as model-propagation corrections. The interval $(0.99\leq q\leq 1.002)$ therefore provides the most reliable domain for a common physical interpretation and a composite estimate of the critical temperature.

</details>


### [105] [Collider-observable curvaton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10114)
*David Cyncynates,Takuya Okawa*
