TL;DR: 本文研究了通过希格斯场超可重整化相互作用实现的曲率子机制，计算了量子和热修正对曲率子势的影响，并表明这些修正在整个宇宙学演化过程中均受控；高暴胀能标下，曲率子通过标准模型等离子体中的虚过程耗散；可行参数空间广泛，且曲率子质量与希格斯门户耦合量级相近的区域有望在轻希格斯混合标量的未来实验中被探测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种技术上自然的曲率子机制实现方式，特别是通过希格斯场的超可重整化相互作用来实现曲率子衰变，并评估其在宇宙学演化中的稳定性与可观测性。

Method: 计算曲率子势的量子与热修正；分析高暴胀能标下曲率子在标准模型等离子体中的耗散机制（特别是离壳过程）；系统扫描参数空间并评估与实验探测前景的关联。

Result: 量子和热修正在整个宇宙学演化中保持可控；高能标下曲率子主要通过标准模型等离子体中的离壳过程耗散；存在广阔的可行参数空间，其中曲率子质量与希格斯门户耦合强度相近的区域处于未来轻希格斯混合标量实验的探测范围内。

Conclusion: 该希格斯门户驱动的曲率子机制在理论自洽性和实验可检验性之间取得了良好平衡，为早期宇宙物理和新粒子探测提供了重要连接。

Abstract: We study a technically natural realization of the curvaton mechanism, in which decay is mediated through the superrenormalizable interaction with the Higgs. We compute the quantum and thermal corrections to the curvaton potential and show that they remain under control throughout its cosmological evolution. For high inflationary scales, the curvaton dissipates through off-shell processes in the Standard Model plasma. The viable parameter space is broad, and the region in which the curvaton mass and dimensionful Higgs-portal coupling are closest in scale lies within the discovery reach of proposed searches for light Higgs-mixed scalars.

</details>


### [106] [Pion and kaon electromagnetic form factors in a flavor-dressed contact interaction: the role of Nf-screening on dynamical chiral symmetry breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10166)
*Aftab Ahmad,Muhammad Abid,Niaz Hussain,Mumtaz Khan,Tariq Mahmood,Alfredo Raya*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过考虑依赖于轻味数Nf的对称性保持接触相互作用，计算了π介子和K介子的电磁形状因子，并研究其随Nf变化的行为；结果表明电荷半径单调增长，主要源于夸克核贡献而非矢量介子修饰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究夸克味数Nf对介子电磁结构（特别是电荷半径）的影响，理解手征对称性破缺如何随Nf变化被屏蔽，并检验该类接触相互作用模型在有限Nf下的物理合理性。

Method: 采用彩虹- ladder截断下的Schwinger-Dyson方程与Bethe-Salpeter方程，结合固有时正则化，并自洽求解 dressed 夸克-光子顶点的非齐次Bethe-Salpeter方程。

Result: π和K介子电荷半径rπ、rK随Nf从2增至6单调增大；其中<r²π>的裸顶点贡献由0.097增至0.226 fm²，而矢量介子修饰贡献仅由0.102增至0.127 fm²；ρ介子极点质量下降但修饰效应减弱；奇异质量仅轻微抑制K介子响应。

Conclusion: 介子电磁结构对Nf敏感，其演化主要由夸克核心驱动，支持在接近手征相变时动力学手征对称性破缺被渐进屏蔽的图像；该接触相互作用模型在低Q²（≤0.6 GeV²）下可与NA7数据及一阶手征微扰论定性一致。

Abstract: We compute the electromagnetic form factors of the pion and the kaon in a symmetry-preserving contact interaction whose effective coupling depends on the number of active light flavors Nf , so that dynamical chiral symmetry breaking is screened as Nf grows toward the chiral transition. The calculation uses the rainbow-ladder truncation of the Schwinger-Dyson and Bethe-Salpeter equations with proper-time regularization, and it includes the dressed quark-photon vertex obtained from its own inhomogeneous Bethe-Salpeter equation in the same truncation. At Nf = 2 the model reproduces the established contact interaction values of the meson masses, decay constants and condensates, and the charge radii rpi = 0.447 fm and rK = 0.417 fm, which undershoot experiment by about one third, as is known for this class of interactions. We therefore restrict every comparison to Q2 less then or equal to 0.6 GeV2, confront it with the NA7 data and with one-loop chiral perturbation theory, and focus on the response to Nf . Both radii grow monotonically with Nf , and the growth survives every variation of the screening function and of the infrared confinement scale that we test. The growth is carried by the quark core rather than by the vector-meson dressing: between Nf = 2 and 6 the bare-vertex contribution to <r2pi> rises from 0.097 to 0.226 fm2, while the dressing contribution moves from 0.102 to 0.127 fm2 even though the rho pole drops from 0.93 to 0.70 GeV. The finite strange mass slows the kaon response only moderately.

</details>


### [107] [Real Intermediate States in Dark Matter Freeze-In from First Principles: Single Mediator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10204)
*Jannis Brirup,Adrian Finke,Michael Klasen,Luca Paolo Wiggering*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文从闭时路径（CTP）非平衡量子场论出发，为s通道冻入机制中不稳定的中间态（RIS）提供了第一性原理描述，避免了传统半经典玻尔兹曼方法中的双计数问题，并系统包含热修正效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半经典玻尔兹曼方法在处理含不稳定中介粒子的s通道暗物质冻入时，因简单叠加衰变与湮灭项并采用有限宽度正则化共振，导致实中间态（RIS）双计数；现有RIS减除方案多属经验性，缺乏第一性原理基础。

Method: 基于闭时路径（CTP）非平衡量子场论框架，构建s通道冻入中单标量、费米子或矢量暗物质产生的CTP动力学描述；对热浴中处于热平衡的标量或矢量中介粒子，采用全质量-动量依赖的1PI自能重求和近似其精确传播子，而非硬热圈（HTL）极限。

Result: CTP方法天然规避非幺正性双计数，并系统纳入重要热修正；数值比较显示，其预言的遗迹丰度与常用RIS减除方案存在可观测差异，证实一致RIS处理具有现象学意义。

Conclusion: CTP方法为含不稳定中介粒子的s通道冻入提供了严格、自洽且具热修正能力的第一性原理描述，显著优于经验性RIS减除方案，应成为未来相关研究的标准工具。

Abstract: We investigate the treatment of real intermediate states (RIS) in freeze-in production of $s$-channel Dark Matter (DM) with unstable mediators, as such models feature a double-counting issue when decay and annihilation collision terms are simply added within the semi-classical Boltzmann approach and a finite width is used for the regularization of the resonance. While several ad-hoc RIS-subtraction schemes are available to mitigate this problem, a first-principles treatment in this context is so far missing. We therefore start from the Closed-Time-Path (CTP) formulation of non-equilibrium Quantum Field Theory and present a CTP-based description of $s$-channel freeze-in pair production of a single scalar, fermion or vector DM species. In addition to being intrinsically free of non-unitary overcounting, the CTP ansatz also has the advantage of systematically capturing thermal corrections known to be important for DM freeze-in. Separate evolution equations assuming a single scalar or vector mediator in thermal equilibrium with the surrounding bath are presented. The exact propagators of the unstable mediators are approximated by resumming one particle irreducible (1PI) self-energy diagrams generated by bath particles and are evaluated with their full mass and momentum dependence in contrast to the commonly adopted Hard Thermal Loop limit. The CTP relic densities are compared numerically with those obtained using the most common RIS-subtraction schemes for two example $s$-channel freeze-in models, demonstrating the phenomenological relevance of a consistent RIS treatment.

</details>


### [108] [$B\to$ invisible decays as probes of light new physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10218)
*Martín Novoa-Brunet*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了B介子稀有衰变B+→K+Emiss异常现象的可能解释，包括重粒子贡献和新轻态产生，提出对撞机实验与暗物质研究的潜在联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释Belle II实验中观测到的B+→K+Emiss衰变超出标准模型预期的现象，探索其背后的新物理机制。

Method: 通过拟合缺失质量分布分析可能的两体末态贡献；结合B→K*Emiss、K*纵向极化及Bs不可见衰变等可观测量约束模型参数；构建含矢量玻色子Z'与暗费米子的U(1)'模型，并考虑电弱圈诱导混合与热退耦机制。

Result: 缺失质量峰约2.1 GeV支持单矢量解释；标量或费米子对（质量0.4–0.6 GeV）仍可行；引入轻暗费米子可同时满足dimuon搜寻限制并解释暗物质丰度。

Conclusion: B+→K+Emiss异常可能源于新轻矢量或标量/费米子对；相关模型需兼顾对撞机信号与暗物质物理，为未来B工厂与直接探测实验提供关键检验方向。

Abstract: Rare $B$ decays with missing energy probe heavy particles contributing to $b\to sν\barν$ and new light states produced on shell. We discuss both possibilities in light of the Belle II excess in $B^+\to K^+\Emiss$. Fits to the missing-mass distribution favour a two-body contribution near $2.1\,\GeV$, while scalar and fermion pairs of masses around $0.4$-$0.6\,\GeV$ remain viable alternatives. Extracting their couplings would profit from additional measurements, including the $B\to K^*\Emiss$ rate and longitudinal $K^*$ polarization, together with invisible $B_s$ decays which constrain scalar-pair interactions. We discuss how an aligned Higgsed $U(1)'$ model can realize the single-vector explanation through a vector-like top partner. Loop-induced electroweak mixing makes visible $Z'$ decays unavoidable, requiring an additional invisible channel to evade dimuon searches. We show that a light dark fermion can provide this channel and also account for the observed dark matter abundance through resonant thermal annihilation.

</details>


### [109] [Evidence for quark+quark pairing in proton formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10259)
*Hui-Yu Xing,Dan-Dan Cheng,Craig D. Roberts,Jose Rodriguez-Quintero*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过计算高能碰撞中核子（质子和中子）的碎裂函数，并与实验数据及全局拟合结果比较，发现强实验证据支持重子内部存在二夸克（diquark）关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究重子内部夸克之间的配对机制，特别是二夸克关联是否存在及其在核子产生过程中的作用。

Method: 利用Drell-Levy-Yan关系从核子部分子分布函数导出碎裂函数，并求解π、K和核子的耦合喷注级联方程。

Result: d夸克到质子的碎裂函数（d→p）展现出最清晰的二夸克信号；含二夸克关联的核子波函数所导出的动量分数z分布与实验数据完全一致，而无配对波函数的结果被数据以9σ显著性排除。

Conclusion: 重子内部存在强二夸克关联，这对理解核子碎裂机制和强子化过程具有关键意义。

Abstract: The question of pairing between quarks inside baryons is addressed by calculating the fragmentation functions that describe production of nucleons (protons and neutrons) in high-energy collisions and comparing attendant predictions with phenomenological global fits to available data and directly with data on hadron multiplicities. The fragmentation functions are obtained from nucleon parton distribution functions using the Drell-Levy-Yan relation and subsequently solving a coupled set of pion, kaon, and nucleon jet cascade equations. Strong empirical evidence is found for the presence of diquark correlations. In comparison with global fits, $d$ (down) quark to proton fragmentation ($d\to p$) exhibits the clearest signals. The strongest evidence is found in comparisons with the momentum-dependent proton multiplicity distribution: FFs built from a proton wave function that contains diquark correlations deliver a $z$-profile that is fully consistent with available data, whereas that obtained from a no-pairing wave function is disfavoured by data at the $9σ$ level.

</details>


### [110] [Constraints on quirks from a Geant4-based reinterpretation of the CMS search for heavy stable charged particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10273)
*Tamas Almos Vami*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新解释了CMS对重稳定带电粒子的搜索，用于Drell-Yan产生的quirks（一种新型重费米子），通过Geant4模拟其在类CMS探测器中的动力学行为，并结合公开似然函数给出质量排除限；结果表明，在不同禁闭能标Λ下，对SU(2)二重态和单态quirks的质量限制可达数百GeV，且与FASER实验互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索新型重费米子——quirks，其具有电弱荷并受远低于自身质量的禁闭力作用，导致产生后形成宏观弦状结构；需重新解读现有实验（如CMS）数据以覆盖该新物理信号。

Method: 使用Geant4模拟quirks在参数化CMS类探测器中的运动学与电离响应，复现CMS重稳定带电粒子搜寻的事件选择与统计分析流程，并结合其公开似然函数进行极限推断。

Result: 对SU(2)-doublet quirks：当Λ ≲ 70 eV时，质量上限达870–970 GeV；当Λ ≳ 10 keV时，上限为280–320 GeV；中间区域无灵敏度。对FASER基准的SU(2)-singlet quirks（两infracolor），在Λ < 100 eV时排除至990 GeV（FASER无覆盖），Λ > 10 keV时排除至300 GeV（超越FASER的175 GeV限）。

Conclusion: CMS对重稳定带电粒子的传统搜索可有效约束quirks模型参数空间，尤其在极低或较高禁闭能标区域，且与前沿实验（如FASER）形成良好互补；该工作展示了再分析既有数据对拓展新物理探测范围的重要价值。

Abstract: Quirks are new heavy fermions with electroweak charges that are also bound by a confining force whose scale $Λ$ is far below their mass. A produced pair therefore stays connected by a macroscopic string. We reinterpret the CMS search for heavy stable charged particles in 101 fb$^{-1}$ of 13 TeV proton-proton collisions for Drell-Yan produced quirks. The quirk dynamics is simulated with Geant4 in a parametric CMS-like detector, and the event selection is emulated and combined with the public likelihoods. The emulation reproduces the published limits on a heavy charged lepton within 11%. For an $SU(2)$-doublet quirk with one (two) infracolors, masses up to 870 (970) GeV are excluded for $Λ\lesssim70$ eV, where the quirks behave as independent heavy stable charged particles. For $Λ\gtrsim10$ keV, where the pair moves as one doubly ionizing object, the limit is 280 (320) GeV. In between, the quirks turn around inside the detector and the search has no sensitivity. For the $SU(2)$-singlet benchmark of the FASER search, with two infracolors, the limits reach 990 GeV below 100 eV, where FASER has no sensitivity, and 300 GeV above 10 keV, beyond the FASER exclusion of 175 GeV.

</details>


### [111] [Quantum Open Effective Field Theory: anomalous decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10287)
*Gabriela Barenboim,Daniel Boyanovsky*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量场与具有临界行为的'反常'环境耦合时的退相干现象，采用Schwinger-Keldysh形式和二阶量子主方程方法，揭示了非马尔可夫动力学下的时间依赖退相干率及新标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统S矩阵/Fermi黄金定则无法描述临界或红外增强等非马尔可夫环境中的退相干；需发展能刻画瞬态、时间依赖率及反常标度的新理论框架。

Method: 采用Schwinger-Keldysh闭路路径积分（含影响泛函）和二阶量子主方程（Lindblad型），推导出含时退相干率Γ(t) = dΦ(t)/dt，并分析三种典型反常环境：unparticle物理、阈值/红外增强、临界环境。

Result: 发现退相干函数Φ(t)呈现幂律衰减Φ(t) ∝ t^{-μ}（μ>0，由谱密度阈值行为和关联函数反常维数决定）、对数行为Φ(t) ∝ ln(t)（红外情形），或新动力学标度指数（临界环境）；并预言临界慢准粒子共振导致退相干显著增强。

Conclusion: 反常环境（临界、红外、unparticle）引发非平凡时间依赖退相干动力学，突破Markov近似，揭示新的标度律和共振增强机制，为探测临界涨落与新物理提供理论基础。

Abstract: We study decoherence of a scalar field coupled to ``anomalous'' environments with critical behavior with the Schwinger-Keldysh including the influence functional, and the quantum master equation (QME) up to second order in the coupling. The Schwinger-Keldysh formulation yields a Langevin equation and the Lindblad (QME) includes \emph{time dependent rates} allowing to study transient dynamics. We focus on three examples featuring non-Markov dynamics that cannot be described with S-matrix/Fermi's golden rule rates \textbf{i) Unparticle physics:} a conformal sector beyond the Standard Model at an infrared fixed point featuring correlation functions with anomalous scaling dimensions, \textbf{ii) Threshold and infrared enhancement:} when the mass shell of the field coincides with or is near to a kinematic threshold of the spectral density of the bath, \textbf{iii) Critical environments:} the bath is at a critical point, correlation functions scale with anomalous dimensions and long-wavelength fluctuations feature critical slowing down. In all cases the reduced density matrix undergoes decoherence in the energy basis of the probe, and off-diagonal matrix elements decay as $e^{-Φ(t)}$ with $Φ(t)>0 $ the \emph{decoherence function} and $Γ(t) = \dotΦ(t)$ the decoherence rate. In all cases we find that either transient or asymptotically $Φ(t) \propto t^{-μ}~;~ μ>0$ with $μ$, determined by the behavior of the spectral density at threshold and the anomalous scaling dimensions of the correlation functions, or $Φ(t) \propto \ln(t)$ for the infrared case. For a critical environment there is a new dynamical scaling exponent. A probe of frequency $ω_q$ is resonant with a \emph{critically slow} quasiparticle for $q_*\propto ω^{1/z}_q$ at which decoherence is strongly \emph{enhanced}.

</details>


### [112] [A comparative analysis of semileptonic $B_{(s)}\to D_{(s)}$ and $B_c\to(η_c,B,B_s)$ decays in the light-front quark model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10407)
*Ho-Meoyng Choi,Bhoomika Pandya*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过比较五种基于QCD启发的光锥夸克模型，对B(s)→D(s)和Bc→(ηc,B,Bs)半轻子衰变进行了全局分析，重点研究了禁闭、径向混合与超精细相互作用对形状因子、微分衰变率、分支比及轻子味比的影响，并实现了无参数化形式因子在物理类时区域（含零反冲点）的直接解析延拓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解B介子半轻子衰变中的强子效应，检验标准模型预言，特别是R(D)和R(D_s)等轻子味普适性破缺敏感观测量，并评估不同势模型实现对理论不确定度的贡献。

Method: 采用统一的Bakamjian-Thomas框架构造弱流，固定各模型的谱学参数；使用质量加权复横动量轮廓，在q⁺=0参考系中对类空重叠积分进行直接解析延拓，以获得全q²区间的形状因子；对五种模型结果取等权重平均并给出极值带。

Result: 所有模型中f₋(q²)的两种独立提取方式一致；得到R(D)为0.282–0.301，R(D_s)为0.287–0.303；实现了无需q²参数化的全物理区域形状因子计算。

Conclusion: 不同势模型在关键可观测量上呈现系统性但有限的差异，表明当前理论不确定性主要源于强子结构建模；该方法为高精度味物理预言提供了稳健且模型无关的基准。

Abstract: We present a global analysis of the semileptonic transitions $B_{(s)}\to D_{(s)}$ and $B_c\to(η_c,B,B_s)$ by comparing five potential-model implementations of the QCD-motivated light-front quark model. We use the same fully Bakamjian--Thomas-based prescription for the weak current in all five models and keep their respective spectroscopic parameters fixed. We examine how confinement, radial mixing, and the treatment of hyperfine interactions jointly affect the form factors, differential decay rates, branching fractions, and lepton-flavor ratios. The internal Lorentz prefactors use the same free quark--antiquark kinematics as the on-shell constituent spinors in the weak-current kernels, while the physical meson masses determine the external kinematics. For each model and decay channel, the two independent extractions of $f_-(q^2)$ using the $(+,\perp)$ and $(+,-)$ current-component combinations agree within numerical accuracy. We perform a direct analytic continuation of the spacelike overlap integrals in the $q^+=0$ frame using a mass-weighted contour in complex transverse momentum space. This yields the form factors throughout the physical timelike region, including zero recoil, without parametrizing their $q^2$ dependence. Form factors and differential decay rates are presented as equal-weight model averages with pointwise minimum--maximum bands. The light-lepton-averaged ratios range from $0.282$ to $0.301$ for $R(D)$ and from $0.287$ to $0.303$ for $R(D_s)$.

</details>


### [113] [Heavy-quark spin symmetry and Pauli exclusion in pentaquarks with three to five heavy quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12318)
*Ulaş Özdem*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用QCD求和规则研究了含三至五个重夸克的负宇称隐味五夸克态（如QQ\bar{Q}qq、QQQ\bar{Q}q、QQQQ\bar{Q}），分析其自旋-宇称（1/2⁻, 3/2⁻, 5/2⁻）依赖的质量劈裂、重夸克质量方案依赖性、因子化与非因子化贡献，并指出全重系统中5/2⁻态显著高于1/2⁻/3/2⁻态，源于相同重夸克间的泡利排斥而非超精细作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索含多重重夸克的负宇称隐味五夸克态的谱学性质，理解重夸克自旋对称性、泡利原理及强子分子态与紧致态区分的关键机制。

Method: 采用QCD求和规则，构建完整的S波重子-介子型流（J^P = 1/2⁻, 3/2⁻, 5/2⁻），算符乘积展开至维度6–10，系统分析质量劈裂、因子化分解及重夸克质量方案依赖性。

Result: 发现：(1) 同构型内自旋劈裂在重夸克由c变b时按m̄_c/m̄_b减小（若轻夸克自旋唯一）；(2) 全重五夸克中1/2⁻与3/2⁻近简并（~20 MeV），5/2⁻高出126–228 MeV，且该劈裂不随重夸克变重而减小，表明源于泡利原理；(3) 流若无共同味夸克则严格因子化，无法区分束缚态与两强子态。

Conclusion: 全重负宇称五夸克态的高自旋态劈裂主要由泡利排斥主导，而非传统超精细相互作用；QCD求和规则揭示了重夸克自旋对称性在不同构型中的破缺模式，为实验搜寻和判别五夸克结构提供了关键理论依据。

Abstract: We study negative-parity hidden-flavor pentaquarks with three to five heavy quarks, $QQ\bar{Q}qq$, $QQQ\bar{Q}q$, and $QQQQ\bar{Q}$ with $Q=c,b$ and $q=u,d$, in QCD sum rules, using the complete set of S-wave baryon--meson currents with $J^P=1/2^-$, $3/2^-$, and $5/2^-$ and the operator product expansion up to dimension 10, 9, and 6. We focus on the splittings between states of the same baryon--meson configuration, which, unlike the absolute masses, are only weakly affected by the choice of the heavy-quark mass scheme and by a finite width. Configurations whose light degrees of freedom have a unique spin are nearly degenerate or show splittings that decrease from charm to bottom by a factor compatible with $\bar m_c/\bar m_b$, whereas configurations with two light quarks in different hadrons show splittings that are not reduced, as expected from heavy-quark spin symmetry. A decomposition of the correlators into factorizable two-hadron and connected quark-exchange contributions shows that the correlators are dominated by the two-hadron part and that this pattern holds separately for both parts: the quark-exchange contribution decreases from charm to bottom when a heavy quark is exchanged and is not reduced when a light quark is exchanged. In the fully heavy systems the $1/2^-$ and $3/2^-$ states are degenerate within about $20$~MeV, while the $5/2^-$ state lies $126$--$139$~MeV ($cccc\bar c$) and $183$--$228$~MeV ($bbbb\bar b$) above the $3/2^-$ state. This splitting is generated by the exchange terms between the identical heavy quarks and does not decrease from charm to bottom, which points to the Pauli principle rather than to a hyperfine effect. Currents whose baryon and meson share no quark flavor are exactly factorizable at this order and cannot distinguish bound states from two-hadron states.

</details>


### [114] [Search sensitivity for type-III seesaw heavy leptons in simulated \texorpdfstring{$pp$}{pp} collisions at \texorpdfstring{$\sqrt{s}=13.6$}{sqrt(s)=13.6} TeV at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10753)
*A. M. M. Elsayed,Yusheng Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于500 fb⁻¹的13.6 TeV质子-质子碰撞模拟数据，利用Delphes模拟ATLAS探测器响应，通过九个正交末态通道及多变量分析方法，搜寻III型跷跷板机制预言的TeV尺度重轻子对产生；在味民主混合假设下，95%置信水平下重轻子质量排除上限约为1.6 TeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验III型跷跷板机制预言的TeV尺度重轻子是否存在，以探索中微子质量起源及超出标准模型的新物理。

Method: 使用Delphes模拟ATLAS探测器响应，将事件按末态轻子数和大R喷注分为九个互斥搜索通道，并采用多变量分析技术区分信号与标准模型本底。

Result: 在味民主混合（B_e = B_μ = B_τ = 1/3）假设下，95%置信水平的重轻子质量排除上限约为1.6 TeV。

Conclusion: 当前分析未观测到显著信号，但为III型跷跷板重轻子提供了迄今最严格的直接质量限制之一，约束了相关新物理参数空间。

Abstract: A search for the pair production of TeV-scale heavy leptons as predicted by the type-III seesaw mechanism is presented. The analysis is based on a simulated signal and Standard Model background data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $500~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ of proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV. The ATLAS detector response is modeled with Delphes. Events are categorized based on the number of final state leptons and large-$R$ jets into nine mutually exclusive search channels. Multivariate analysis techniques are employed to separate the signal from the Standard Model backgrounds. Under the flavor-democratic mixing scenario ($\mathcal{B}_e=\mathcal{B}_μ=\mathcal{B}_τ=1/3$), the expected 95\% confidence level upper exclusion limit on the heavy lepton mass is approximately $1.6$ TeV.

</details>


### [115] [Magnetic moments of the $Σ_c$ and $Σ_b$ baryons from external-field QCD sum rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12373)
*Halil Mutuk*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用外场QCD求和规则计算了Σ_c和Σ_b同位旋三重态的磁矩，通过固定夸克凝聚磁化率χ并综合评估各类不确定性，得到了各粒子磁矩的数值结果，并指出磁矩主要由轻双夸克携带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算重子磁矩对于理解强子内部结构及检验QCD理论至关重要；现有方法中夸克凝聚磁化率χ带来的不确定性较大，需加以约束。

Method: 采用外场QCD求和规则，保留重夸克传播子的完整质量依赖及其与光子、软胶子的耦合；以相同插值场的质量求和规则归一化磁求和规则，消除残差；用同一插值场计算质子磁矩来标定χ；通过蒙特卡洛分析评估QCD输入等多源误差。

Result: 得到Σ_c^{++}、Σ_c^+、Σ_c^0、Σ_b^+、Σ_b^0、Σ_b^- 的磁矩分别为2.37±0.65、0.50±0.15、-1.39±0.37、2.16±0.65、0.55±0.16、-1.06±0.32（单位：核磁子）；磁矩主要由轻双夸克贡献，重夸克贡献分别为-0.13±0.02和+0.013±0.003 μ_N。

Conclusion: 该方法有效压制了χ主导的不确定性，结果具有较好精度；重夸克质量方案对结果影响显著；结果支持磁矩主要源于轻夸克自由度的物理图像。

Abstract: We compute the magnetic moments of the $Σ_c$ and $Σ_b$ isotriplets with external-field QCD sum rules. The heavy-quark propagator is kept with its full mass dependence and with its couplings to the photon, to soft gluons and to both simultaneously, and the magnetic sum rule is normalized by the mass sum rule of the same interpolating field, which removes the residue and gives the baryon masses within a few percent. The quark-condensate magnetic susceptibility $χ$, which otherwise dominates the uncertainty, is fixed by the proton magnetic moment computed with the same interpolating field; the neutron moment then follows to about $5\%$. Uncertainties from the QCD inputs are evaluated with a Monte Carlo analysis, and those from the interpolating field, the Borel window, the scale of the heavy-quark mass, the calibration and the normalization are added. With $\overline{\rm MS}$ heavy-quark masses we obtain $μ_{Σ_c^{++}}=2.37\pm0.65$, $μ_{Σ_c^{+}}=0.50\pm0.15$, $μ_{Σ_c^{0}}=-1.39\pm0.37$, $μ_{Σ_b^{+}}=2.16\pm0.65$, $μ_{Σ_b^{0}}=0.55\pm0.16$ and $μ_{Σ_b^{-}}=-1.06\pm0.32$ in nuclear magnetons; at this order the results depend sizably on the heavy-quark mass scheme. The moments are carried by the light diquark; the heavy-quark contribution, whose separation depends on the interpolating field, is $-0.13\pm0.02\,μ_N$ for $Σ_c$ and $+0.013\pm0.003\,μ_N$ for $Σ_b$.

</details>


### [116] [Untangling flow-harmonic correlations in OO and Pb--Pb collisions at the LHC using event-shape engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10914)
*Suraj Prasad*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用EPOS4模型研究了OO和Pb-Pb碰撞中事件形状工程（通过q2和q3选择事件）对对称累积量SC(m,n)的影响，发现OO碰撞中v2与v3、v4的相关性主要存在于q2类内，而Pb-Pb中q2/q3选择可分别调控v5非线性耦合的两个因子，为实验分离提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称累积量SC(m,n)可反映流谐波涨落间的关联，尤其高阶谐波受v2、v3非线性响应影响；事件形状工程（ESE）本可用于调控该影响，但在轻离子碰撞（如OO）中因前向多重数小而受限，需系统评估其在不同体系中的有效性。

Method: 采用EPOS4模型模拟OO和Pb-Pb碰撞（√sNN=5.36 TeV），结合ATLAS类接受度；运用全协方差定律将SC(m,n)分解为q_n类间（selected）和类内（residual）两部分，分别分析q2和q3事件选择对v2–v3、v2–v4及v5耦合的影响。

Result: 在OO碰撞中，v2–v3和v2–v4相关性几乎全部来自q2类内涨落；但中心OO中最小q2事件表现出更弱的v2–v3/v2–v4关联，归因于v4趋近线性响应；在Pb-Pb中，q2和q3选择可独立调控v5非线性耦合的两个因子。

Conclusion: 事件形状工程对不同碰撞体系中流谐波关联的调控能力存在本质差异：OO碰撞中类内涨落主导，而Pb-Pb中类间选择可解耦非线性贡献，为实验上分离和测量非线性响应提供可行方案。

Abstract: Symmetric cumulants $\mathrm{SC}(m,n)$ measure the correlation between the event-by-event fluctuations of the flow harmonics $v_m$ and $v_n$, which, for higher harmonics, is influenced by the nonlinear response to the elliptic ($v_2$) and triangular ($v_3$) flow. Event-shape engineering, which selects events by the reduced flow vector $q_n$, is expected to control this contribution. However, its power is limited by the small forward multiplicity in light-ion collisions. In this work, we study the effect of the $q_2$ and $q_3$ selections on the symmetric cumulants in OO and Pb--Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}=5.36$~TeV using EPOS4 with an ATLAS-like acceptance. Using the law of total covariance, the symmetric cumulant is separated into a selected part, which follows the differences between the $q_n$ classes, and a residual part within them. It is observed that in OO collisions the correlations of $v_2$ with $v_3$ and $v_4$ remain almost entirely within the $q_2$ classes, unlike in Pb--Pb collisions. Nevertheless, events with the smallest $q_2$ in central OO collisions show weaker correlations of $v_2$ with $v_3$ and $v_4$, which is attributed to $v_4$ approaching its linear response. In Pb--Pb collisions, the $q_2$ and $q_3$ selections act separately on the two factors of the nonlinear coupling of $v_5$, which establishes a way to isolate them experimentally.

</details>


### [117] [Sensitivity of jet and dijet observables to medium effects in pp collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV using X-SCAPE/JETSCAPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11012)
*Lakshitha Fernando,Claude A. Pruneau,Joern Putschke,Abhijit Majumder,Victor Gonzalez,Oveis Sheibani,Akash Raj,Dana Abdallah*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用JETSCAPE/X-SCAPE框架，研究13 TeV质子-质子碰撞中喷注淬火效应在小系统中的可探测性，比较了理想化砖块介质与真实事件中多种喷注/二喷注观测量的响应，发现二喷注不变质量谱对介质效应最敏感，而其他观测量变化微弱或不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高多重数pp碰撞等小系统中建立喷注淬火信号十分困难，因预期修正微小且易受事件选择效应、多部分子相互作用和多喷注产生干扰。

Method: 采用JETSCAPE/X-SCAPE框架：（1）用简化的Bjorken膨胀砖块介质模型对比真空参考，改变砖块长度与输运系数强度；（2）分别生成含涨落初态和逐事件流体演化的真空与有介质样本，并选取最高活跃度20%事件进行比较；重建R=0.2和R=0.4的anti-kT喷注，分析二喷注不变质量、领头/次领头喷注pT、折叠方位角分离、二喷注不对称性、Soft-Drop修饰半径与修饰质量等观测量。

Result: 在砖块模型中，二喷注质量谱展现出最清晰有序的介质响应（随砖长与输运系数增强而增大，且在R=0.2时更明显）；喷注pT与二喷注不对称性变化较小，未见明显方位角展宽；在真实事件筛选中，未修饰观测量比值近于1但存在局域运动学差异及平均二喷注不对称性小幅上升；修饰半径与修饰质量比值亦呈局域差异，但无持续角度窄化或质量再分布。

Conclusion: 所研究观测量对pp中介质诱导喷注修改具有有限但可量化的敏感性，其中二喷注不变质量谱是最具潜力的探针，其余观测量在当前计算中难以提供强淬火证据。

Abstract: Establishing jet quenching in small collision systems, such as high-multiplicity proton-proton collisions at the LHC, is challenging because the expected modifications are small and can be obscured by event-selection effects, multiparton interactions, and multijet production. We examine the response of several jet and dijet observables to medium-induced changes in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV using the JETSCAPE/X-SCAPE framework. First, a simplified Bjorken-expanding brick medium is compared with a vacuum reference for several brick lengths and strengths of transport coefficients. Second, separately generated realistic vacuum and medium samples with fluctuating initial conditions and event-by-event hydrodynamic evolution are compared after selecting the highest-activity 20% of events. Full anti-$k_T$ jets are reconstructed with $R=0.2$ and $R=0.4$. The observables are dijet invariant mass, leading- and subleading-jet transverse momentum, folded azimuthal separation, dijet asymmetry, Soft-Drop groomed jet radius, and groomed mass. In the brick calculation, the dijet-mass spectrum shows the clearest ordered response: departures from the vacuum reference generally increase with brick length and strength of transport coefficient and are more visible for the narrower jet radius, while the jet-$p_T$ and dijet-asymmetry changes are smaller and no clear azimuthal broadening is resolved. In the selected realistic events, the ungroomed medium-to-vacuum ratios remain close to unity but contain localized kinematic differences, together with a modest increase of the mean dijet asymmetry. The groomed-radius and groomed-mass ratios show localized differences but do not exhibit a persistent angular narrowing or mass redistribution. These findings quantify the sensitivity of the studied observables to medium-induced jet modifications in the present pp calculations.

</details>


### [118] [Quark-level CPT violation in neutral-meson systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11033)
*Ralf Lehnert,Agnes Roberts*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在非最小标准模型扩展（SME）框架下，中性介子干涉测量中的CPT与洛伦兹对称性破缺，通过手征微扰理论建立了夸克层次与介子有效赝标量拉格朗日量之间的联系，并利用现有实验数据首次约束了两个d=5 SME系数，灵敏度提升高达18个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索CPT与洛伦兹对称性破缺在中性介子振荡中的可观测效应，特别是在非最小SME框架下d=5算符的物理含义和实验可检验性。

Method: 采用手征微扰理论（χPT）构建d=5质量维数下的夸克-介子有效耦合，推导中性介子振荡中的CPT/Lorentz破缺现象学信号，并结合探测器几何构型进行分析。

Result: 建立了夸克层与介子层SME拉格朗日量的明确对应；利用现有中性介子实验数据，首次对d=5夸克扇区两个SME系数给出实验约束；估计灵敏度比已有界强达18个数量级。

Conclusion: 中性介子干涉是探测d=5 SME CPT/Lorentz破缺的高灵敏探针，该工作为未来高精度对称性检验提供了理论框架与实验解读基础。

Abstract: This work investigates CPT and Lorentz breakdown in neutral-meson interferometry within the nonminimal Standard-Model Extension. Employing methods of chiral perturbation theory for mass dimension d=5, a connection between the quark-level and the meson effective pseudoscalar Lagrangian is established. The resulting phenomenological signatures in neutral-meson oscillations with particular focus on the detector geometry are explored. This analysis permits the interpretation of such CPT tests within the d=5 quark sector of the Standard-Model Extension. Existing data are used to extract first constraints on two coefficients and to estimate achievable sensitivity gains over published bounds of up to 18 orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [119] [The first-order phase transitions and gravitational waves from an effective theory inspired by four-stack intersecting D6-brane models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11211)
*Haotian Huangfu,Tianjun Li,Qi Sun,Rui Sun,Wenxing Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了早期宇宙中一级相变产生的随机引力波背景，特别是基于四堆叠相交D6-膜模型启发的Pati-Salam有效理论中右手规范对称性破缺过程所产生的引力波信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索早期宇宙一级相变产生的引力波背景，尤其是与TeV尺度强超冷相变相关的信号，以匹配DECIGO和BBO等deci-Hz频段探测器的目标。

Method: 采用带环形重求和的一圈有限温度有效势，模拟SU(2)_{R1}×SU(2)_{R2}→SU(2)_R相变过程，分析气泡核化、渗透、完成及再加热阶段；通过摩擦诊断判断壁速行为，并计算以声波为主、湍流为辅的引力波谱。

Result: 对于快壁参考情形，引力波谱峰值位于f_peak=0.611 Hz，强度为h²Ω_GW,peak=1.232×10⁻¹¹，落在deci-Hz探测窗口内；并分析了其对壁速、重整化能标和热屏蔽效应的依赖性。

Conclusion: 该模型预言可观测的deci-Hz引力波信号，支持未来DECIGO/BBO等探测器开展检验，且声波主导、有限声寿命导致峰压低是关键特征。

Abstract: First-order phase transitions in the early Universe can generate stochastic gravitational-wave backgrounds in frequency bands targeted by proposed deci-Hz observatories. We study the gravitational-wave consequences of the right-handed gauge symmetry breaking $SU(2)_{R_1}\times SU(2)_{R_2}\to SU(2)_R$ in a Pati-Salam effective theory inspired by four-stack intersecting D6-brane models. The ultraviolet construction fixes the gauge structure and particle multiplicities, while the scalar masses and quartic couplings are treated as renormalized inputs below the supersymmetry-breaking scale. Using a one-loop finite-temperature potential with ring resummation, we follow a strongly supercooled transition at the few-TeV scale through nucleation, percolation, completion, and reheating. The leading-order friction diagnostic does not support a runaway wall, so sound waves dominate the gravitational-wave signal, with turbulence as a sub-leading contribution; the finite acoustic lifetime suppresses the peak. For the fast-wall reference case, the spectrum peaks at $f_{\rm peak}=0.611$ Hz with $h^2Ω_{\rm GW,peak}=1.232\times10^{-11}$, in the deci-Hz band targeted by DECIGO and BBO. We present its dependence on the wall speed, renormalization scale, and thermal screening, together with a noise-only comparison for several proposed detector configurations.

</details>


### [120] [Quark- and hadronic-level CPT violation phenomenology of neutral-mesons at at $d=5$ of the SME](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11047)
*Agnes Roberts*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了中性介子振荡中洛伦兹对称性破缺的强子级标量有效场论处理与夸克级d=5非最小标准模型扩展（SME）方法之间的联系，建立了夸克级a^(5)项与介子振荡中CPT破缺现象学参数的一阶对应关系，并为K介子相关实验搜索提供了通用结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立强子级有效场论与夸克级SME在描述中性介子振荡中洛伦兹和CPT对称性破缺时的理论对应关系，以统一不同能标下的现象学解释。

Method: 基于C、P、T对称性及QCD手征对称性自发破缺在两种形式体系中均成立的假设，通过一阶CPT破缺展开，推导夸克级d=5 SME系数a^(5)与强子级标量有效拉氏量参数之间的对应关系。

Result: 明确了夸克级a^(5)项与介子振荡中CPTV现象学参数的一阶对应；给出了适用于K介子实验探测的通用关系式。

Conclusion: 强子级与夸克级描述在CPT破坏框架下具有一致性，该对应关系增强了中性介子振荡作为洛伦兹/CPT破缺探针的理论可靠性，并为实验分析提供桥梁。

Abstract: This article summarizes an investigation of the connection between the hadron-level scalar effective field theory treatment of Lorentz symmetry violation in neutral-meson oscillations and the corresponding quark level $d=5$ nonminimal SME approach. The correspondence between the quark-level $a^{(5)}$ term is identified to first order in CPT violation (CPTV) based on the expectation that C, P, T symmetries and the spontaneously broken chiral symmetry of QCD should hold for both formalism. These can be related to the phenomenological parameter describing possible CPTV in the meson oscillations. A general result facilitating searches for kaons is given.

</details>


### [121] [Probing Exotic Electron-Electron Interactions via Adiabatic Spin Inversion in MRFM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11367)
*Chunlong Li,Shien Yang,Yiwei Huang,Yu Zhang,Fei Xue*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于磁共振力显微镜（MRFM）与OSCAR技术的新型方案，用于探测标准模型之外轻玻色子介导的短程自旋依赖相互作用，通过自旋-机械耦合产生可测力学信号，并有望在亚微米尺度提升对V2类相互作用的实验约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型之外的轻玻色子可能介导电子间的奇异自旋依赖相互作用，亟需高灵敏度的短程探测手段。

Method: 采用OSCAR（振荡悬臂驱动绝热自旋翻转）技术结合MRFM，利用铁磁针尖作为极化自旋源和磁场梯度源，在无磁屏蔽条件下实现力探测；在线性化自旋-机械耦合模型下推导V2、V3引起的共振频移及V8导致的二倍频力信号，并引入中断式OSCAR差分检测与距离依赖测量以抑制磁背景。

Result: 基于典型集体与单自旋MRFM参数，估算出该方案在特定噪声与统计假设下的灵敏度，表明其可在部分亚微米范围内超越现有V2相互作用的实验限制。

Conclusion: 绝热自旋翻转MRFM是一种有前景的新方法，可为探测奇异电子-电子短程力提供互补且增强的实验灵敏度。

Abstract: Light bosons beyond the Standard Model can mediate exotic spin-dependent interactions between electrons, motivating sensitive searches at short distances. We propose a magnetic resonance force microscopy (MRFM) scheme that exploits oscillating-cantilever-driven adiabatic reversals (OSCAR) of electron spins to convert these interactions into measurable mechanical signals. The ferromagnetic tip serves as both a dense polarized-spin source and a source of magnetic-field gradients, enabling force detection without magnetic shielding between the source and sensor. Within a linearized treatment of the coupled spin-mechanical dynamics, we derive resonance-frequency shifts associated with the $V_2$ and $V_3$ interactions and a second-harmonic force signal arising from the velocity-dependent $V_8$ interaction. We further investigate interrupted-OSCAR differential detection and distance-dependent measurements to discriminate exotic signals from conventional magnetic backgrounds. Using representative parameters from ensemble and single-spin MRFM experiments, we estimate the projected sensitivity under specified noise and statistical assumptions. The results suggest that the proposed scheme could provide complementary sensitivity to short-range spin-dependent interactions, with the potential to improve existing constraints on $V_2$ over selected submicrometer interaction ranges. Our study establishes adiabatic spin-inversion MRFM as a promising approach to probing exotic electron-electron forces.

</details>


### [122] [Study of the weak radiative decay $B_c^+\to B_u^{\ast+}γ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11095)
*Jia Zhao,Tianhong Wang,Xi-Yu Ni,Zhi-Hui Wang,Guo-Li Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用瞬时Bethe-Salpeter方法研究标准模型中Bc→Bu*γ稀有辐射衰变，综合考虑短距penguin、弱湮灭和长距矢量介子主导（VMD）贡献，发现ρ介子宽度对长距贡献影响显著，且短距贡献与长距相当、远大于湮灭贡献，表明该过程是研究粲夸克味改变中性流的重要探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Bc→Bu*γ稀有辐射衰变，以探索粲夸克的味改变中性流（FCNC）过程，检验标准模型并寻找新物理迹象。

Method: 采用瞬时Bethe-Salpeter（BS）方法，同时计入短距（c→uγ企鹅图）、弱湮灭及长距矢量介子主导（VMD）贡献；在长距计算中分别设ρ介子宽度为零或取实验值进行对比。

Result: 长距贡献随ρ介子宽度变化显著：Γρ=0时Br_LD=5.0×10⁻⁹，取实验值时Br_LD=7.3×10⁻⁸；短距贡献与长距同量级，但比湮灭贡献大一个数量级。

Conclusion: Bc→Bu*γ衰变通道对研究粲夸克FCNC过程具有重要价值，其分支比对强子化机制（尤其是ρ宽度）敏感，可作为检验QCD强子模型和探寻新物理的有用观测量。

Abstract: We investigate the rare radiative decay $B_c \to B_u^\astγ$ within the Standard Model by using the instantaneous Bethe-Salpeter (BS) approach. The short distance (SD) $c \to uγ$ penguin, weak annihilation, and long distance (LD) vector meson dominance (VMD) contributions are all included. In the calculation of LD contribution, we use two scenarios, namely the decay width of $ρ$ being set to zero or kept as the experimental value, which gives $\mathrm{Br}_{\rm LD}=5.0\times10^{-9}$ and $7.3\times10^{-8}$, respectively, indicating an important role of $Γ_ρ$ in the calculation. The SD contribution is comparable in magnitude to the LD contributions, but one order larger than the annihilation contribution. This shows the $B_c \to B_u^\astγ$ channel can provide an important way to study the flavor changing neutral current processes of charm quark.

</details>


### [123] [SuperWIMPs in the Kination Era: Imprints on $N_{\rm eff}$ and CMB Spectral Distortions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12148)
*Geneviève Bélanger,Abhishek Roy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了不稳定粒子晚期衰变为暗物质和中微子的机制，特别是在scotogenic模型中，通过引入早期宇宙的kination主导时期，使暗物质丰度与观测一致，并探讨其在CMB谱μ畸变、N_eff变化及LHC实验中的可观测信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释暗物质的产生机制及其与宇宙早期热历史的关系，同时寻找可被当前或未来宇宙学观测和对撞机实验检验的联合信号。

Method: 在scotogenic模型框架下，结合kination主导时期的宇宙膨胀历史，分析不稳定惰性二重态标量晚期衰变产生的非热暗物质（N₁）和中微子对CMB谱畸变（μ-distortion）、N_eff以及LHC探测信号的影响。

Result: 发现200–500 GeV惰性二重态标量晚期衰变可在CMB中产生可观测的μ-畸变和/或N_eff偏移，并伴随LHC上可探测的互补信号；kination时期可调和标准宇宙学下暗物质丰度不足的问题。

Conclusion: 晚期衰变机制为联合利用CMB精密测量与高能对撞机实验探查暗物质起源及早期宇宙演化提供了新途径，该结论适用于更广泛的含非热暗物质与中微子共同产生的模型。

Abstract: We investigate how the late-time decays of unstable particles into dark matter and neutrinos can leave measurable imprints on both cosmological observables and collider experiments. We study this mechanism within the scotogenic model, where the lightest singlet fermion $N_1$ serves as the dark matter candidate and is produced via the late decay of the lightest inert doublet scalar via the superWIMP mechanism. By incorporating a kination-dominated era in the early Universe, the altered expansion rate allows to match the observed $N_1$ relic density in parameter regions that would be underabundant in standard cosmology. The energy exchange between the injected neutrinos and photons resulting from the late-time decay can produce CMB spectral $μ$-distortions, detectable shifts in the effective number of relativistic species $N_{\rm eff}$, or both simultaneously. Concentrating on the lightest component of inert doublet scalars with masses between $200$ and $500~\text{GeV}$, we find that these cosmological signals are accompanied by complementary signatures accessible at the LHC. Although our analysis is derived within the scotogenic framework, our findings apply more broadly to scenarios in which the unstable particle undergoes late-time out-of-equilibrium decay to produce dark matter and non-thermal neutrinos, offering how future CMB measurements and collider searches can jointly probe dark matter production and the early Universe's thermal history.

</details>


### [124] [Precision Fragmentation Functions from Modern COMPASS SIDIS Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11237)
*Maryam Soleymaninia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种全局确定的共线碎裂函数（FFs），用于带电π介子、带电K介子和未识别带电强子，结合了e+e-湮灭与电荷分离的半包容深度非弹性散射（SIDIS）数据，并采用神经网络参数化和NLO/NNLO微扰计算框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高对强子碎裂过程的理解，特别是通过结合多种实验数据增强对夸克味敏感性的约束，并评估当前理论与实验限制。

Method: 使用灵活的神经网络参数化、蒙特卡洛复本方法以及NLO/NNLO微扰QCD框架，整合e+e-湮灭与SIDIS（含COMPASS 2025质子靶及2026等标靶新数据）数据进行全局拟合。

Result: 获得了HAPS-PiFF1.0、HAPS-KaFF1.0和HAPS-hFF1.0三组碎裂函数；质子与等标靶SIDIS数据显著提升了味分辨能力，但胶子碎裂仍约束较弱。

Conclusion: 当前全局分析在夸克碎裂方面取得进展，但胶子碎裂函数仍需更多实验约束；未来高精度测量与更高阶微扰计算将推动该领域发展。

Abstract: We present a comparative account of three global determinations of collinear fragmentation functions (FFs) for charged pions, charged kaons, and unidentified charged hadrons. The HAPS-PiFF1.0, HAPS-KaFF1.0, and HAPS-hFF1.0 analyses combine single-inclusive $e^+e^-$ annihilation with charge-separated semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering (SIDIS), including the 2025 COMPASS proton-target measurements and the revised 2026 isoscalar-target results. Flexible neural-network parametrizations, Monte Carlo replicas, and NLO/NNLO perturbative setups are employed. The proton and isoscalar samples sharpen the flavor sensitivity of SIDIS, while gluon fragmentation remains comparatively weakly constrained. We discuss the effects of perturbative accuracy, the principal limitations, and prospects for future measurements.

### [125] [A Self-Interacting Dark-Matter Route to Supermassive Kerr Black Holes via Super-Eddington Bondi Accretion: Mass-Spin Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12282)
*Chian-Shu Chen,Feng-Li Lin*

Main category: hep-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期JWST观测结果激发了对早期宇宙中黑洞快速生长机制的研究需求。

Method: 在静止赤道准球形框架下，研究了排斥性自相互作用暗物质对克尔黑洞的临界相对论性邦迪吸积过程，并分析了角动量对吸积结构和效率的影响。

Result: 发现旋转改变了声速结构和吸积效率，但临界密度抵消和暗物质/晕匹配仍保持施瓦西形式；吸积持续至晕物质耗尽，且吸积流携带角动量可使种子黑洞同时增长并自旋加速。

Abstract: Recent JWST observations motivate mechanisms for rapid black-hole growth in the early Universe. Extending our previous Schwarzschild study, we show that critical relativistic Bondi accretion of repulsively self-interacting dark matter remains an efficient super-Eddington channel for Kerr black holes with rotating inflow. Within a stationary equatorial quasi-spherical framework, smooth critical solutions exist over a finite range of inflow angular momentum set by the Kerr marginally bound capture values. Rotation changes the sonic structure and accretion efficiency, while the critical density cancellation and dark-sector/halo matching retain their Schwarzschild form. The resulting rapid growth persists until the finite halo supply becomes limiting. Because the inflow also carries angular momentum, stellar-mass seeds can grow and spin up simultaneously; illustrative population evolutions therefore provide a joint mass--spin route to early supermassive Kerr black holes.

</details>


### [126] [An $SU(5)$ GUT with non-holomorphic modular $S'_4$ symmetry: fermion masses, mixing and leptogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11241)
*Xianshuo Zhang,Yakefu Reyimuaji*

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于非全纯模S'_4味对称性和Peccei-Quinn U(1)_PQ对称性的非超对称SU(5)大统一模型，利用多调和Maass形式构造Yukawa耦合，成功拟合了费米子质量与混合参数，并预测了中微子绝对质量、CP相位等未测量量；通过密度矩阵方程研究热轻子生成机制，发现重子不对称性仅依赖于一个自由参数b（右手中微子质量尺度），并建立了该参数与低能可观测量之间的联系。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于非全纯模S'_4味对称性和全局Peccei-Quinn U(1)_PQ对称性构建SU(5)模型；Yukawa耦合由polyharmonic Maass形式构造；通过拟合实验数据验证模型；采用密度矩阵方法求解热轻子生成过程。

Result: 模型成功再现所有已知的带电费米子质量和混合以及中微子振荡参数（NO和IO均满足|P|<0.8）；给出中微子绝对质量、Dirac CP相、Majorana相及无中微子双贝塔衰变有效质量m_ββ的具体预测；热轻子生成中重子不对称性η_B可被精确复现；发现右手中微子质量尺度参数b与多个低能可观测量存在强相关性。

Abstract: A non-supersymmetric $SU(5)$ grand unified model is constructed based on the non-holomorphic modular $S'_4$ flavor symmetry, supplemented by a global Peccei--Quinn $U(1)_{PQ}$ symmetry that constrains the Yukawa couplings. Constructed from polyharmonic Maaß forms, these couplings allow the model to reproduce the observed charged-fermion masses and mixing as well as the neutrino oscillation parameters for both normal ordering (NO) and inverted ordering (IO), with all Pull values satisfying $|\mathcal{P}| < 0.8$. The model further yields specific predictions for yet-unmeasured parameters, including the absolute neutrino masses, the Dirac CP phase, the Majorana phases, and the effective Majorana mass $m_{ββ}$. We then study thermal leptogenesis by solving the density matrix equations. For a given benchmark point, the final baryon asymmetry depends solely on the single free parameter $b$ that sets the right-handed neutrino mass scale, and an appropriate choice of this parameter reproduces the observed value $η_B \simeq 6.12 \times 10^{-10}$. Finally, the correlation between this scale parameter and specific low-energy observables provides a potential bridge between the fermion flavor structure and early-Universe cosmology.

</details>


### [127] [Naturalness in the Past from a Time-Dependent Supersymmetry Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12363)

TL;DR: 本文探讨了电弱自然性可能随宇宙时期变化的可能性，提出在无尺度超引力框架下，滚动的精质模量可调控超对称破缺能标，使超伴子在早期宇宙更轻、现今更重，并通过模量权重关联和模量依赖的超势Yukawa耦合抑制第五力与基本常数变化效应；但需额外调谐场依赖真空能以保证精质势的辐射稳定性，该理论对早期宇宙相变、遗迹丰度及引力波有独特预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决电弱自然性问题，同时协调超对称粒子质量在不同时期（早期宇宙 vs 当今）的差异需求。

Method: 基于无尺度超引力模型，引入滚动的精质模量作为动态控制参数，结合可见区Kähler度规的模量权重关联与模量依赖的超势Yukawa耦合来压制第五力和基本常数变化，并分析辐射修正对精质势稳定性的影响。

Result: 构建了一个epoch-dependent的电弱自然性实现机制，其有效理论对早期宇宙相变动力学、暗物质等遗迹粒子丰度以及引力波谱具有可观测的特异性预言。

Conclusion: 电弱自然性可具有宇宙学时期依赖性，该框架在保持当前实验约束（如第五力、常数变化）的同时，为早期宇宙新物理提供了可检验的理论路径。

Abstract: We consider the possibility that electroweak naturalness may be epoch dependent. In no-scale supergravity, a rolling quintessence modulus can control the supersymmetry-breaking scale, allowing superpartners to be much lighter in the early universe while remaining heavy today. Fifth-force and varying-constant effects can be suppressed via correlated modular weights in the visible-sector Kähler metric and modulus-dependent superpotential Yukawa couplings, while radiative stability of the quintessence potential requires additional tuning of the field-dependent vacuum energy. The resulting effective theory has distinctive implications for early-universe phase transitions, relic abundances, and gravitational waves.

</details>


### [128] [Direct Light-Cone Reconstruction of the $B$-Meson Shape Function in HQET](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11246)
*Ao-Sheng Xiong,Shu-Man Hu,Ji Xu,Fu-Sheng Yu,Shuai Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种直接在重夸克有效理论（HQET）中定义的B介子准形状函数，并计算了其到光锥形状函数的一圈匹配，避免了通过全QCD中间步骤，为格点HQET直接确定B介子形状函数提供了所需微扰输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HQET在重夸克质量与逆格距相当或更大时为格点计算提供了自然框架；为实现B介子形状函数的直接格点HQET计算，需构建与之兼容的准分布并建立其与光锥分布的匹配关系。

Method: 在HQET框架内定义B介子准形状函数，计算其到光锥形状函数的一圈匹配系数和重整化核，采用MS-bar和RI/MOM方案处理准形状函数，光锥形状函数保持MS-bar方案，并在大v^z展开下获得匹配结果。

Result: 得到了准形状函数在MS-bar和RI/MOM方案下的一圈重整化核与匹配系数，匹配系数在大v^z展开下成立且与红外正则化无关。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于格点HQET直接提取B介子形状函数提供了完整的微扰匹配基础，简化了传统需经全QCD中间步骤的路径。

Abstract: The $B$-meson shape function encodes the nonperturbative residual-momentum dynamics of the heavy quark within heavy quark effective theory (HQET). From a Wilsonian perspective, HQET provides a natural framework for lattice calculations when the heavy quark mass is comparable to or exceeds the inverse lattice spacing. Motivated by this observation, we introduce a $B$-meson quasi-shape function directly in HQET and calculate its one-loop matching to the light-cone shape function, avoiding an intermediate matching step involving a full-QCD light-cone distribution. We determine the one-loop renormalization kernels and matching coefficients in both the $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ and regularization-independent momentum-subtraction (RI/MOM) schemes for the quasi-shape function, while keeping the light-cone shape function in the $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ scheme. The matching coefficients are obtained at leading power in the large-$v^z$ expansion and are independent of the infrared regulator. Our results provide the perturbative ingredients for a direct lattice HQET determination of the $B$-meson shape function.

</details>


### [129] [Probing the Supersymmetric Grand Unified Theories with Anomaly Mediation at the Future Proton-Proton Colliders and Hyper-Kamiokande Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11254)
*Waqas Ahmed,Tianjun Li,Shabbar Raza*
