TL;DR: 本文研究了基于大沙漠假设的超对称大统一理论（SUSY GUTs），采用最小反常介导超对称破缺（AMSB）机制，探讨其在100 TeV质子对撞机（如FCC-hh、SppC）和Hyper-Kamiokande质子衰变实验中的可探测性。结果表明：当GUT标度MGUT ≥ 0.84×10¹⁶ GeV时，胶微子和前两代标量夸克质量上限分别为8 TeV和6 TeV，可在未来对撞机中探测；当MGUT ≤ 0.84×10¹⁶ GeV时，六维质子衰变寿命≤~5×10³⁴年，可在Hyper-K中探测。文中还给出了对应两类情形的基准点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续前期关于SUSY GUTs在100 TeV对撞机及质子衰变实验中可探测性的研究计划，结合大沙漠假设与最小AMSB机制，系统评估其唯象学可行性与实验信号。

Method: 基于大沙漠假设和最小AMSB机制构建SUSY GUT模型，计算不同GUT标度下胶微子、标量夸克等超粒子质量谱，并结合LHC外推及未来对撞机探测能力、Hyper-K质子衰变灵敏度进行分析，给出典型基准点。

Result: 1) MGUT ≥ 0.84×10¹⁶ GeV时，胶微子质量上限8 TeV、前两代标量夸克上限6 TeV，可在FCC-hh/SppC探测；2) MGUT ≤ 0.84×10¹⁶ GeV时，六维质子衰变寿命≤~5×10³⁴年，可在Hyper-K探测；3) 给出两类GUT标度条件下的具体基准点，并讨论了胶微子与winos的探测策略及其它超粒子质量谱特征。

Conclusion: 该框架下SUSY GUT具有清晰的可检验性：高MGUT情形主要依赖100 TeV对撞机探测强相互作用超粒子，低MGUT情形则由Hyper-K质子衰变实验主导检验，二者形成互补探针，为未来高能物理实验提供了明确方向。

Abstract: With the grand desert hypothesis, we investigate the Supersymmetric (SUSY) Grand Unified Theories (GUTs) with the minimal anomaly mediated supersymmetry breaking (AMSB) as a continuation of our program of studying SUSY GUTs at the future 100 TeV proton-proton Collider such as $\rm FCC_{hh}$ and SppC as well as proton-decay experiment such as Hyper-Kamiokande experiment. We show that for the GUT scale $M_{GUT} \ge 0.84 \times 10^{16}$ GeV, the upper mass bounds on the gluino and first two generation squarks are 8 TeV and 6 TeV, respectively. Therefore, the gluino and first two generation squarks can be probed at the $\rm FCC_{hh}$ and SppC. In addition, we discuss the mass spectra for the colored sparticles, sleptons, pseudo-scalar Higgs and electroweakinos. We also shed light on the possible search strategies of gluino and winos at the future 100 TeV colliders. Moreover, for $\rm M_{\rm GUT} \le 0.84 \times 10^{16}$ GeV, the proton lifetime for dimension-six proton decay via heavy gauge boson exchange is less than or around $5 \times 10^{34}$ years, and thus we can probe it at the Hyper-Kamiokande experiment. To be concrete, we present a couple of benchmark points representing the above two mentioned GUT conditions on $\rm M_{\rm GUT}$.

</details>


### [130] [Quality time in extra dimensions: axion quality and radion cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12765)
*Falguni Biswas,Yeshan Juberi,Ahmed Rakin Kamal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维圆紧致化中由阿贝尔规范场产生的轴子及其强CP问题的解决，重点分析了通过Casimir能稳定半径/径子的两种机制，并探讨了电荷物质和磁规整对轴子解的影响及径子衰变对暗物质丰度的稀释效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高形式对称性虽可保护额外维轴子，但额外的对称性破缺仍可能破坏强CP问题的解；本文旨在探讨在五维圆紧致化中如何通过稳定径子来维持轴子解的有效性。

Method: 采用五维圆紧致化模型，轴子源自阿贝尔规范场，半径动力学；利用中性于阿贝尔规范场的场的Casimir能量（含/不含绕紧致圆周期性标量）稳定径子；分析电荷物质与磁规整的破缺效应、径子质量与衰变通道、以及再加热过程。

Result: Minkowski极小值下径子质量为100–170 TeV，电荷物质导致残余强CP角约10^{-17}；磁规整在小耦合下兼容；增大半径抑制电荷破缺但增强磁破缺；径子衰变使轴子丰度稀释，f_a=10^{14} GeV且初始倾角θ_i≈0.43时可解释全部暗物质；辐射主导情形需θ_i≈0.065；可见扇区再加热温度约85 MeV，ΔN_eff≈0.02。

Conclusion: 在所考虑模型中，通过Casimir机制稳定径子可在保持轴子解有效性的同时协调暗物质产生与宇宙学演化，为强CP问题与暗物质统一提供可行框架。

Abstract: Higher-form symmetries can protect extra-dimensional axions, but additional symmetry breaking can still spoil the solution to the strong CP problem. We focus on electrically charged matter and magnetic gauging in a five-dimensional circle compactification where the axion arises from an Abelian gauge field and the radius is dynamical. We discuss two mechanisms for stabilising the radius/radion using Casimir energy from fields neutral under the Abelian gauge field, with and without an additional periodic scalar winding around the compact circle. Both admit Minkowski and de Sitter minima. At the Minkowski minima (with radion masses of $100$--$170\,\mathrm{TeV}$), electrically charged matter induces a residual strong CP angle of order $10^{-17}$ and shifts the radius negligibly. Magnetic gauging remains compatible after including its additional degree of freedom in the Casimir spectrum, provided its coupling is sufficiently small. We observe that increasing the radius suppresses charged-matter breaking relative to QCD but increases magnetic breaking contribution. We also determine the radion decay channels, including the graviphoton, and study reheating from a possible radion-dominated era without scalar winding or magnetic gauging. For a non-minimal Higgs-curvature coupling $ξ=7$, the visible-sector reheating temperature is about $85\,\mathrm{MeV}$ with a dark-radiation contribution of $ΔN_{\rm eff}\approx0.02$. The entropy released during radion decay dilutes the axion abundance, allowing axions with $f_a=10^{14}\,\mathrm{GeV}$ and an initial misalignment angle $\vartheta_i\approx0.43$ to account for the entire observed dark matter abundance. For the same decay constant, reproducing this abundance in radiation domination requires $\vartheta_i\approx0.065$.

</details>


### [131] [Rapid, High-Precision Numerical Evaluation of Multiloop Feynman Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11292)
*Richard Easther*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出使用Sinc函数逼近法数值计算多圈费曼图，相较于蒙特卡洛方法，在高精度（如六位有效数字）下显著提升效率，尤其适用于电子g-2的3环、4环乃至单个5环图计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高多圈费曼图数值计算的精度与效率，尤其是电子反常磁矩（g-2）相关高阶修正的精确评估需求。

Method: 采用Sinc函数逼近将多维积分转化为快速收敛的多重求和，并与蒙特卡洛方法进行系统性对比。

Result: Sinc方法在约1%精度时与蒙特卡洛相当；达六位精度时，3环图计算仅需数分钟，而蒙特卡洛需数年；成功计算了单个5环g-2积分，并探讨了可提升性能的外推技术。

Conclusion: Sinc函数方法是一种高效、高精度且具普适潜力的多圈费曼图数值计算新策略，有望推广至更一般费曼图。

Abstract: We numerically evaluate multiloop Feynman diagrams by using Sinc function approximations to transform the integrals into rapidly convergent, multidimensional sums. We compare the efficiency of this approach to Monte Carlo evaluations of electron g-2 terms. We show that the two strategies reach parity at precisions of around 1% for the full sets of three and four loop diagrams. Beyond this level Sinc function methods are dramatically faster - computing 3-loop integrals to six digit precision takes minutes whereas Monte Carlo timings map to years. We show that the method can evaluate individual 5-loop g-2 integrals and discuss extrapolation techniques that would further improve performance. We note that the method appears to be applicable to more general diagrams.

</details>


### [132] [How to isolate charm production mechanisms using charm-anticharm rapidity and azimuthal angle differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11422)
*Marcus O'Riordan,Peter Christiansen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用PYTHIA模拟研究了强子对撞中粲夸克与反粲夸克强子之间的关联，发现不同产生机制（硬过程、末态劈裂、初态辐射）在快度差Δy和方位角差上具有显著不同的分布特征；通过选择Δy可有效增强或压制末态劈裂贡献，从而区分产生机制；该方法可用于LHC及重离子碰撞实验，以探测粲夸克在夸克胶子等离子体中的不同介质效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粲夸克在强子对撞中的产生机制多样且难以从第一性原理计算，需借助可观测关联来区分不同产生过程。

Method: 基于PYTHIA事件生成器的数值模拟，分析粲与反粲强子在快度差（Δy）和方位角差（Δφ）上的关联分布，并结合LHCb Run 1的D⁰D̄⁰关联数据进行比对。

Result: 硬过程、末态劈裂和初态辐射产生的粲夸克在Δy-Δφ相空间中占据明显不同区域；通过Δy选切可显著调控末态劈裂成分；该方法有望在更大统计量的LHC数据及重离子碰撞中进一步区分介质对不同来源粲夸克的影响。

Conclusion: 粲-反粲强子关联，尤其是快度差Δy，是区分其产生机制的有效探针；该方法为理解QGP中重夸克与胶子的不同能量损失机制提供了新途径。

Abstract: Charm production in hadronic collisions proceeds via many different processes, which makes it hard to calculate from first principles. Using PYTHIA simulations, we study correlations between charm and anticharm hadrons and show that hard-process charm, final-state splitting charm, and initial-state-shower charm populate markedly different regions of rapidity and azimuthal-angle difference. In particular, selections on the pair rapidity separation, $Δy$, can strongly enrich or suppress the near-side final-state splitting contribution and thereby provide experimental sensitivity to the different production processes. A comparison with existing LHCb $D^0\bar{D}^0$ correlation measurements from Run 1 motivates more differential studies using the substantially larger LHC data sets now available. Extending such measurements to heavy-ion collisions could further distinguish medium modifications of charm produced directly in the hard scattering from charm originating from a parent gluon, providing a new handle on the different interactions of heavy quarks and gluons with the quark-gluon plasma.

</details>


### [133] [After the Higgs: Looking for the Universe Behind the Particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11458)
*Veronica Sanz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了希格斯玻色子从被发现的目标转变为探索自然真空、检验量子场论和寻找新物理原理的工具的角色转变，并讨论了在缺乏理论保证新物理存在的情况下，如何规划基础科学研究的未来路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在希格斯玻色子发现十四年后，其角色已从被搜寻对象转变为探索自然基本规律的工具；同时，当前缺乏理论保证新物理在实验可及范围内，且基础科学长期支持日益困难，因此需重新思考研究策略。

Method: 作者回顾了从理论驱动的发现到精密测量计划的发展路径，分析希格斯场的独特性及其与宇宙学等其他领域的联系，并提出应对未知发现路径的研究规划原则。

Result: 提出四项核心建议：追求精度但不预设关键测量、维持多样化的实验布局、保护理论研究的风险承担空间、以及基于基础探索自身价值向公众阐明其意义。

Conclusion: 希格斯玻色子因其独特性质成为连接粒子物理与宇宙学等多领域的桥梁；面对无定论指引的探索时代，应以开放、多元和自主的方式推进基础科学研究。

Abstract: Fourteen years after its discovery, the Higgs boson has changed roles. It was once the object of the search, but today it is an instrument with which we interrogate Nature's vacuum, test quantum field theory and look for new principles. This shift comes at a time when, unlike before the Higgs discovery, we have no theoretical argument guaranteeing new physics within experimental reach. At the same time, securing long-term support for fundamental science is becoming increasingly challenging. I revisit the path from a theoretically motivated discovery to a precision programme, explain why the Higgs is such an unusual quantum field, and argue that this same peculiarity makes it a gateway to cosmology and other sectors. I then turn to a less technical question: how should a field plan for exploration when no theorem guarantees the next discovery? My answer is to pursue precision without pretending to know in advance which measurement will matter, sustain a diverse experimental portfolio, protect theoretical risk-taking, and make the public case for fundamental exploration on its own terms.

</details>


### [134] [Short-range dynamics of $X(3872)$ and $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ in a coupled-channel framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11472)
*Fang-Zheng Peng,Li-Sheng Geng,Ju-Jun Xie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了X(3872)和Tcc(3875)+的短程动力学，采用耦合通道方法处理D D̄* 和 D D* 系统，结合接触相互作用、矢量介子交换饱和与显式π交换（减去局域项），并纳入D*自能以反映三体动力学。通过Tcc极点固定接触项后，调节矢量介子交换磁偶极项可预测X(3872)极点位置；结果表明纯分子态描述存在张力，Tcc更可能源于DD*与紧致ccūd̄四夸克态的混合；空间结构分析显示二者均含少量短程成分（X约3–10%，Tcc约1–14%），支持联合处理分子与紧致自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决X(3872)与Tcc(3875)+在纯分子图像下的理论张力，探究其是否均为松散束缚分子态，或其中Tcc需引入紧致四夸克成分；同时厘清π交换与矢量介子交换局域项对束缚能及同位旋破缺的影响。

Method: 采用S波耦合通道Lippmann-Schwinger方程，包含D*D̄/DD*通道、D*自能（体现D D̄ π / D D π三体效应）、显式π交换（减去局域项）及由标量/矢量介子交换饱和导出的接触相互作用；利用Feshbach投影分析分子与紧致成分耦合；结合Born-Oppenheimer有效场论计算不同径向分离下的组分权重。

Result: 1）保留完整局域磁偶极项时，X(3872)预测极点比实验质量低5–12 MeV；2）π交换对X的束缚能影响中等，但显著增强其同位旋破缺；3）Feshbach分析表明Tcc不能单靠DD*分子解释，需混入紧致ccūd̄态；4）X与Tcc的短程（紧致）成分占比分别为3.0–9.7%和1.3–14.4%（0.5–1.5 fm半径内）。

Conclusion: X(3872)可较好由分子图像描述，但Tcc(3875)+本质是DD*分子与紧致四夸克态的混合态；应保留矢量介子交换的局域贡献，并在统一框架下协同处理长程分子与短程紧致自由度。

Abstract: We study the short-range dynamics in the coupled-channel description of the $S$-wave $D \bar{D}^*$ and $DD^*$ systems for $X(3872)$ and $T_{cc}(3875)^+$, respectively. Contact interactions are related through scalar- and vector-meson exchange saturation, while pion exchange is included explicitly with its local term subtracted. The coupled Lippmann-Schwinger equations incorporate the self-energy of the unstable $D^*$ mesons, thereby accounting for the three-body $D \bar{D} π$ ($D Dπ$) dynamics. Fixing the contact interaction from the $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ pole, we trace the predicted $X(3872)$ pole as the local magnetic dipole term from vector-meson exchange is varied. Retaining this term in full produces the shallowest poles, which remain approximately $5$--$12~\mathrm{MeV}$ below the observed $X(3872)$ mass. It is found that the pion exchange has a moderate effect on the binding energy and a stronger effect on the isospin violation for the $X(3872)$ state. The residual tension between the pure-molecular bound descriptions of $X(3872)$ and $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ is analyzed with the Feshbach projection. This analysis indicates a $DD^*$ interaction weaker than that implied by the purely molecular fits, and shows that the $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ state instead arises from the mixing of the $DD^{*}$ channel with a compact $cc\bar{u}\bar{d}$ state. We further examine the spatial structure using Born--Oppenheimer effective field theory. For separation radii of $0.5$--$1.5~\mathrm{fm}$, the combined charmonium and short-distance tetraquark weight in $X(3872)$ is $3.0$--$9.7\%$, while the short-distance weight in $T_{cc}(3875)^+$ is $1.3$--$14.4\%$. These results favor retaining the local vector-exchange contribution within the saturation framework and motivate a joint treatment of molecular and compact dynamics for the $T_{cc}(3875)^+$.

</details>


### [135] [Next-to-leading-order QCD corrections to radiative decays of fully heavy tetraquarks into quarkonia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11511)
*Yu-Dong Zhang,Zai-Fu Jiang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在非相对论QCD因子化框架下，计算了四夸克态T_{4Q}（Q=c,b）到S波粲偶素/底偶素加光子衰变的NLO QCD修正，给出了短距系数解析表达式，并预测了LHC上的可观测事例产额。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究四夸克态T_{4Q}=QQ\bar Q\bar Q的电磁衰变特性，特别是其到重夸克偶素加光子的衰变过程，以深化对多夸克强子结构与QCD动力学的理解，并为实验探测提供理论依据。

Method: 采用非相对论QCD（NRQCD）因子化方法，在重夸克速度展开的领头阶下，计算T_{4Q}^{0^{++}}, T_{4Q}^{2^{++}}, T_{4Q}^{1^{+-}}到V_Q+γ或η_Q+γ衰变的下一阶（NLO）量子色动力学（QCD）修正；精确处理紫外发散并通过对耦合常数和波函数的重整化实现抵消；推导出O(αα_s^5)精度下的短距系数解析形式，包含0^{++}态两种颜色构型的干涉项。

Result: NLO修正显著增强衰变宽度：T_{4Q}^{2^{++}}→V_Q+γ增强约2.94（2.33）倍，T_{4Q}^{1^{+-}}→η_Q+γ增强约2.22（1.83）倍；多数通道中重整化尺度μ_R依赖性在NLO下减弱；预测在√s=13 TeV、积分亮度100 fb^{-1}的pp碰撞中，T_{4c}→J/ψ+γ产额达10^6–10^7量级，T_{4c}→η_c+γ约10^4量级。

Conclusion: NLO QCD修正对四夸克态T_{4Q}的光子辐射衰变至关重要，显著提升理论预言精度与可靠性；T_{4c}→J/ψ+γ信号可通过ℓ^+ℓ^-γ不变质量谱清晰识别，为LHC上四夸克态的发现与性质研究提供了新且互补的探针。

Abstract: Within nonrelativistic QCD factorization, we calculate the next-to-leading-order (NLO) QCD corrections to the decays of compact $T_{4Q}=QQ\bar Q\bar Q$ states ($Q=c,b$) into an $S$-wave quarkonium and a photon. Conservation of charge conjugation allows $T_{4Q}^{0^{++}}$ and $T_{4Q}^{2^{++}}$ to decay into $V_Q+γ$, with $V_c=J/ψ$ and $V_b=Υ$, while $T_{4Q}^{1^{+-}}$ can decay into $η_Q+γ$. At leading order in the heavy quark velocity expansion, we derive the short-distance coefficients for these decay widths in analytic form through $\mathcal{O}(αα_s^5)$, including the interference contribution between the two color configurations of $T_{4Q}^{0^{++}}$. All ultraviolet divergences at NLO are exactly canceled by renormalization. The NLO QCD corrections enhance the decay widths for $T_{4Q}^{2^{++}}\to V_Q+γ$ and $T_{4Q}^{1^{+-}}\toη_Q+γ$ by factors of approximately $2.94$ ($2.33$) and $2.22$ ($1.83$), respectively. The dependence on the renormalization scale $μ_R$ is reduced at NLO in most channels, although it remains sizable. Based on the NLO branching fractions, we estimate yields of order $10^6$--$10^7$ for $T_{4c}\to J/ψ+γ$ and $10^4$ for $T_{4c}\toη_c+γ$ in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13~\mathrm{TeV}$ with an integrated luminosity of $100~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$. The $J/ψ+γ$ channels can be investigated through $J/ψ\to\ell^+\ell^-$ in the $\ell^+\ell^-γ$ invariant-mass spectrum, complementing di-charmonium searches.

</details>


### [136] [Spin entanglement in $pp$ and $pA$ collisions: high-energy TMD effects and fragmentation probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11568)
*Michael Fucilla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在k_T因子化框架下，利用冲击波形式理论研究了质子-质子或质子-离子碰撞中产生的夸克-反夸克对的自旋-自旋关联，构建了包含和衍射通道的完整自旋密度矩阵，并揭示了线性偏振胶子导致的量子纠缠与非局域性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高能强子碰撞中夸克对的量子纠缠特性，特别是其自旋关联如何受小-x胶子分布、饱和效应及Pomeron交换机制影响，为探测QCD真空结构和胶子极化提供新途径。

Method: 采用k_T-因子化与冲击波形式理论，在背对背极限下构造包含与衍射通道的自旋密度矩阵；结合小-x胶子TMDs、BFKL演化与饱和模型分析纠缠度量（如concurrence、Rényi熵、量子失谐）及Bell非局域性；关联Collins与二强子碎裂函数以提出实验可观测量。

Result: 在包含通道中发现由线性偏振胶子引起的cos(2φ)调制的量子纠缠信号，该信号在稀疏（BFKL）极限下最强且与横向动量失衡q无关，但在饱和区域显著压低；在衍射通道中夸克对始终纠缠，其自旋密度矩阵秩至多为二、边缘态最大混合，导致纠缠与Bell非局域性等价并全域存在；提出双Collins不对称性作为无极化强子碰撞中的纠缠探针。

Conclusion: 夸克-反夸克对的自旋关联是探测小-x胶子自旋结构与QCD色玻璃凝聚体特性的灵敏量子探针；衍射过程因Pomeron的排他性和偶极子性质天然支持鲁棒量子关联，而包含过程中的调制效应可区分胶子饱和与否；实验上可通过碎裂函数相关观测量实现验证。

Abstract: Spin-spin correlations of a quark-antiquark pair produced in proton-proton or proton-Ion collisions are investigated in $k_T$-factorization within the shockwave formalism. We construct the full spin-density matrix of both inclusive and diffractive channels in the limit in which the two quarks are back-to-back. In the inclusive channel, due to linearly polarized gluons, we show that a $\cos (2 φ)$-modulation of the concurrence, stabilizer Rényi entropy, and quantum discord emerges and depends on the interplay between six small-$x$ gluon TMDs. The modulation is predicted to be maximally strong and independent of the transverse momentum imbalance, $q$, from a dilute (BFKL) perspective, while it is considerably suppressed by saturation effects. In diffraction, we observe that the pair is always entangled and exhibits the same properties found in diffractive DIS. We explain how these properties follow directly from the exclusive and eikonal nature of the Pomeron exchange. We trace this behavior to a spin-density matrix of rank at most two with maximally mixed marginals. For this class of states, entanglement and Bell nonlocality coincide even when the state is mixed, while the explicit form of the light-front helicity amplitudes ensures that both persist in the whole phase space. We suggest accessing the spin correlations in unpolarized hadron collisions through the Collins and dihadron fragmentation functions. We point out that, in inclusive production, for massless quarks, suitably chosen double-Collins asymmetries provide promising entanglement witnesses.

</details>


### [137] [Hadronic final-state effects on open-charm production at LHC energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11569)
*Taesoo Song,Elena Bratkovskaya,Krzysztof Redlich*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用PHSD输运模型，探讨了强子末态相互作用对LHC能区pp和Pb+Pb碰撞中开粲强子观测量的影响，发现末态强子散射对积分产额影响小，但显著改变横动量谱和椭圆流，表明在利用微分开粲观测量提取QGP性质时必须考虑强子相演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从微分开粲强子观测量（如横动量谱、椭圆流）中提取夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）性质时，需厘清强子末态相互作用的影响程度。

Method: 采用微观Parton-Hadron-String Dynamics (PHSD)输运模型，对比包含与不包含粲介子强子再散射的模拟结果，并与ALICE实验数据及统计强子化模型预测进行比较。

Result: 相对积分D介子产额对强子相互作用不敏感；PHSD成功再现非奇异D介子比值的弱系统依赖性及Ds+/D0比值从pp到Pb+Pb的上升趋势；强子再散射显著重分布低—中横动量谱并增强椭圆流v2。

Conclusion: 强子相演化对微分开粲观测量有显著影响，忽略其效应可能导致对QGP性质的误判；因此，在利用此类观测量提取QGP信息时，必须纳入强子末态相互作用。

Abstract: We investigate the influence of hadronic final-state interactions on open-charm observables in pp and Pb+Pb collisions at LHC energies within the microscopic Parton-Hadron-String Dynamics (PHSD) transport approach. Calculations with and without charm-hadron rescattering are compared with ALICE measurements and predictions of the Statistical Hadronization Model for charm. The relative integrated $D$-meson multiplicities are essentially insensitive to hadronic interactions. PHSD reproduces the weak system dependence of the nonstrange $D$-meson ratios and the increase of $D_s^+/D^0$ from pp to Pb+Pb collisions, consistent with the statistical-model trend from strangeness undersaturation towards equilibrium. In contrast, hadronic rescattering redistributes the transverse momentum spectra at low and intermediate momenta and enhances the elliptic flow. These results demonstrate the importance of accounting for the hadronic evolution when extracting QGP properties from differential open-charm observables.

</details>


### [138] [HEART: a resonance-cascade model of soft hadron production in p+p collisions at 158 GeV/c](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11570)
*Ondrej Chvala*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了HEART模型，用于解释NA49实验中p+p碰撞产生的强子谱，通过将末态视为靶与弹核激发系统的衰变叠加，并在事件层次上实现该图像；模型基于共振态与连续谱的级联两体衰变，精确满足守恒律，并成功拟合了多种粒子微分截面数据，显著优于EPOS-LHC、UrQMD和PYTHIA8等主流模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释NA49实验中p+p碰撞在158 GeV/c下识别强子谱的结构，特别是其靶/弹核成分分离现象，并提供一个物理直观、事件级可实现的模型。

Method: 构建HEART模型：将单/双衍射与非衍射过程产生的色单态激发系统分为共振态（<1.7–2.2 GeV）与共振连续谱（>该阈值），通过严格满足守恒律的级联两体衰变进行退激；引入一π交换机制产生前向中子；用14个参数拟合NA49的π±, K±, p, p̄双微分截面及中子数据共1262点。

Result: 在考虑系统误差并设5%模型底限下，χ²=2263/1248 dof；相比EPOS-LHC、UrQMD、PYTHIA8（已调优版本），其χ²低4.2–14.4倍；成功再现K⁺前向缺失、K*(892)⁰前向超量、Λ的平直xF分布、Ξ⁻达NA61/SHINE产额的61%等关键现象；指出重激发系统荷电多重数随质量线性增长，快于e⁺e⁻湮灭。

Conclusion: HEART模型无需中心源即可统一描述前向强子产生，凸显碎片区可观测量对区分级联、弦与火球模型的关键作用，支持固定靶宽角谱仪在该区域的高精度测量价值。

Abstract: Fischer and co-workers interpret the NA49 identified-hadron spectra in p+p collisions at 158 GeV/c as the sum of a target and a projectile component, each the decay of an excited hadronic system. This paper supplies an event-level realization of that picture, the HEART model (Hadronic Excitations And Resonance Transitions), and tests it on the published NA49 data. Single diffraction, double diffraction, and non-diffractive exchange produce color-singlet excited systems. Each system is a catalogued resonance below a duality boundary of 1.7 to 2.2 GeV or a member of a resonant continuum above it. It de-excites by successive two-body transitions with exact conservation laws, and one-pion exchange produces the leading neutrons. Fourteen parameters are fitted to 1,254 points of the $π^\pm$, $K^\pm$, $p$, and $\bar{p}$ double-differential cross sections and eight neutron points. Under an error model that profiles the correlated systematic uncertainties, with a declared 5% model floor, the fit yields $χ^2 = 2263$ on 1248 degrees of freedom. On the same points, EPOS-LHC, UrQMD, and PYTHIA 8 at their published tunes give $χ^2$ values 4.2 to 14.4 times higher. The model needs no central source. The forward $K^+$ deficit, the forward $K^*(892)^0$ excess, and the flat $Λ$ $x_F$ distribution point to the momentum sharing at the vertex where the leading baryon turns into a hyperon. The $Ξ^-$ reaches 0.61 of the NA61/SHINE yield. The charged multiplicity of a heavy excited system grows linearly with its mass, faster than in $e^+e^-$ annihilation. The observables that distinguish a cascade from string and fireball models lie in the fragmentation region, where wide-acceptance fixed-target spectrometers can measure them.

</details>


### [139] [Threshold resummation for top-quark pair production in association with a jet at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11600)
*Bakar Chargeishvili*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了LHC上顶夸克对加喷注不变质量分布的阈值重求和，采用软-共线有效理论（SCET）在NNLL精度下进行，并匹配到精确的NLO截面；发现现有方法在高不变质量（1-4 TeV）下因初态辐射缺失及质量比影响而失效，导致重求和修正不可控，因此仍以NLO为中央预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高顶夸克对加喷注过程在高能区（如TeV量级）不变质量分布的理论预测精度，解决现有阈值重求和方法在大质量区域适用性差、修正不可控的问题。

Method: 基于软-共线有效理论（SCET）实现不变质量分布的NNLL精度阈值重求和，将喷注半径对数作为固定阶处理，并与精确NLO截面匹配；修正文献中的一圈软反常维矩阵，验证硬函数，并将主导幂次核与精确实辐射进行对比。

Result: 发现在不变质量约1–4 TeV、喷注能量阈值设为不变质量15%时，重求和核在阈值附近有效，但当辐射能量超过100–200 GeV时失效；失效部分源于核中缺失的初态辐射项（依赖τ=M²/S）及M/mₜ增大带来的偏差；由此导致的NNLO以上重求和修正幅度达1–23%，且不具微扰可控性。

Conclusion: 由于重求和修正幅度大且缺乏微扰收敛性，无法可靠替代NLO结果；因此仍将NLO作为中心预测，并将其不确定性与已知NNLO修正、部分子喷注匹配结果及重求和可能效应进行比较。

Abstract: We study threshold resummation for the invariant-mass distribution of a top-quark pair and a jet at the LHC. We perform the resummation at next-to-next-to-leading logarithmic accuracy in soft-collinear effective theory, with the logarithms of the jet radius treated at fixed order, and match it to the exact next-to-leading-order (NLO) cross section. We correct the one-loop soft anomalous-dimension matrices in the literature and validate the hard functions. We compare the leading-power kernel with exact real emission, and validate this comparison on Drell-Yan production. At invariant masses up to about 1 TeV with a fixed jet threshold the method does not apply. At 1-4 TeV with a jet threshold of 15% of the invariant mass, the kernel describes the exact real-emission density close to threshold but fails once the emitted energy exceeds about 100-200 GeV. We trace part of this failure to initial states absent from the kernel, which depend on $τ=M^2/S$, with $M$ the invariant mass and $\sqrt{S}$ the hadronic centre-of-mass energy. In addition, at fixed $τ$ the difference between the kernel and the exact result grows with the ratio $M/m_t$ of the invariant mass to the top-quark mass. As a consequence, the resummed correction beyond NLO, for a scale equal to the invariant mass, ranges from 1-2% to 17-23% for different treatments of this region. The upper end becomes 13-16% for a jet threshold of 20% of the invariant mass and 23-33% for 10%. The range is not a bound, and the correction is not under perturbative control. We therefore keep NLO as the central prediction. We compare the possible effect of resummation with the NLO scale uncertainty, with the known next-to-next-to-leading-order corrections, and with NLO matched to a parton shower.

</details>


### [140] [A Poisson-gas approach to medium-induced gluon radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11606)
*Manoel Rodríguez Calvo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Laplace超叠加高斯散射中心的解析方法，用于精确计算Yukawa势下BDMPS-Z辐射谱，适用于静态和膨胀介质，并支持横向动量截断，具有高效、可并行的蒙特卡洛实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 喷注淬火现象学进入精度时代，需要对介质诱导胶子辐射进行定量控制。

Method: 将Yukawa偶极截面表示为高斯函数的Laplace超叠加，将全重求和辐射谱表达为泊松分布散射中心构型的平均值，每个构型可解析求解。

Result: 得到了适用于任意介质剖面（静态或膨胀）的k⊥微分及带k⊥截断的辐射谱主公式；蒙特卡洛评估高效且易并行；在有k⊥截断时发现与先前结果存在显著相对偏差。

Conclusion: 该方法可推广至更复杂的介质内部分子级联基本单元（如有限能量分数发射、色天线辐射等），并为解析近似提供自然起点；代码已开源。

Abstract: The precision era of jet-quenching phenomenology requires a quantitative control of medium-induced gluon radiation. We consider the BDMPS-Z spectrum in the soft-gluon limit for a Yukawa (Gyulassy-Wang) potential. Writing the Yukawa dipole cross section as a Laplace superposition of Gaussians, we express the fully resummed spectrum as an average over Poisson-distributed configurations of Gaussian scattering centers, each of which is solved in closed form. This yields a master formula for the spectrum both differential in the transverse momentum $k_\perp$ and integrated with a cut on $k_\perp$, valid for static and expanding media with arbitrary profiles. Its evaluation by Monte Carlo sampling of the configurations is efficient and trivially parallelizable. The method reproduces the first order in opacity and published results without kinematic cuts, while with a cut we find sizeable relative deviations from previous results. We argue that the same construction extends to the more complex building blocks of an in-medium parton cascade, such as finite energy fraction and emissions off color antennas, and provides a natural starting point for analytical approximations. The code is publicly available.

</details>


### [141] [The $φ$-$η$ Puzzle and the Five-Layer Effective-Temperature Hierarchy in $pp$ Collisions at 2.76 and 7 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11612)
*M. Waqas,Wolfgang Bietenholz,Zhen-Yan Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过分析质子-质子碰撞中不同粒子的横动量谱，发现了一个稳健的五层有效温度层级结构，揭示了强子退耦顺序不仅依赖质量，更受奇异数、截面和OZI规则影响；φ(1020)和η在7 TeV下同处最高温层构成新谜题，为小系统中QGP类物质形成及强子化模型提供了新约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高能碰撞末态强相互作用物质的动力学演化，特别是基于新提出的四重冻结场景，理解粒子逐次动能冻结背后的物理机制。

Method: 采用修正的Tsallis-Pareto函数拟合√s = 2.76和7 TeV pp碰撞中多种识别粒子的横动量谱，提取各粒子种类的有效温度T_eff和非广延性参数q。

Result: 发现稳健的五层有效温度层级：非奇异强子、单奇异强子、多重奇异强子、轻核、φ(1020)介子；7 TeV时η介子与φ(1020)同属最高温层；各邻层温差均超3σ，顶层差超7σ；温度整体随能量升高而上移；该分层与粒子内部结构（如OZI抑制）和相互作用密切相关，而非仅由质量决定。

Conclusion: 强子的顺序动能冻结高度敏感于其内部结构（如奇异数、OZI规则）和介质相互作用截面，挑战了仅以质量排序的传统图像；五层结构为强子化理论和小系统中QGP类行为提供了关键新约束；η与φ在7 TeV的温度一致性暗示可能存在共同早期产生机制或未知的介质效应。

Abstract: Sequential kinetic freeze-out in high-energy collisions probes the dynamics governing the final stages of strongly interacting matter. Inspired by the recently established quadruple freeze-out scenario, we present a thermodynamic analysis of transverse momentum spectra for a set of identified particles produced in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 2.76$ and $7$ TeV. Using a modified Tsallis-Pareto function, we extract the effective temperature and the non-extensivity parameter for each species. We observe a robust five-layer hierarchy in the effective temperature. At both energies, the layers are: non-strange hadrons, singly-strange hadrons, multi-strange hadrons, light nuclei, and the $φ(1020)$ meson. At $7$~TeV, the $η$ meson joins the $φ(1020)$ meson in the highest-temperature layer. At both energies, each pair of neighboring layers is separated by more than $ 3σ$, and the separation at the top exceeds $7σ$. The grouping persists at both energies, while the temperatures shift upward at 7 TeV. The observed pattern shows that the decoupling sequence is driven by an interplay of strangeness, cross-sections, and the OZI rule rather than by particle mass alone. Notably, the $φ(1020)$ meson has a mass close to the proton, but a significantly higher temperature due to its OZI-suppressed coupling to the hadronic medium. The unexpected coincidence of $η$ and $φ(1020)$ at 7 TeV presents a puzzle that may point to a common early production mechanism or in-medium effects, which are not yet understood. The five-layer hierarchy provides new constraints on theoretical models of hadronization. It suggests that the sequential decoupling of particles from the evolving medium is even more sensitive to their internal structure and interaction than previously assumed, with implications for the QGP-like matter formation in small systems.

</details>


### [142] [Nanohertz gravitational waves and asymmetric dark matter from a supercooled dark sector phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11694)
*Wonsub Cho,Ki-Young Choi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了不对称暗物质与纳米赫兹引力波之间的联系，通过一个经典共形暗区中的超冷一级相变实现；暗区包含实标量、复标量和狄拉克费米子，其质量源于辐射对称性破缺；利用Affleck-Dine机制产生费米子不对称性并加热暗浴，守恒的暗电荷固定费米子-反费米子不对称性，可见区延迟再加热过程中的熵产稀释了暗物质遗迹并决定引力波谱红移；采用三维有效理论结合有限密度修正、次领头阶贡献及涨落行列式（NLOdet）计算热成核率，并耦合演化暗区与可见区热历史；基准模型在MeV温度完成相变，产生纳米赫兹引力波信号，并与脉冲星计时阵列自由谱后验分布对比，同时检验其与大爆炸核合成的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不对称暗物质起源与可观测纳米赫兹引力波信号之间的内在联系，尤其聚焦于超冷一级相变在经典共形暗区中的实现机制及其宇宙学可观测后果。

Method: 构建含实标量、复标量和狄拉克费米子的经典共形暗区模型，引入Affleck-Dine机制生成费米子不对称性；采用三维有效理论，计入有限密度修正、次领头阶贡献和涨落行列式（NLOdet）精确计算热成核率；自洽耦合求解暗区与可见区热演化方程；分析相变动力学、引力波谱特征、暗物质丰度及大爆炸核合成约束。

Result: 基准参数下，相变在MeV量级温度完成，产生可被当前脉冲星计时阵列探测的纳米赫兹引力波信号；引力波谱呈碰撞主导型，与自由谱后验分布具一定兼容性；模型能同时满足暗物质观测丰度与大爆炸核合成限制。

Conclusion: 超冷一级相变驱动的经典共形暗区可自然实现不对称暗物质生成，并伴随特征纳米赫兹引力波信号，为联合探针暗物质本质与早期宇宙相变物理提供了新途径。

Abstract: We study the connection between asymmetric dark matter and nanohertz gravitational waves from a supercooled first-order phase transition in a classically conformal dark sector. The dark sector contains a real scalar, a complex scalar and a Dirac fermion, whose mass arises from radiative symmetry breaking. An additional Affleck--Dine condensate generates a fermion asymmetry and heats the dark bath before nucleation. The conserved dark charge fixes the asymmetry between fermions and antifermions, while entropy production during delayed visible-sector reheating dilutes the relic and determines the redshift of the gravitational-wave spectrum. We calculate the thermal nucleation rate in a three-dimensional effective theory with finite-density corrections, next-to-leading-order contributions and fluctuation determinants (NLOdet). We follow the coupled thermal evolution of the dark and visible sectors. For a benchmark chosen to match the observed dark-matter abundance, the transition completes at MeV-scale temperatures and produces a nanohertz signal under the adopted dynamical and thermal assumptions. We compare the predicted collision-dominated gravitational-wave spectra with free-spectrum posteriors from pulsar timing arrays. For a benchmark point and variations of the dark-sector parameters, the gravitational-wave spectra and dark matter relic abundances are compared, and compatibility with Big Bang nucleosynthesis is assessed.

</details>


### [143] [Origin of Tensor Polarized Structure of the Deuteron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11749)
*Satvir Kaur,Chandan Mondal,Xingbo Zhao,Chueng-Ryong Ji*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用基于QCD的光前框架，引入隐藏色成分和相对论自旋-动量关联，发现氘核的张量极化结构函数b1主要源于隐藏色动力学，而非传统认为的D波成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决氘核张量极化结构函数b1的微观起源问题，尤其是传统核模型无法重现HERMES实验观测结果的困境。

Method: 采用基于QCD的光前框架，引入隐藏色组分和相对论自旋-动量关联等超越常规核子图像的自由度。

Result: 发现即使很小的隐藏色混合也能产生显著的张量响应b1(x)，而b1对D波成分几乎不敏感；实测D波概率0.85%仅使b1改变1.7%，主导贡献来自有色簇S波部分。

Conclusion: 氘核的张量极化结构主要由隐藏色动力学驱动，而非D波成分。

Abstract: The microscopic origin of the deuteron tensor polarized structure function $b_1$ remains unresolved. Conventional nuclear models attribute a nonzero $b_1$ mainly to the $D$-wave component of the deuteron wave function, but generally fail to reproduce the magnitude and momentum-dependence observed by the HERMES experiment. Using a QCD-based light-front framework with degrees of freedom beyond the conventional nucleonic picture, in particular including hidden-color components and relativistic spin--momentum correlations, we find that a small but nonzero estimated hidden-color admixture can be sufficient to generate a substantial tensor response ($b_1(x)$), while it is essentially insensitive to the $D$-wave component. The physical $D$-wave probability of $0.85\%$, fixed by the empirical quadrupole moment, changes $b_1$ by only $1.7\%$, with the dominant contribution arising from the colored-cluster $S$-wave sector. These results demonstrate that the tensor-polarized structure of the deuteron is driven primarily by hidden-color dynamics rather than by the $D$-wave component.

</details>


### [144] [Probing the inhomogeneous phases in magnetized quark matter via the bosonic two-point function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11795)
*Kang Deng,Bo Feng,Tianle Wei,Shuai Yuan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了强磁场下夸克物质中手性凝聚体的稳定性，发现朗道量子化导致一维费米面嵌套引发严重Peierls不稳定性，并识别出预示非均匀手性对称性破缺的‘moat’色散区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强磁场环境下致密夸克物质的相结构，特别是均匀手性凝聚体在磁场中的稳定性问题。

Method: 在双味Nambu-Jona-Lasinio模型框架下，将有效作用量展开至二次项，解析推导恒定磁场中动量依赖的玻色型两点函数，并结合数值计算分析不同磁场与温度下的动量空间拓扑结构。

Result: 零温下各占据朗道能级在对应费米动量处引发尖锐对数发散（Peierls失稳）；强磁场形成负‘峡谷’，热涨落仅使其变浅但不立即消除；高化学势下出现具有平缓曲率的正‘盆地’，呈现非单调moat色散。

Conclusion: 磁场诱导的手性不稳定性具有维度还原特征，moat色散是向非均匀手性破缺演化的关键前兆，为磁化致密夸克物质的相图提供了新见解。

Abstract: We investigate the stability of homogeneous chiral condensates in magnetized quark matter within the two-flavor Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model. By expanding the effective action to quadratic order, we derive the full momentum-dependent bosonic two-point function in a constant magnetic field. At zero temperature, our analytical results show that each occupied Landau level produces a sharp logarithmic divergence at its corresponding Fermi momentum. This severe Peierls instability is driven by one-dimensional Fermi surface nesting within the effectively 1+1-dimensional subsystems created by Landau quantization. Numerically, we map out the momentum-space topography of the two-point function across varying magnetic fields and temperatures. We find that strong magnetic fields induce deep negative canyons. While thermal fluctuations smooth these canyons into shallower valleys as the temperature increases, the negative regions remain robust at intermediate temperatures and only vanish entirely at sufficiently high temperatures. Intriguingly, at larger chemical potentials where the system is strictly stable, a broad positive basin with a flat curvature at the origin persists at non-zero momentum. This non-monotonic dispersion is the hallmark of a moat regime, acting as a precursor state that strongly signals a residual tendency toward inhomogeneous chiral symmetry breaking. Our findings provide valuable insights into the phase structure of dense, magnetized quark matter.

</details>


### [145] [Interplay of Scalar and Pseudo-Scalar Mesonic Channels in $Λ\barΛ$ Spin Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11882)
*Kun Xu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究重离子碰撞中ΛΛ̄超子对的自旋关联，通过标量和赝标量介子通道介导s̄s对产生，发现赝标量通道主导导致负的净自旋关联，并揭示其与开角、碰撞能量及手征相变边界的敏感依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解重离子碰撞中ΛΛ̄超子对的自旋关联有助于探测量子色动力学（QCD）相结构，特别是手征相变的位置和性质。

Method: 基于三味Nambu-Jona-Lasinio模型，在随机相位近似（RPA）框架下，分析标量（0⁺）和赝标量（0⁻）介子通道对s̄s对产生的贡献，并计算其谱权重及自旋关联张量。

Result: 赝标量通道占主导，导致整体自旋关联分数为负；自旋关联随开角θ增强而减弱（θ=0最强，θ=π最弱）；动量积分后的自旋关联随碰撞能量变化呈现非单调行为，尤其当演化轨迹靠近手征交叉区域时。

Conclusion: ΛΛ̄自旋关联可作为探测QCD手征相边界位置的灵敏探针，其角度与能量依赖性为实验验证相变结构提供了新途径。

Abstract: The spin correlation of $Λ\barΛ$ hyperon pairs produced in heavy-ion collisions is investigated through scalar and pseudo-scalar mesonic channels that mediate the creation of $s\bar{s}$ pairs. Because a $J=0$ meson forces $L=S$ while parity requires $(-1)^{L+1}=+/-1$, the two vertices project the pair onto distinct total-spin states: a scalar ($0^+$) vertex selects the $L=S=1$ configuration with a spin weight of $+1$, whereas a pseudo-scalar ($0^-$) vertex selects the $L=S=0$ configuration with a weight of $-3$; these weights are the traces of the correlation tensor, corresponding to $P_{s\bar{s}}=+1/3$ and $-1$, respectively. Implementing this framework within the three-flavor Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model at the Random-Phase-Approximation level, we compute the scalar and pseudo-scalar spectral weights. Our results show that the pseudo-scalar channel dominates, resulting in a negative net correlation fraction throughout. And the spin correlation also depends on the opening angle: strongest spin correlation at around $θ=0$ while weakest at $θ=π$. Furthermore, we show that the collision-energy dependence of the momentum-integrated spin correlation along the freeze-out line is sensitive to the trajectory's path relative to the chiral phase boundary. Trajectories passing near the crossover region exhibit a non-monotonic structure, providing a sensitive probe for the location of the chiral phase transition.

</details>


### [146] [Axion-Photon Conversion in Variable-Domain Magnetic Fields and the Highest-Energy Photons from GRB 221009A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11983)
*Rick Sundar Garai,Tanmoy Kumar,Sourov Roy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究光子-轴子-like粒子（ALP）转换机制，以同时解释LHAASO观测到的GRB 221009A超高能光子和Carpet-3探测到的约300 TeV光子；通过建模宿主星系、星系际介质和银河系中的磁场，发现宽域长分布可提高高能光子存活概率，并仅靠光子-ALP转换即可兼容Carpet-3数据；最佳拟合参数为ma=3.85×10⁻⁷ eV、gaγ=3.07×10⁻¹¹ GeV⁻¹，低于CAST实验限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LHAASO与Carpet-3在极高能段观测到的异常光子事件，尤其是单个~300 TeV光子难以用标准天体物理模型解释，需新物理机制如光子-ALP振荡。

Method: 构建包含随机取向、变长度磁域的宿主星系磁场模型，结合IGM与银河系磁场，数值求解光子-ALP耦合薛定谔方程，联合拟合LHAASO与Carpet-3能谱。

Result: 宽磁域长度分布显著提升>100 TeV光子存活率；最佳拟合参数ma=3.85×10⁻⁷ eV、gaγ=3.07×10⁻¹¹ GeV⁻¹，且1σ/2σ置信区间部分与CAST实验上限兼容。

Conclusion: 光子-ALP转换可在不引入额外天体物理假设下统一解释两个实验的超高能光子信号，且参数空间尚未被现有实验完全排除，支持ALP作为暗物质候选者或新物理探针的可行性。

Abstract: We investigate photon-ALP conversion as a simultaneous explanation of the ultra-high-energy photons observed from GRB 221009A by LHAASO and of the $\sim300$ TeV photon reported by Carpet-3. We model the magnetic field of the host galaxy as a set of domains with randomly oriented field directions and variable domain lengths, and propagate the photon-ALP system through the host galaxy, the intergalactic medium and the Milky Way. Fitting the combined LHAASO and Carpet-3 spectra, we find that a broad distribution of domain lengths raises the photon survival probability at the highest energies and accommodates the Carpet-3 point with photon-ALP conversion alone. The best fit, $m_a=3.85\times10^{-7}$~eV and $g_{aγ}=3.07\times10^{-11}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$, lies below the CAST bound, and parts of the $1σ$ and $2σ$ regions are also compatible with it.

</details>


### [147] [Required spectral contributions in glueball QCD sum rules beyond single-pole fits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12073)
*Yong-Qi Kang,Zi-Yan Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种连续验证的诊断方法，用于区分单极点拟合与QCD求和规则矩所要求的质量区域；发现即使候选窄质量区间为空，矩仍可能要求宽质量带的贡献，表明单极拟合成功并不意味着其对应质量区必要，而排除所有单极也不等于消除宽区贡献的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决单极点拟合在胶球物理中可能掩盖真实谱结构的问题，厘清‘拟合成功’与‘物理必要性’之间的概念混淆。

Method: 基于QCD求和规则矩，对14种双/三胶子流进行固定尺度、无连续形变、1%匹配容差下的系统分析，引入‘可空候选带’与‘必需宽带’的诊断分类。

Result: 12个非空输入中，9个仍要求30%宽带贡献；其中4个在满足固定尺度条件的子窗口中仍保持正下界；对最接近X(2370)的GG,0^{-+}流，含X(2370)的20%带可为空，但30%带需承担约44.2%的低能Borel积分。

Conclusion: 单极拟合不能替代对宽质量谱贡献的检验；X(2370)的物理解释需结合更深入的理论分析与实验验证。

Abstract: We provide a continuously verified diagnostic that distinguishes a compatible single-pole fit from a mass region required by QCD sum-rule moments. For specified glueball sum-rule inputs, the moments can require contributions in broad mass bands even when narrower candidate bands are empty. We compare fourteen two- and three-gluon currents at central fixed-scale inputs, undeformed continua and 1\% matching tolerance. All twelve nonempty inputs permit an empty candidate band, yet nine still require a broader contribution. This separates two statements that a pole fit conflates: a successful pole fit does not make its mass region necessary, and excluding every single pole does not remove the information that a broader region must contribute. The five diagnostic classes of the survey organize these outcomes by whether a pole is compatible and whether the 30\% band is required or can be empty; two inputs admit no nonnegative spectrum. Four of the nine positive bounds persist on subwindows satisfying the fixed-scale input criteria. For the $GG,0^{-+}$ current, closest to the nominal $X(2370)$ mass, the 20\% band containing $X(2370)$ can be empty while the 30\% band requires about 44.2\% of each spectrum's own low-energy Borel integral. Determining the physical nature of $X(2370)$ requires further theoretical and experimental progress.

</details>


### [148] [Theoretical constraints on beyond Standard Model extensions of the Higgs potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12105)
*Javier E. Pena-Vico,Carlos Quezada-Calonge,Juan J. Sanz-Cillero*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了扩展希格斯扇区中势能的理论约束条件，重点研究实单态扩展（RSE）和两希格斯二重态模型（2HDM），并开发了可公开使用的代码来检验复杂势能的正定性，最终推导出新重标量粒子质量的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保量子场论中希格斯势能在所有场方向上有下界（即V(Φ)不趋于负无穷），是理论自洽性的基本要求；现有对扩展希格斯扇区稳定性的约束条件缺乏系统比较与统一处理工具。

Method: 推导并比较不同扩展希格斯模型（RSE、Z₂守恒/破缺的2HDM）中势能有下界的充分必要条件；开发开源代码用于自动检验一般复杂势能的正定性；将稳定性条件映射到可观测物理参数空间，结合实验约束推导新标量质量界限。

Result: 给出了RSE和各类2HDM中势能有界性的明确解析/数值判据；提供了首个可处理一般Z₂破缺2HDM正定性检验的公开工具；得到了新重标量质量的严格上下界。

Conclusion: 扩展希格斯扇区的稳定性约束具有显著模型依赖性，Z₂破缺情形更宽松但计算更复杂；所提方法与工具为未来BSM模型构建和LHC等实验数据分析提供了关键理论支持。

Abstract: A fundamental requirement for the consistency of quantum field theories is that their Higgs potential is lower-bounded, such that no direction in the $Φ$ field space leads to $V(Φ)\to \, -\infty$. This work presents a comprehensive analysis of the various theoretical constraints on potentials in extended Higgs sectors, highlighting their relative impact and restrictiveness. We illustrate our study on the potential parameters with two particular Higgs sector extensions: the Real Singlet Extension (RSE) and the Two-Higgs-Doublet Model (2HDM), separating $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$-conserving and $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$-violating cases. In order to do this, we derive and compare these conditions to other studies found in the literature. Additionally, we developed a publicly available code capable of testing positivity conditions in complex potentials, including particularly challenging cases such as the general $\mathbb{Z}_2$-violating 2HDM. Finally, we apply those restrictions to the physical parameters and deduce an upper and a lower bound on the masses of new heavy scalars.

</details>


### [149] [FORM 5.0 and further developments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12157)
*Ana Costa Pereira,Joshua Davies*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了计算机代数系统FORM 5.0版本的新特性，并重点讨论了自该版本发布以来，特别是在性能方面的最新进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足高能物理理论计算中大规模符号运算的需求，需持续改进核心软件工具如FORM的功能与性能。

Method: 通过回顾FORM 5.0版本的主要功能，并分析其后续开发中的性能优化策略与实现技术。

Result: 总结了FORM 5.0的关键新特性，并展示了近期在计算性能上的显著提升。

Conclusion: FORM 5.0及其后续优化进一步巩固了其作为高能物理符号计算核心工具的地位，提升了大规模理论计算的效率与可行性。

Abstract: In these proceedings we review the features of the recently-released version 5.0 of the FORM computer algebra system, which is one of the core software packages for the large-scale symbolic manipulations required by calculations in theoretical high-energy physics. We also discuss developments since the release of version 5.0, focussing in particular on recent performance improvements.

</details>


### [150] [Constraints on the Real Singlet Scalar Dark Matter Model from Higgs Self-Couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12234)
*Hao-Yu Luo,Kun-Peng Chen,Han Hu,Yun Wei,Ming Zhong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文以实单态标量暗物质模型为例，研究了希格斯三重耦合测量和微扰幺正性对模型参数空间的限制。结果表明，当前κ₃测量精度远不如幺正性限制和不可见衰变分支比上限（BR_inv ≤ 0.107）严格；κ₃和κ₄均呈现随暗物质质量m_S增大而趋于零的解耦行为；现有及未来对撞机实验在κ₃测量上难以超越现有暗物质观测（遗存密度、不可见衰变、直接/间接探测）的约束力，除非κ₃测量精度达0.1%量级或更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究希格斯三重耦合（κ₃）测量在暗物质模型参数限制中的实际作用，并评估其相对于其他理论与实验约束（如幺正性、不可见衰变、暗物质探测）的有效性与潜力。

Method: 基于实单态标量暗物质模型，结合Higgs三重耦合修正κ₃、四重耦合修正κ₄的解析表达式，分析其随暗物质质量m_S的变化行为；对比当前及未来κ₃实验精度、微扰幺正性边界、以及BR_inv ≤ 0.107等约束对参数空间的限制强度。

Result: κ₃当前最高精度测量约束弱于幺正性约束，而后者又弱于BR_inv上限；κ₃和κ₄均在m_S > 60 TeV时趋近于零（解耦）；现有及规划中对撞机实验无法超越现有暗物质多信使观测的综合约束，除非κ₃测量精度达O(0.1%)量级。

Conclusion: Higgs三重耦合测量作为暗物质新物理探针的潜力目前有限，其约束力显著落后于传统暗物质实验与理论要求；仅当未来实验精度突破0.1%阈值时，才可能对大质量WIMP参数空间施加非平凡约束。

Abstract: Taking the real singlet scalar dark matter model as a representative test case, we examine the constraints imposed on the model's parameter space by Higgs trilinear coupling measurements and perturbative unitarity requirements. For the low dark matter mass regime, the constraining strength delivered by the current highest-precision $κ_3$ measurement is notably inferior to the exclusion limit derived from perturbative unitarity requirements, while the perturbative unitarity bound itself remains considerably less restrictive than the constraints extracted from the extant experimental upper limit on the Higgs invisible decay branching ratio $\mathrm{BR}_{\mathrm{inv}} \leq 0.107$. Both the coupling modifiers $κ_3$ and $κ_4$ exhibit a well-defined decoupling behavior, with their respective magnitudes monotonically decreasing as the dark matter mass $m_S$ increases. As a direct consequence, the corrections to both couplings asymptotically approach zero for dark matter masses in excess of 60 TeV, when the parameter $a_2$ remains within its perturbative validity domain. Neither existing collider measurements of the Higgs trilinear coupling nor measurements targeted for planned future collider facilities possess the requisite sensitivity to impose more stringent constraints on the model's viable parameter space than the well-established bounds derived from experimental probes of dark matter relic density, invisible Higgs decay signatures, dark matter direct detection, and dark matter indirect detection. Nevertheless, under the scenario that the experimental measurement uncertainty of $κ_3$ reaches the $\mathcal{O}(0.1\%)$ scale or lower, non-negligible constraints acting on a subset of the large mass parameter space associated with the WIMP dark matter scenario can be expected.

</details>


### [151] [NLO QCD corrections to $W^{+}$-boson decays into doubly heavy baryons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12253)
*Hong-Hao Ma,Xu-Chang Zheng,Juan-Juan Niu,Xing-Gang Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在非相对论QCD框架下，研究了W+玻色子衰变产生双重重子Ξ_cc和Ξ_bc的NLO QCD修正，发现NLO修正显著增强衰变宽度（K因子达2.27和2.95），对低不变质量区有增强效应，并给出NLO精度下的分支比基准值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提高双重重子产生过程理论预言的精度，需考虑NLO QCD修正以减小尺度依赖及夸克质量不确定性影响。

Method: 在非相对论QCD（NRQCD）框架下，计算W+→Ξ_cc+ c̄ + s̄ + X 和 W+→Ξ_bc+ b̄ + s̄ + X 过程的NLO QCD修正，分析色单态（3̄）与色六重态（6）S波态的衰变宽度、运动学分布及参数不确定性。

Result: NLO修正使Ξ_cc和Ξ_bc衰变宽度分别提升127%和195%（K因子为2.27和2.95）；各通道K因子介于1.94–5.53；NLO增强低不变质量区，压低能谱高能端；分支比分别为8.86×10⁻⁵和3.63×10⁻⁶。

Conclusion: NLO QCD修正是定量预言双重重子产生过程不可或缺的部分，本文结果为LHC等实验搜寻Ξ_cc和Ξ_bc提供了可靠的NLO理论基准。

Abstract: The next-to-leading-order (NLO) QCD corrections have been investigated for the production of doubly heavy baryons via the inclusive $W^{+}$-boson decays $W^{+}\toΞ_{cc}+\bar{c}+\bar{s}+X$ and $W^{+}\toΞ_{bc}+\bar{b}+\bar{s}+X$ within the nonrelativistic QCD framework. The decay widths, the kinematic distributions, and the uncertainties induced by the heavy-quark masses and the renormalization scale have been analyzed for both the color-antitriplet ($\bar{\mathbf{3}}$) and the color-sextet ($\mathbf{6}$) $S$-wave states. With the central input parameters $m_{c}=1.8~\mathrm{GeV}$ and $m_{b}=5.1~\mathrm{GeV}$, the $K$ factors are $2.27$ for $Ξ_{cc}$ and $2.95$ for $Ξ_{bc}$, i.e., the NLO corrections increase the corresponding LO widths by about $127\%$ and $195\%$, which shows that the NLO corrections are indispensable for quantitative predictions. The individual-channel $K$ factors range from $1.94$ to $5.53$, with the largest one occurring for $Ξ_{bc}$ in the $[{}^{3}S_{1}]_{\mathbf{6}}$ channel. The distributions show that the NLO corrections enhance the low invariant-mass region and shift the baryon energy spectrum toward smaller energy fractions. With $Γ_{W}=2.085~\mathrm{GeV}$, the inclusive branching ratios are $8.86\times10^{-5}$ and $3.63\times10^{-6}$ for $Ξ_{cc}$ and $Ξ_{bc}$, respectively, which provide an NLO benchmark for future experimental searches.

</details>


### [152] [From oxygen to lead: testing a common QGP description with high-$p_\perp$ probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12404)
*Bithika Karmakar,Dusan Zigic,Magdalena Djordjevic*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过事件到事件的流体动力学演化结合DREENA能量损失模型，统一解释了从氧到铅不同碰撞系统中高横动量粒子的抑制（RAA）和椭圆各向异性（v2）现象，并预测了多系统间的一致性行为及实验检验方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O+O和Ne+Ne碰撞中观察到的高pT粒子抑制与有限椭圆各向异性，引发了一个关键问题：是否存在一个统一的夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）描述，能同时解释从小系统（氧、氖）到大系统（氙、铅）的这些现象？

Method: 采用由软探针观测量约束的事件到事件流体动力学演化模型，耦合DREENA辐射与碰撞能量损失模型，并在O+O、Ne+Ne、Xe+Xe和Pb+Pb系统中使用完全相同的微观参数集进行预测。

Result: 计算成功再现了不同系统中RAA的观测排序，并预测10–20%中心度下四系统的v2^SP大小相近；预测轻离子碰撞中v2^SP/(1−RAA)在高pT近似与动量无关，v2{4}/v2{2}弱依赖动量且趋势与软区一致；还给出了D/B介子的预测以拓展味依赖性检验。

Conclusion: 系统尺寸、谐波阶数、涨落模式及夸克味依赖性的综合预测，为检验QGP描述的普适性边界提供了多维度实验判据，表明统一QGP图像在轻离子碰撞中仍具相当适用性。

Abstract: The observation of suppression and finite high-$p_\perp$ elliptic anisotropy in O+O and Ne+Ne raises the question of whether a common quark--gluon plasma (QGP) description can simultaneously account for both observables across systems from oxygen to lead. We present predictions using event-by-event bulk evolution constrained by soft-sector observables and coupled to DREENA radiative and collisional energy loss, with the same microscopic parameter set for O+O, Ne+Ne, Xe+Xe, and Pb+Pb. The calculation reproduces the observed ordering of suppression and predicts elliptic anisotropies of comparable magnitude across the four systems in 10--20$\%$ collisions. We predict a modest centrality dependence of $v_2^{\mathrm{SP}}$ and a more pronounced decrease of $v_3^{\mathrm{SP}}$ toward peripheral light-ion collisions, in contrast to the corresponding trends in larger systems. In light ions, we predict that $v_2^{\mathrm{SP}}/(1-R_{AA})$ is nearly independent of momentum at high $p_\perp$. We further predict that the differential ratio $v_2\{4\}/v_2\{2\}$ depends only weakly on momentum at high $p_\perp$, with magnitudes and centrality trends broadly comparable to those of the measured soft-sector ratios, suggesting that the soft-sector fluctuation pattern persists in hard--soft correlations. Predictions for D and B mesons extend the comparison to probes of different flavors. Taken together, these system-size, harmonic, fluctuation, and flavor dependences provide experimental tests of how far a common QGP description extends.

</details>


### [153] [A singlet framework for the mu problem and type-II seesaw in a generalized NMSSM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12625)
*Xiaokang Du,Yusi Pan,Fei Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个广义NMSSM模型，其中通过一个规范单态将希格斯双线性项与II型跷跷板尺度联系起来，该单态真空期望值贡献了μ_eff并生成了矢量样三重态对的超对称质量；通过积分掉三重态得到Weinberg算符，其规范荷允许可能的信使解释；在超对称极限下，由于超对称非重整化定理，μ_eff保持在软破缺能标附近仍具自然性；所研究的bino型和singlino型中性微子解可兼容高达10^15 GeV的三重态输入尺度及电弱至TeV能标的暗物质，并满足重希格斯共振、胶子/ wino共湮灭机制下的遗迹丰度与直接探测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决μ问题并实现高能标下（如10^15 GeV）的中微子质量生成（通过II型跷跷板机制），同时保持超对称模型中μ_eff参数的自然性。

Method: 构建含规范单态的广义NMSSM模型，引入矢量样三重态作为II型跷跷板媒介；利用超对称非重整化定理分析μ_eff的自然性；结合暗物质遗迹丰度计算与直接探测约束，分析bino-like和singlino-like中性微子解的可行性。

Result: μ_eff可在超对称极限下自然地维持在软破缺能标附近；三重态输入尺度可达~10^15 GeV；bino/singlino主导的中性微子暗物质可满足Planck观测的遗迹丰度及XENON等直接探测实验约束，尤其在重希格斯共振或与gluino/wino共湮灭区域。

Conclusion: 该广义NMSSM框架成功统一处理μ问题、中微子质量起源与TeV尺度暗物质，兼具理论自然性与可观测性。

Abstract: We study a generalized NMSSM in which a gauge singlet links the Higgs bilinear to the type-II seesaw scale. The singlet vacuum expectation value contributes to $μ_{\rm eff}$ and generates the supersymmetric mass of a vectorlike triplet pair. Integrating out the triplets gives the Weinberg operator, and their gauge charges admit a possible messenger interpretation. Large cancellation between the bare $μ_0$ term and the singlet VEV generated bilinear Higgs term that keeps $μ_{\rm eff}$ near the soft scale is still natural in the supersymmetric limit (by the supersymmetric non-renormalization theorem), although the concrete value of $μ_{\rm eff}$ also depends slightly on the soft SUSY breaking parameters and finite threshold matching. The studied bino-like and singlino-like neutralino solutions accommodate triplet input scales approaching $10^{15}$ GeV together with electroweak-to-TeV dark matter. Representative spectra consistent with heavy-Higgs funnels or coannihilation with gluinos or winos yield allowed relic abundances while satisfying direct-detection constraints.

</details>


### [154] [Angular analysis of the purely baryonic decays $Λ_b^0\to n p\bar p$ and $Λ_b^0\toΛp\bar p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12630)
*Chao-Qiang Geng,Chao Han*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在广义因子化模型下对Λ_b^0→np̄p和Λ_b^0→Λp̄p衰变进行了角分布分析，计算了对映角分布、前后半球不对称性、勒让德矩及Λ极化等可观测量，并探讨了不同流贡献与相空间选择对结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解纯重子衰变Λ_b^0→np̄p和Λ_b^0→Λp̄p的动力学机制，为后续理论研究和实验测量提供基准。

Method: 采用现象学广义因子化模型，相干叠加两种产生相同末态的流贡献，计算各角分布与自旋观测量。

Result: 得到n̄p与p̄p参考系中的前后不对称性（分别为4.46×10⁻⁴和0.856），发现小不对称源于不同质量区符号相反贡献的抵消；Λ通道中因m(p̄p)<2.85 GeV截断导致Λ纵向极化由−0.803变为−0.954。

Conclusion: 角分布与自旋观测量显著依赖于相对流贡献和相空间区域选择，该模型分析是理解此类衰变动力学的第一步，可为未来理论与实验对比奠定基础。

Abstract: An angular analysis of the purely baryonic decays $Λ_b^0\to np\bar p$ and $Λ_b^0\toΛp\bar p$ is presented within a phenomenological generalized-factorization model. Within this model, two current contributions leading to the same final state are combined coherently. Pair-angle distributions, forward-backward hemisphere asymmetries, and Legendre moments are calculated in both channels, together with the polarization of the produced $Λ$ for an unpolarized parent. For the neutron channel, the full-region forward-backward asymmetries are $4.46\times10^{-4}$ in the $n\bar p$ frame and $0.856$ in the $p\bar p$ frame at $N_c^{\mathrm{eff}}=3$ and zero additional relative phase. From mass-bin integration, the small first asymmetry is found to arise from compensation between signed contributions from different regions. In the $Λ$ channel, the angular distributions are modified by the selection $m(p\bar p)<2.85\,\mathrm{GeV}$, and the rate-weighted longitudinal polarization along the $Λ$ flight direction is shifted from $-0.803$ to $-0.954$. These results show how angular and spin observables depend on the relative current contributions and the selected phase-space region. The model-dependent analysis is intended as a first step toward understanding the dynamics of these decays and as a basis for further theoretical studies and comparisons with measurements.

</details>


### [155] [Pentagon Functions for Two-Loop Leading-Colour $pp\to t\bar{t}H$ Amplitudes with a Light-Quark Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12661)
*Simone Devoto,Manfred Kraus,Ben Page*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一组特殊函数的基底及高效的数值实现方法，用于计算pp→t̄tH过程中带轻夸克圈的领色振幅所涉及的平面双圈主积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持LHC上tt̄H产生过程的NNLO理论研究，需高效、高精度地计算相关双圈主积分。

Method: 构建包含嵌套根号的特殊函数基底，解析给出权1和2的结果（对数与二重对数），更高权通过单重积分数值计算；针对无法用单一对数表示原函数的dlog形式，采用自适应切比雪夫逼近进行数值处理；并基于PentagonFunctions++库开发C++实现。

Result: 实现了高精度、快速的数值计算程序，在物相空间点上验证了其准确性和效率，满足唯象学应用需求。

Conclusion: 该工作为tt̄H产生过程的NNLO精确计算提供了关键基础模块。

Abstract: We present a basis of special functions and an efficient numerical implementation for the planar two-loop master integrals that enter the leading-colour amplitudes for $pp\to t\bar{t}H$ with a closed light-quark loop. We provide analytic results for the special functions at transcendental weight one and two in terms of logarithms and dilogarithms, while higher weights are evaluated as one-fold integrals. We focus on the nested roots that arise, studying their analytic structure on the physical region and constructing numerically stable representations of letters and $\mathrm{d}\log$ forms that involve them. We find that a number of $\mathrm{d}\log$ forms have an antiderivative that cannot be represented by a single logarithm over the physical region and tackle them numerically with adaptive Chebyshev approximation. We implement the functions in \texttt{C++} as an extension of the \texttt{PentagonFunctions++} library. We study the performance of our implementation over physical phase-space points and find that the evaluation is accurate and fast enough for phenomenological application. This provides an important building block for NNLO studies of $t\bar{t}H$ production at the LHC.

</details>


### [156] [Amplitudes separation and strong-electromagnetic phases determination in the $\boldsymbol{J/ψ}$ decays into baryon-antibaryon pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12673)
*Chiara Alice,Antonio Amoroso,Matteo Barbagiovanni,Monica Bertani,Diego Bettoni,Fabrizio Bianchi,Alberto Bortone,Damiano Cabiati,Alessandro Calcaterra,Gianluigi Cibinetto,Fabio Cossio,Marco Destefanis,Francesca De Mori,Emma Di Fiore,Luciano Fava,Sara Garbolino,Isabella Garzia,Michela Greco,Marco Maggiora,Simonetta Marcello,Federico Matias Melendi,Giulio Mezzadri,Piero Patteri,Simone Pacetti,Angelo Rivetti,Manuel Dionisio Da Rocha Rolo,Francesco Rosini,Marco Scodeggio,Stefano Sosio,Stefano Spataro,Umberto Zarantonello*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过构建基于味SU(3)有效拉格朗日量的分波参数化方法，提取了矢量粲偶素衰变到自旋1/2重子-反重子对过程中强与电磁振幅间的相对相位，并给出两组解，其中一组与Σ⁺Σ̄⁻标量扫描相位一致，但与其他电-磁产生相位存在张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定强与电磁相互作用振幅之间的相对相位，有助于理解QCD在非微扰区域的行为及粲偶素衰变机制。

Method: 基于味SU(3)对称性的有效拉格朗日量（含矢量和张量流），构造分波系数参数化形式，从中导出电相位、磁相位、积分复重叠相位及有效率角。

Result: 得到两组可能的相位解；两组解均能在误差范围内描述Σ⁺Σ̄⁻标量扫描相位，但与多个已知电-磁产生相位测量结果存在张力。

Conclusion: 该参数化框架能统一描述多种相位观测量，但当前理论与部分实验数据存在不一致性，提示需进一步考虑破缺效应或更高阶贡献。

Abstract: We extract the relative phase between strong and electromagnetic amplitudes of the decays of a vector charmonium into spin-$1/2$ baryon-antibaryon pairs. Starting from a flavour-SU(3) effective Lagrangian with vector and tensor currents, we construct a parametrization in terms of partial waves coefficients. The electric and magnetic phases, the phase of the integrated complex overlap and the effective rate angle are derived from the same source amplitudes. We retain two possible solutions and both of them describe the scalar $Σ^+\barΣ^-$ scan phase within its uncertainty, while presenting tension with several electric-magnetic production phases.

</details>


### [157] [Nucleon transversity from dihadron data and x-dependent lattice QCD constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12745)
*Hongxin Dong,Yuan-Yuan Huang,Tianbo Liu,Xiaomin Shen,Peng Sun,Xiaonu Xiong,Jian-Hui Zhang,Yuxiang Zhao,Yiyu Zhou,Ruilin Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过联合神经网络分析，结合格点QCD计算的等矢量横自旋分布，提取了共线横自旋分布和双强子碎裂函数，并发现包含格点输入会改变下夸克横自旋分布并增强大x区上夸克横自旋，其结果超出Soffer不等式，提示需进一步检验正定性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为更精确地确定横自旋分布，特别是利用格点QCD提供的互补信息来约束夸克与反夸克的味差异，弥补实验数据在动量分数范围上的不足。

Method: 采用联合神经网络分析方法，整合电子-正电子对撞、半包容深度非弹性散射和极化质子-质子碰撞中的双强子产生数据，并引入格点QCD计算的等矢量横自旋作为约束条件。

Result: 加入格点QCD输入后，下价夸克横自旋分布变得更负，上价夸克横自旋在大x区增强；中心下价夸克结果幅度超出Soffer不等式。

Conclusion: 格点QCD输入虽未显著改善整体拟合质量，但显著影响横自旋分布的形状，尤其对下夸克分布和大x区上夸克分布，提示现有理论约束（如Soffer不等式）可能需重新审视。

Abstract: We extract collinear transversity distributions and dihadron fragmentation functions in a joint neural-network analysis of dihadron production in electron-positron annihilation, semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering, and polarized proton-proton collisions. We incorporate lattice QCD calculation of isovector transversity, which constrains the up-down flavor differences for quarks and antiquarks over broad momentum fraction range complementary to data. Matched fits with and without the lattice input describe the experimental data comparably well. Including the lattice input shifts the down-valence distribution toward more negative values and enhances up-valence transversity at large $x$. The magnitude of the central down-valence result exceeds the reference Soffer inequalities, motivating further test of positivity constraints.

</details>


### [158] [A possible signature of neutrino spin precession in the J-PARC muon $g-2$ experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12749)
*S. Carneiro,F. C. Sobrinho*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了J-PARC E34实验中中微子角动量对缪子磁矩反常测量可能产生的影响，通过显式处理衰变运动学并积分中微子对的纵向动量，发现该效应在J-PARC比费米实验室小约0.415±0.176 ppm；提出可通过比较不同缪子动量下的测量来独立检验该效应，最优灵敏度出现在缪子束能量245 MeV时，显著性达4.88σ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子角动量可能带来缪子g-2测量中未被考虑的系统误差，尤其在低能缪子实验（如J-PARC）中，前向发射近似失效，需显式处理衰变动力学以评估该效应。

Method: 基于弱衰变理论谱推导正电子能谱与不对称性，结合事件计数权重N(E)A(E)，显式积分中微子对的纵向动量，并对比Fermilab与J-PARC实验参数下的修正量；进一步分析不同缪子动量配置下的信号差异与统计显著性。

Result: J-PARC中该角动量修正比Fermilab小0.415±0.176 ppm（2.36σ）；固定正电子阈值200 MeV时，缪子能量245 MeV下相对于标称J-PARC配置的差异可达4.88σ（最乐观精度假设）。

Conclusion: 该计算识别出一个可独立于强子贡献检验中微子角动量效应的实验方案，支持通过跨实验或变能量测量验证该新物理敏感项。

Abstract: We examine a possible signature of the neutrino angular-momentum contribution to the muon magnetic anomaly measurement in the J-PARC E34 experiment. In previous papers we argued that the angular momentum carried by the unobserved neutrino pair may leave an uncertainty in the anomaly inferred from the time modulation of detected positrons. At Fermilab the highly relativistic muons make the forward-emission approximation suitable for this estimate. The lower muon momentum at J-PARC requires the decay kinematics to be treated explicitly. We integrate the longitudinal momentum of the neutrino pair using the positron energy distribution and asymmetry derived from the theoretical weak-decay spectrum, and the same event-counting weight $N(E)A(E)$ is employed for both experiments. For positron energies between $1.5$ and $2.9$ GeV at Fermilab and between $200$ and $275$ MeV at J-PARC, the proposed correction is smaller at J-PARC by $0.415\pm0.176$ ppm, when the most optimistic E34 precision is assumed. The expected difference amounts to $2.36$ standard deviations if the future measurement is centred on this value. The calculation thus identifies an experimental comparison which may test the proposed effect independently of the hadronic contribution to the Standard Model prediction. We also examine the possibility of testing the effect by comparing measurements at two different muon momenta. With a fixed positron energy threshold of 200 MeV, the maximum sensitivity is obtained at a muon beam energy of 245 MeV. The predicted difference relative to the nominal J-PARC configuration has significance of $4.88σ$ for the most optimistic experimental sensitivity.

</details>


### [159] [Electroweak Parton Distribution Functions for Future Lepton Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12793)
*Tao Han,Wolfgang Kilian,Yang Ma,Krzysztof Mękała,Jürgen Reuter,Keping Xie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了将电弱部分子分布函数（EW PDFs）形式化框架实现在蒙特卡洛事件生成器Whizard中的工作，用于描述未来高能对撞机中轻子分裂产生的光子和规范玻色子主导的碰撞过程，并探讨其在新物理研究及电弱喷注现象中的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来高能对撞机（如多TeV缪子对撞机或高能正负电子线性对撞机）中，电弱规范玻色子的共线辐射成为主导效应，需一种能系统处理标准模型中各类轻子分裂导致的大对数效应的理论框架。

Method: 将电弱部分子分布函数（EW PDFs）形式化方法嵌入蒙特卡洛事件生成器Whizard，并与固定阶全计算及有效弱玻色子近似（EWBA）进行对比分析。

Result: 成功实现了EW PDF在Whizard中的数值实现，验证了其在多种物理过程中的适用性，并明确了其相较于其他近似方法的优势场景。

Conclusion: EW PDF框架是迈向‘电弱喷注’时代的关键一步，为未来轻子对撞机的新物理探测和精确唯象研究提供了可靠、高效且具物理直观性的工具。

Abstract: At future high-energy colliders, such as a multi-TeV muon collider or the high-energy stages of an $e^+e^-$ linear collider, collinear radiation of electroweak gauge bosons becomes a dominant phenomenon. In this regime, many key processes are primarily initiated by photons and gauge bosons originating from lepton splittings. Electroweak Parton Distribution Functions (EW PDFs) provide an appropriate framework to describe these interactions by resumming large collinear logarithms arising from all possible Standard Model splittings, thereby enabling a partonic interpretation of the hard scattering. The formalism is particularly suitable for studying new physics beyond the Standard Model in the electroweak sector at higher scales. In this paper, we present the implementation of the EW PDF formalism within the Monte Carlo event generator \texttt{Whizard}. We demonstrate several applications of this framework and analyze its interplay with full fixed-order calculations as well as the purely kinematic Effective Weak Boson Approximation. We highlight scenarios where the EW PDF approach is especially useful for phenomenology and physics-potential studies for future lepton colliders. The EW PDF formalism is a vital step towards the ``electroweak jet regime'' where electroweak particles behave similarly to QCD jets.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>
