### [160] [Surface Enhanced Anisotropy and Its Impacts on Massive Neutron Star Configurations in Einstein Gravity : Stability, Structure, and Multimessenger Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09974)
*Ritabrata Biswas,Arpan Bhattacharya,Subhajit Pal*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在广义相对论框架下，采用三阶多项式状态方程和Buchdahl类I度规，构建了各向异性中子星的物理自洽解，并通过多信使观测约束验证其合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为构建符合广义相对论、满足物理合理性（如能量条件、稳定性、因果性）且与当前多信使天文观测（如GW170817、NICER）兼容的各向异性中子星模型。

Method: 采用三阶多项式状态方程 p_r = Aρ + Bρ² + Cρ³ 和 Buchdahl 类-I 度规 e^{q(r)} = L(1+τr²)/(L−τr²)，结合 Darmois-Israel 拼接条件匹配外部 Schwarzschild 时空；分析物质分布、压力各向异性、TOV 平衡、致密性、红移、潮汐形变、因果性及动力学稳定性；并开展贝叶斯 MCMC 分析以约束关键参数。

Result: 获得有限、正定、单调递减的能量密度与压力分布；各向异性中心为零、向外平滑增大；满足广义TOV平衡、Chandrasekhar稳定性判据、因果性及Herrera开裂条件；致密性低于Buchdahl极限；最大质量高于观测值，部分落入质量间隙低区；潮汐形变与GW170817/NICER一致；质量-致密性关系与观测吻合；MCMC给出Love数、致密性和扰动参数的可靠统计约束。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了在广义相对论下描述各向异性中子星的物理自洽、观测兼容的理论框架，为理解致密天体内部结构与强场引力提供了新途径。

Abstract: Anisotropic neutron-star configurations are investigated within Einstein's General Relativity using a third-order polynomial equation of state, (p_r=Aρ+Bρ^2+Cρ^3), and the Buchdahl class-I metric ansatz, (e^{q(r)}=\frac{L(1+τr^2)}{L-τr^2}). Physically viable interior solutions are constructed and smoothly matched to the exterior Schwarzschild spacetime using the Darmois-Israel junction conditions. The stellar properties are examined through the matter distribution, pressure anisotropy, hydrostatic equilibrium, compactness, surface redshift, tidal deformability, causality, and dynamical stability. The energy density and radial and tangential pressures remain finite, positive, and monotonically decreasing throughout the interior, while the anisotropy vanishes at the centre and increases smoothly toward the surface. Generalized Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equation confirms equilibrium through the balance of gravitational, hydrostatic, and anisotropic forces. Furthermore, adiabatic index satisfies the Chandrasekhar stability criterion, and both sound speeds obey the causality and Herrera cracking conditions. The compactness remains below the Buchdahl limit, with physically acceptable surface redshifts. The predicted maximum masses exceed the corresponding observed masses, with some configurations extending into the lower mass-gap region. The tidal deformabilities are consistent with multimessenger constraints from GW170817 and NICER. In addition, the theoretical mass-compactness relation and its prediction intervals show good agreement with observational estimates. Finally, Bayesian Markov Chain Monte Carlo analysis provides statistically reliable constraints on the tidal Love number, compactness, and perturbation parameter. These results establish a physically viable and observationally compatible framework for describing anisotropic neutron stars within General Relativity.

</details>


### [161] [Periodic orbits and gravitational wave radiation of a phantom Dirac--Born--Infeld regular black hole Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09975)
*N. Heidari,A. A. Araújo Filho,Iarley P. Lobo,V. B. Bezerra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由幻影Dirac-Born-Infeld标量场支撑的渐近平直正则黑洞时空中的周期轨道与引力波辐射，分析了类时测地线、有效势、圆轨道及zoom-whirl轨迹，并结合S2星观测约束正则核参数，最后在数值kludge近似下计算了相应引力波信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索幻影Dirac-Born-Infeld正则黑洞的引力特性，特别是其对周期轨道和引力波信号的影响，并利用S2星观测提供对正则核参数的可能限制。

Method: 基于类时测地线方程分析有效势和圆形轨道；构造zoom-whirl周期轨道；研究能量微扰对轨道闭合性的影响；利用S2星近日点进动约束正则核参数；在四极矩数值kludge近似下计算引力波波形（含两种偏振模式）。

Result: 获得了该类正则黑洞中典型周期轨道的构型及其对正则核参数的敏感性；给出了S2星观测对正则核参数的限制；计算并分析了对应引力波信号的波形特征，揭示了正则核对轨道与波形的影响。

Conclusion: 正则核心显著影响轨道结构与引力波波形，S2星观测可为幻影DBI正则黑洞模型提供可观测约束，该工作为检验此类新黑洞模型提供了引力波与恒星动力学双重探针。

Abstract: We investigate periodic orbits and gravitational wave radiation in the spacetime of an asymptotically flat regular black hole supported by a phantom Dirac--Born--Infeld scalar field. Starting from the timelike geodesic equations, we examine the effective potential and the characteristic circular orbits and construct periodic zoom--whirl trajectories. We further explore the sensitivity of orbital closure to small energy perturbations and examine the periastron advance of the $S2$ star around Sgr A$^*$ to assess possible constraints on the regular core parameter. The gravitational wave signals associated with representative periodic trajectories are then calculated within a quadrupole based numerical kludge approximation, considering both polarization modes. Particular attention is given to the influence of the regular core on orbital configurations and waveform characteristics, providing a complementary perspective on gravitational aspects of phantom Dirac--Born--Infeld regular black holes.

</details>


### [162] [Observational signatures of Bardeen black holes under nonlinear electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09979)
*Haiyuan Feng,Dan-Dan Cui*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了非线性电动力学（NLED）框架下Bardeen黑洞的阴影和光学外观，通过构建其有效光学几何，分析光子球、视界与阴影之间的不等式关系，并结合薄盘和球对称吸积模型模拟光学图像，揭示了磁荷参数g/M对阴影大小、环结构及强度分布的影响，表明NLED效应在强场区域可能产生可观测的独特特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非线性电动力学（NLED）对Bardeen黑洞阴影及光学外观的影响，以寻找区别于标准Bardeen黑洞的可观测特征，验证NLED在强引力场中的物理效应。

Method: 基于NLED诱导的有效度规推导Bardeen黑洞的有效光学几何；分析视界-光子球-阴影不等式；数值模拟薄盘与球对称（静态与下落）吸积模型下的光子轨迹与成像，分类直接辐射、透镜环与光子环成分；系统研究磁荷参数g/M对阴影半径、临界冲击参数、强度峰值等的影响。

Result: NLED-Bardeen黑洞不满足完整的视界-光子球-阴影不等式链；g/M增大使阴影更紧凑、发射峰内移；薄盘模型中不同模型呈现1–3个强度峰，峰数与磁荷相关；球对称吸积中静态情形在临界冲击参数处强度增强，而下落吸积因多普勒红移抑制强度；阴影尺寸、环结构和强度分布可作为NLED强场效应的观测标识。

Conclusion: NLED-Bardeen黑洞在阴影形态、光子环结构与辐射强度分布上展现出与普通Bardeen黑洞可区分的特征，这些差异为未来EHT等高分辨率观测验证非线性电磁理论提供了潜在判据。

Abstract: We investigate the shadow and optical appearance of the Bardeen black hole (BH) within nonlinear electrodynamics (NLED). Since photon propagation in nonlinear electromagnetic field can be equivalently described as null-geodesic motion in the effective metric induced by NLED, we derive the corresponding effective optical geometry of the NLED-Bardeen BH. Furthermore, by examining the horizon-photon sphere-shadow inequality, we demonstrate that the NLED-Bardeen BH does not satisfy the complete inequality chain. In addition, we investigate photon dynamics and the corresponding optical images generated by both thin disk and spherical accretion models. Increasing $g/M$ reduces the photon sphere radius and critical impact parameter, resulting in a more compact shadow and an inward shift of the emission peak. For the thin disk accretion model, photon trajectories are classified into direct emission, lensing ring, and photon ring components according to the corresponding impact parameter. We find that Model 1 exhibits three distinct intensity peaks, whereas Models 2 and 3 display two and one peaks. The systematic dependence of these features on the magnetic charge indicates that the NLED-Bardeen BH may exhibit observational signatures distinguishable from those of the Bardeen BH. In static spherical accretion, the intensity peaks near the critical impact parameter because of the enhanced optical path length, while infalling spherical accretion introduces an additional Doppler redshift that suppresses the observed intensity. These results indicate that the shadow size, ring structure, and intensity distribution can serve as potential observational signatures of NLED effects in the strong-field regime.

</details>


### [163] [Analogue Gravity: the Subcritical Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10006)
*F. Belgiorno,S. L. Cacciatori,A. Viganò*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了类比引力中亚临界情况下的对产生现象，特别是在无视界条件下的亚临界白洞情形，并通过Corley模型和过势垒散射方法进行解析分析，发现复数转折点对模式转换至关重要，且该对产生是非普朗克谱、无霍金伴子的；同时确认了亚临界与超临界之间存在一个不发生对产生的临界区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究类比引力中无事件视界时的量子对产生机制，特别是亚临界白洞情形下是否仍存在非霍金型对产生。

Method: 采用Corley色散模型，在弱色散近似下运用受量子力学启发的过势垒散射方法，重点分析复数转折点导致的模式转换效应。

Result: 证实亚临界白洞中仍存在非普朗克谱的对产生，且无霍金伴子；发现并确认了亚临界与超临界之间的零对产生临界区域。

Conclusion: 复数转折点是实现无视界对产生的关键机制；亚临界类比白洞中的对产生本质上不同于霍金辐射，其谱特性与动力学机制需重新理解。

Abstract: We focus on the subcritical case in Analogue Gravity for a subluminal dispersion relation, and investigate the problem of pair creation in the absence of an horizon analytically, for the subcritical white hole case. We limit our attention to weakly dispersive media. In particular, we take into account for simplicity the Corley model and adopt an approach inspired by quantum mechanics in the so-called overbarrier scattering. The presence of complex turning points is shown to play a fundamental role in allowing a mode conversion which would be absent in a naive WKB approach. We show that there is still a pair creation phenomenon, which, in the subcritical case, is non-Planckian and without a Hawking partner. Our fully analytical results are qualitatively in agreement with the literature at hand. Furthermore we confirm the existence of a critical regime betwen the subcritical and the transcritical ones where no pair creation occurs.

</details>


### [164] [Scalable early warning for binary neutron-star mergers in third-generation gravitational-wave detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10133)
*Martin Gerini,Rodrigo Tenorio,Davide Gerosa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于短时傅里叶变换和卷积神经网络的新方法，用于第三代引力波探测器中双中子星并合信号的低延迟搜索，显著降低了计算成本，同时保持了与匹配滤波相当的检测灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三代引力波观测站将观测到持续数小时的双中子星并合信号，地球运动和长信号持续时间使传统搜索方法在低延迟检测中计算开销巨大。

Method: 采用短时傅里叶变换（STFT）提取时频特征，并结合卷积神经网络（CNN）隐式边缘化外参，仅需搜索啁啾质量；网络学习信号非线性时频形态，逼近最优贝叶斯检测统计量。

Result: 在ETΔ和ET2L两种爱因斯坦望远镜配置下，对5 Hz至2 Hz最低频率（对应25分钟至6小时信号长度）的模拟信号测试表明，该网络检测灵敏度与GPU优化的匹配滤波基线相当，但计算成本降低超四个数量级，处理时间从分钟级降至毫秒级。

Conclusion: 所提方法大幅提升了计算效率，为第三代探测器时代多信使天文学的快速预警提供了可行技术路径。

Abstract: Third-generation gravitational-wave observatories will observe binary neutron-star coalescences for hours before merger. Over these extended time windows, Earth's motion and the long signal durations make standard search methods computationally demanding for low-latency detection. We propose an approach based on short-time Fourier transforms and convolutional neural networks that implicitly marginalizes over extrinsic parameters, effectively reducing the search to the chirp mass. The network learns the signals' nonlinear time-frequency morphology, approximating the optimal Bayesian detection statistic. Using a large-scale campaign of simulated signal injections, we compare the network's performance with a GPU-optimized matched-filtering baseline for both the ET$Δ$ and ET2L configurations of the Einstein Telescope and signal durations from approximately 25 minutes ($f_{\rm min} = 5$ Hz) to approximately 6 hours ($f_{\rm min} = 2$ Hz). The network achieves comparable detection sensitivity while reducing computational costs by over four orders of magnitude, bringing processing times from minutes to milliseconds. This efficiency paves the way for rapid early-warning alerts for multi-messenger astronomy in the third-generation detector era.

</details>


### [165] [Stealth scalars on prescribed dark-energy backgrounds: reconstruction, branch regularity, and perturbative bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10231)
*José Blanco,Cuauhtemoc Campuzano,Víctor H. Cárdenas,Giovany Cruz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在给定FLRW宇宙学背景下重建均匀‘隐形’标量场的方法，该标量场自身应力张量为零，不直接贡献宇宙膨胀动力学，而是由独立背景成分驱动；通过消除自相互作用势导出Riccati方程，并据此区分严格隐形性、全局正则性及与暗能量密度的强对应关系；证明该强对应在混合wCDM模型中不具备动力学不变性；对无辐射wCDM背景，利用超几何函数分离正则与奇异解分支；共形耦合下问题简化为一个关于共动距离的精确积分；CPL参数化下穿越幽灵边界不会导致背景方程奇异性；标量-张量框架下高频扰动以光速传播且有效普朗克质量为正；若标量场局域跟踪宇宙均值且无屏蔽机制，则Cassini实验严重限制其晚时引力效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能再现观测宇宙膨胀历史（如暗能量行为）但自身不直接贡献应力-能量的‘隐形’标量场模型，以探索暗能量本质与引力理论修正之间的关系，并检验其理论自洽性与观测约束。

Method: 基于FLRW度规，设定标量场为均匀（仅依赖时间），要求其应力张量恒为零（即‘隐形’），从而将膨胀历史完全归因于独立背景成分；通过消去自相互作用势，导出描述标量场演化的Riccati微分方程；结合解析技巧（如超几何函数表示、共形耦合简化、CPL参数化分析）分类求解并分析解的正则性、唯一性与动力学不变性；进一步讨论线性扰动传播性质（声速、有效普朗克质量）及太阳系尺度（Cassini）实验约束。

Result: 1) Riccati方程可严格区分隐形性、全局正则性与暗能量密度强对应；2) 强对应在混合常w宇宙学中不具动力学不变性；3) 无辐射wCDM下存在背景依赖的正则/奇异解阈值；4) 共形耦合下获得适用于任意平直正则历史（含辐射）的显式积分解；5) CPL幽灵穿越不引发背景方程奇点；6) 高频扰动传播速度为光速，有效普朗克质量为正；7) Cassini实验对无屏蔽局域跟踪情形施加极强晚时引力响应压制。

Conclusion: ‘隐形’标量场作为暗能量替代理论虽在背景演化上灵活，但其理论自洽性（如动力学不变性、正则性）与观测兼容性（尤其太阳系尺度）面临严峻挑战；共形耦合提供更稳健框架，而局域无屏蔽情形已被高精度实验强烈排除。

Abstract: We reconstruct homogeneous stealth scalar fields on prescribed Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker backgrounds with constant or evolving dark-energy equations of state. The scalar stress tensor vanishes, so the expansion history is supported by an independent background sector. Eliminating the self-interaction potential yields a Riccati equation for the scalar profile. We use it to distinguish exact stealthness from global regularity and from the stronger identification of a term in the scalar stress tensor with the prescribed dark-energy density. We prove that this sector-preserving identification is not dynamically invariant in a mixed flat constant-$w$ cosmology. For radiation-free $w$CDM backgrounds, a hypergeometric representation allows the regular and singular branches to be separated by a background-dependent threshold. At conformal coupling the reconstruction instead reduces to an exact comoving-distance quadrature, valid for arbitrary regular flat histories, including radiation. A CPL phantom-divide crossing does not singularize the background stealth equation, although the perturbations of the prescribed dark-energy sector require an independent treatment. The scalar--tensor description gives luminal high-frequency propagation under a positive effective Planck mass. If the field tracks its cosmological value locally without screening, Cassini constraints strongly suppress the corresponding late-time gravitational responses.

</details>


### [166] [Relativistic Simulations of Self-Gravitating Radiation Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10320)
*Bing-Jyun Tsao,Pablo Laguna,Octavio Guerrero*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在广义相对论流体力学框架下模拟纯辐射流体的数值方法，将辐射建模为零静止质量的自引力完美流体，并基于Whisky代码和Einstein Toolkit实现了相关修改与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强引力时空（如黑洞附近）中准确模拟辐射流体行为的需求，现有方法难以兼顾辐射的相对论性与自引力效应。

Method: 将辐射视为零静止质量的自引力完美流体；在Whisky（Einstein Toolkit内）中修改源项、变量恢复方案和黎曼求解器；通过标准激波管测试验证。

Result: 成功模拟了辐射向单个黑洞的吸积过程；构造并演化了处于不稳定流体静力学平衡的‘光子星’解，展示了其在扰动下的坍缩与弥散行为。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效处理强场中辐射流体的自引力与相对论耦合问题，为研究高能天体物理过程（如伽马暴、活动星系核辐射输运）提供了新工具。

Abstract: We present an approach for numerical simulations of pure radiation fluids within the general relativistic hydrodynamics framework. The approach models radiation as a self-gravitating perfect fluid with vanishing rest mass, enabling simulations of radiation fluids in strong-gravity spacetimes. Building on the existing relativistic hydrodynamics code Whisky, within the Einstein Toolkit framework, we introduce modifications to incorporate radiation fluids, including updates to the source terms, recovery scheme, and Riemann solvers. Modifications are validated using standard shock tube tests. We present results for radiation accretion onto a single black hole, as well as a photon star solution for radiation in unstable hydrostatic equilibrium, demonstrating both collapse and dispersion under perturbations.

</details>


### [167] [Shallow-water black holes and lattice superradiance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10539)
*Antonino Flachi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过构建浅水涡旋的晶格流体模型，实现了旋转类比黑洞，并利用地形门槛设定事件视界，从而在实验室中观测到超辐射现象；实验区分了视界本身与流体耗散对能量提取的贡献，揭示了超辐射机制，并为类比引力实验提供了低成本、高灵活性平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分离黑洞视界本身的作用与周围流体耗散的影响，以更清晰地理解超辐射机制，并在实验室中实现可控的类比引力系统。

Method: 构建基于浅水涡旋的晶格流体模型，用顶部地形门槛定义事件视界，使唯一耗散源位于超临界层后（不可逃逸区）；测量不同旋转模式波的能量通量方向，并在三种晶格粘度下扫描分析视界与内部粘性区域对能量提取的相对贡献。

Result: 观测到共转且模式转速慢于视界的波出现向外能量通量（即超辐射），而更快、反向旋转或非旋转情形则为向内通量；低频时视界主导吸收，近能带边时粘性贡献达一半，最低粘度下粘性贡献消失；排水锚定视界也呈现相同符号特征。

Conclusion: 超辐射的能量提取主要源于视界本身的几何与运动学特性，而非流体粘性耗散；该晶格流体平台能有效解耦视界与耗散效应，为类比引力研究提供新范式。

Abstract: Rotating black holes can amplify waves that scatter off them, a process known as superradiance that fluid analogues bring into the laboratory. Separating the horizon's role from dissipation in the surrounding fluid remains difficult. Here we realise a rotating analogue black hole in a lattice fluid: a shallow-water vortex whose horizon is set by a topographic sill, so that the only sink lies behind a supercritical layer that lets nothing out. We measure superradiance as energy extraction: the flux at the horizon is outward for co-rotating waves whose pattern rotates more slowly than the horizon, and inward for faster waves, counter-rotating waves and a non-rotating hole. A scan over three lattice viscosities separates the extraction at the horizon, which is inviscid, from that of the viscous fluid inside the corotation radius: well below the band edge the horizon is the absorber; near the edge the viscous share is half at the operating viscosity and vanishes at the lowest. A drain-anchored horizon, as in water-tank experiments, gives the same signs. Our analysis gives insight into the mechanism of superradiance and offers a cheap, flexible platform for designing analogue-gravity experiments.

</details>


### [168] [Engineering a digital acoustic black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10540)
*Antonino Flachi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于同步数字电路（FPGA）实现类比黑洞的方法，定义了其声速、流场和视界，并通过电学测量验证了视界稳定性、因果锥、环振及旋转超辐射等现象，表明数字电路可作为可重构的引力类比系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索引力现象的实验模拟方法，特别是利用易于控制和重构的人工系统（如数字电路）来类比黑洞等广义相对论效应。

Method: 将类比黑洞建模为同步数字电路，定义其逻辑层面的声速、流场与视界；在FPGA上实现该电路，并用电信号发生器和示波器进行电学探测。

Result: 成功观测到视界稳定性、因果结构（因果锥）、环振行为和旋转超辐射；发现视界内单个翻转比特的信息以指数形式向外泄露。

Conclusion: 数字电路可作为一类新型、可重构的引力类比系统，且其实现本质上是一种细胞自动机形式的类比黑洞。

Abstract: We show how to design and build an analogue black hole as a synchronous digital circuit. We define the sound speed, the flow and the horizon of such a circuit in terms of its own logic, and realise one on a field-programmable gate array, where the black hole is probed electrically with a signal generator and an oscilloscope. We measure the stability of the horizon, its causal cone, the ringdown and rotational superradiance. A single flipped bit inside the horizon leaks out by an amount that falls exponentially with depth. Digital circuits thus form a reconfigurable class of gravitational analogues and realise an analogue black hole as a cellular automaton.

</details>


### [169] [ERG, axions, and a PT-symmetric accelerating Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10584)
*Nick E. Mavromatos,Sarben Sarkar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于PT对称性的引力理论，用以解释宇宙晚期加速膨胀现象，无需暗能量成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释宇宙晚期加速膨胀现象，无需引入暗能量成分。

Method: 基于弦理论启发的场景，结合PT对称性、反常Chern-Simons项与轴子场耦合，并在精确重整化群（ERG）框架下分析其β函数奇点。

Result: 发现ERG β函数的奇点可能标志场论从厄米相向PT对称相的红外相变，对应引力出现排斥性的牛顿常数。

Conclusion: PT对称引力理论可能为宇宙加速膨胀提供新机制，替代传统暗能量模型。

Abstract: In conventional quantum mechanics, the Hamiltonian is required to be Hermitian, ensuring real energy eigenvalues. Certain non-Hermitian Hamiltonians can also possess entirely real spectra when they are invariant under the combined action of parity ($\mathcal P$) and time reversal ($\mathcal T$). Such Hamiltonians are called unbroken parity--time ($\mathcal{PT}$)-symmetric. We conjecture that, in a string-inspired scenario, a repulsive phase of gravity may be due to a $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric gravitational theory, characterised by anomalous Chern-Simons terms, coupled to axion fields. The conjecture is based on the behaviour of the chiral anomalous couplings of these axions to electromagnetic potentials. Within the Exact Renormalisation Group (ERG) framework in the modified local potential approximation, we argue that singularities in the ERG beta functions for such couplings may indicate an infrared phase transition. At the energy scales of these singularities, we conjecture that the field theory transitions from the Hermitian phase to a ${\mathcal P}{\mathcal T}$-- symmetric theory, of gravity, characterised by a repulsive Netwon's ``constant''. This may induce the acceleration observed in the Universe at late eras, without the need for a dark--energy component.

</details>


### [170] [Generalized Second Law of Thermodynamics in $f(Q)$ Symmetric Teleparallel Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10604)
*Nadeem Azhar,Kayoomars Karami*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了f(Q)对称度规无关引力理论中宇宙学视界上的广义热力学第二定律（GSL）有效性，推导出视界熵时间导数与物质熵率结合的简洁主公式，并验证了三种可观测f(Q)模型在整个宇宙演化中均满足GSL，其鲁棒性源于哈勃参数时间导数的符号（即无幽灵穿越）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验广义热力学第二定律在f(Q)对称度规无关引力框架下的普适性与有效性，拓展引力热力学基础并联系观测可行模型。

Method: 采用Wald型熵公式Af_Q/4G并结合由修正弗里德曼方程导出的修正项，推导并符号验证f(Q)引力中视界熵的闭式表达式；构建总熵变率主公式；将该形式应用于幂律、指数和对数三类观测可行f(Q)模型，并分析其能量条件。

Result: 得到简洁的总熵变率主公式T_AṠ_tot = 4πḢ(Ḣ f_Q + Hḟ_Q)/H⁴（8πG=1），且在GR极限下正确还原；三种f(Q)模型在整个宇宙历史中均满足GSL；GSL成立的普适判据简化为Ḣ的符号（即无phantom crossing）；不同模型违反不同能量条件。

Conclusion: 在f(Q)引力中，只要总有效膨胀历史不发生幽灵穿越（即Ḣ ≥ 0），GSL即普遍成立；该结论独立于具体f(Q)模型形式，揭示了GSL深层几何结构约束；不同模型虽满足GSL，但其几何能动量张量表现出差异化的能量条件破缺行为。

Abstract: We investigate the validity of the generalized second law (GSL) of thermodynamics on the cosmological apparent horizon in $f(Q)$ symmetric teleparallel gravity, using the Wald-type entropy prefactor $Af_Q/4G$ together with a correction term derived directly from the modified Friedmann equations. We derive, and verify by explicit symbolic computation, a closed-form expression for the apparent horizon entropy in $f(Q)$ gravity and show that its time derivative combines with the matter entropy rate into a remarkably compact master formula, $T_A\dot S_{\rm tot} = 4π\dot H(\dot H f_Q + H\dot f_Q)/H^4$ (in units $8πG=1$), which correctly reduces to the known general-relativistic limit. We apply this formalism to three observationally viable $f(Q)$ models --- power-law, exponential, and logarithmic. We find that the GSL is satisfied throughout cosmic history for all three models, and relate this robustness to a general, theory-independent structural condition: for ordinary pressureless matter obeying the null energy condition, GSL validity reduces to the sign of $\dot H$, i.e., to the absence of a phantom crossing in the total effective expansion history. The geometric sector exhibits model-dependent energy conditions, power-law and exponential models violate only the strong condition, while the logarithmic model violates the null, weak, and dominant conditions for \(z>-1\), and the strong condition for \(z<3.84\).

</details>


### [171] [Scalar-tensor reconstruction of coherent quantum black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10616)
*R. Casadio,A. Giusti,R. da Rocha*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个标量-张量理论，用于描述由量子相干态产生的球对称黑洞几何结构，该结构具有有限核心和有限延展性；研究了非最小耦合、动能耦合和标量势，并指出动能项仅在有限区域内为正；进一步表明同一几何也可由含导数耦合的Horndeski作用量实现；但两种重构均不包含事件视界，也无法同时精确复现黑洞内外任意大片区域，暗示其量子起源部分超出了半经典引力理论的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索黑洞内部量子结构如何在半经典引力框架下（特别是标量-张量理论）被有效描述，理解量子相干态所诱导的无奇点、有限尺度黑洞几何的理论实现方式。

Method: 通过逆向工程（reconstruction）方法，从给定的球对称、具有限核心与延展性的黑洞度规出发，反推满足该几何的标量-张量理论中的非最小耦合函数、动能耦合函数及标量势；并进一步推广至Horndeski理论框架，分析其导数耦合形式及有效性区域。

Result: 成功构造出对应于该量子启发几何的标量-张量模型，发现动能项正定性仅在有限径向区域内成立，该区域边界自然关联核心尺寸与空间延展；同时获得等效的Horndeski实现，并明确其定义域；两种理论均无法包含事件视界，且不能一致覆盖黑洞大范围内外区。

Conclusion: 该类源于量子相干态的正则黑洞几何虽可在特定标量-张量或Horndeski理论中局部实现，但其整体结构（尤其涉及视界与大范围时空）本质上超越了当前半经典引力的有效适用范围，提示需更基本的量子引力描述。

Abstract: We construct a scalar-tensor realisation of spherically symmetric black hole geometries that effectively arise from quantum coherent states characterised by a finite core and finite extension. We determine the non-minimal coupling to the Einstein-Hilbert Lagrangian, kinetic coupling, and scalar potential and show that the kinetic term is positive only in finite domains. For fixed black hole mass, the boundaries of these domains provide a natural mechanism that relates the core size and spatial extension to the dynamics of the scalar-tensor realisation. We further show that the same geometry can also be obtained from a Horndeski action with derivative coupling and determine its domain of validity. Neither reconstruction includes the event horizon nor can it simultaneously reproduce arbitrarily large portions of the interior and exterior of the black hole, whose quantum origin therefore appears to remain partially outside the regime of applicability of semiclassical theories of gravity.

</details>


### [172] [Black-hole ringdown at an exceptional point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10661)
*Kyriakos Destounis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了旋转带电dS黑洞在异常点附近光子球与近视界模式合并时的响应特性，发现其响应可带有线性时间因子，并通过约束爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦数据计算了该因子振幅；结果显示双模拟合精度（0.00383%）优于单指数拟合（0.01401%），且可通过调节初始扰动抑制线性项，进而影响短时环down拟合所得频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞环down信号中异常点附近的模式合并行为及其对引力波数据分析的影响，特别是初始条件如何调控可观测频率。

Method: 基于约束的爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦方程组，数值求解旋转带电de Sitter黑洞背景下的准正规模，分析光子球与近视界模式在异常点处的合并行为及响应函数的时间演化特征。

Result: 在异常点附近，模式合并导致响应含线性时间因子；七模拟合下曲率误差为0.00383%（双模）和0.01401%（重拟合指数）；通过调制初始扰动可抑制线性项，并改变短时环down拟合所推断的频率。

Conclusion: 黑洞环down的频谱解析不仅依赖于几何参数，还敏感依赖于初始扰动；在模式接近共alescence时，仅改变初始条件即可显著影响频率提取结果，这对引力波波形建模和参数估计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Black holes ring in damped oscillations. At an exceptional point, photon-sphere and near-horizon modes merge, and their response can acquire a linear time factor. We compute its amplitude from constrained Einstein-Maxwell data for a rotating, charged black hole in de Sitter space. With seven competing modes fixed, the late-time curvature error is 0.00383$\%$ for the pair and 0.01401$\%$ for a refitted exponential. We suppress the linear term by tuning the initial disturbance at fixed geometry. Hence, near coalescence, changing only the initial disturbance can shift the frequency inferred from a short ringdown fit.

</details>


### [173] [Deep-Learning-Assisted Bayesian Inference for Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11922)
*Jian-Jun Song,Zhong-Wu Xia,Wen-Hong Ruan,Zu-Cheng Chen,Hongwei Yu,Puxun Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合深度学习、粒子群优化和MCMC的贝叶斯推断框架，用于解决极端质量比旋进（EMRI）信号参数估计中似然面高度多峰、难以定位高似然区域的问题。该方法无需真实参数初始化即可获得与参考MCMC结果一致的后验分布，单信号推理耗时约10小时（训练完成后）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRI信号的贝叶斯参数推断面临似然面复杂、高度多模、窄带高似然区域难定位的挑战，且真实源参数在实际观测中未知，导致传统方法依赖真值初始化，不具实用性。

Method: 提出一种深度学习辅助的贝叶斯推断框架：首先用神经网络引导参数空间定位，再结合粒子群优化（PSO）粗略搜索高似然区域，最后用MCMC进行精细采样；应用于六个月模拟EMRI信号。

Result: 该框架在无需真实参数初始化的前提下，成功恢复出与以真值初始化的参考MCMC分析高度一致的后验分布；训练完成后单信号推理时间约为10小时。

Conclusion: 深度学习可有效提升EMRI贝叶斯推断的鲁棒性与效率，克服对先验真值的依赖，为未来LISA等引力波探测任务中的实时或准实时参数估计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) pose a major challenge to Bayesian parameter inference because of their complex, highly multimodal likelihood surfaces. Locating narrow high-likelihood regions is particularly difficult without knowledge of the true source parameters, which is unavailable in real observations. We develop a deep-learning-assisted Bayesian inference framework that combines neural-network-guided parameter-space localization, particle swarm optimization, and Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) sampling. Applied to simulated six-month EMRI signals, the framework recovers posterior distributions in close agreement with reference MCMC analyses initialized near the true parameter values, without requiring such knowledge for initialization. Once the neural network has been trained, the complete inference pipeline takes approximately 10 hours per signal.

</details>


### [174] [The Energy-Momentum Tensor of a Compact Object in General Relativity to all Multipole Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10783)
*Trevor Scheopner,Felix Lichtner*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon形式的自然张量基，用于唯一地分离广义相对论中致密天体的自旋和潮汐效应，并给出了能量-动量张量的精确分布公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广义相对论中，有效场论方法将致密天体的自旋与潮汐效应编码为黎曼张量及其导数构成的算符，但许多此类算符对可观测量存在简并贡献，单个算符常影响多个多极矩，需构建独立且一一对应的张量基。

Method: 采用Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon形式论，构造一组彼此独立的张量，每个张量严格共轭于一个约化多极矩；并据此推导出致密天体能量-动量张量的精确分布表达式。

Result: 得到了致密天体能量-动量张量关于其约化多极矩和所构造张量基的新型精确分布公式。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了多极展开中算符与多极矩之间的一一对应关系，为高精度引力波建模和致密双星动力学研究提供了更清晰、无简并的理论框架。

Abstract: In effective field theory approaches to compact object motion in general relativity, spin and tidal effects are encoded by operators formed from the Riemann tensor and its derivatives. Many such operators contribute degenerately to observables, with a single one often affecting several multipole moments of the body. Using the Mathisson--Papapetrou--Dixon formalism, we construct a natural basis of independent tensors which are each canonically conjugate to precisely one multipole. We find the novel exact distributional formula for the energy-momentum tensor of a compact object in terms of its reduced multipole moments and these tensors.

</details>


### [175] [Variational Approach to the Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon Equations of Motion for Extended Objects in General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10794)
*Trevor Scheopner,Felix Lichtner*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文综述了Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon方程，提出了一种适用于相对论致密天体有效描述的规范不变变分原理，并讨论了协变自旋补充条件的适用条件及与物体多极矩共轭的曲率张量自然基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为精确描述有限尺寸物体在弯曲时空中的运动，并构建适用于相对论致密天体的有效理论框架。

Method: 基于规范不变的变分原理，系统推导并重构Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon理论，分析自旋补充条件及曲率张量与多极矩的共轭关系。

Result: 建立了直接、规范不变的Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon理论构建方法，明确了协变自旋补充条件的适用条件，并识别出与多极矩共轭的曲率张量自然基。

Conclusion: 该工作为致密双星等系统的后牛顿和有效场论建模提供了坚实、几何清晰且规范不变的理论基础。

Abstract: The exact motion of a finite size object in curved spacetime is captured by the Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon equations. They associate a unique worldline, momentum, and spin to that object and determine the evolution of such in terms of its fully independent multipole moments. We provide a thorough review of this theory and show how to build it directly via a manifestly gauge invariant variational principle well-suited for effective descriptions of relativistic compact objects. We discuss conditions under which the covariant spin supplementary condition can be enforced and identify the natural basis of curvature tensors conjugate to the object's multipole moments.

</details>


### [176] [A note on Positivity and Rigidity for an Expanding Quasi-Local Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10880)
*Eric Ling,Annachiara Piubello*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了与恒定脐形初始数据相关的扩展拟局部能量E_λ的正性与刚性，证明了当E_λ在临界阈值α处为零时，区域(Ω,g)等距于半径至多为1/λ的闭欧氏3维球，并构造反例否定了此前关于该能量正性与刚性的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作已建立E_λ在λ低于几何阈值α时的正性，并发现E_λ=0在λ=α时迫使边界为球面；本文旨在加强该端点刚性结论并检验其极限有效性。

Method: 结合Shi-Tam关于Brown-York能量的刚性定理与严格凹性论证，并显式构造反例。

Result: 证明了当E_λ=0且λ=α时，(Ω,g)必等距于半径≤1/λ的闭欧氏3维球；同时给出反例表明E_λ可在λ>α时为负，且E_λ=0不一定蕴含刚性。

Conclusion: E_λ在临界阈值处具有更强的几何刚性，但其正性与刚性仅在特定范围内成立，超出后可能失效。

Abstract: We study positivity and rigidity for an expanding quasi-local energy $E_λ$ associated with constantly umbilic initial data $(Ω,g,k=λg)$, introduced in earlier work [ALP26] as an analogue of the Brown-York energy adapted to an expanding flat de Sitter background. Under nonnegative scalar curvature and suitable boundary assumptions, our previous results established positivity for $λ$ below a geometrically determined threshold $α$, while vanishing $E_λ$ at $λ=α$ forced the boundary to be round. In this paper, we strengthen this endpoint rigidity statement by showing that, if $E_λ=0$ when $λ=α$, then $(Ω,g)$ is isometric to a closed Euclidean 3-ball of radius at most $1/λ$. The proof combines the rigidity theorem of Shi-Tam for the Brown-York energy with a strict concavity argument. We also construct an explicit counterexample to the positivity and rigidity conjecture formulated in [ALP26], showing that the energy can become negative beyond this threshold and that vanishing energy need not imply rigidity.

</details>


### [177] [Qubit representation of the mass observable in quantized Schwarzschild interior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10933)
*Babak Vakili*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子计算框架，用于模拟惠勒-德维特量子化施瓦西黑洞内部的质量可观测量，通过构建有限维质量算符表示并映射到量子比特上，量化了所需量子资源，并在两量子比特变分本征解算器中验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立惠勒-德维特量子引力与量子计算之间的直接联系，并为引力可观测量的量子模拟提供结构化路径。

Method: 在由关联双模谐振子态张成的物理子空间中，构造质量算符的有限维表示，并通过泡利分解映射到量子比特算符；分析不同截断尺寸下的非零泡利项数与独立测量设置数的标度律；实现两量子比特变分量子本征解算器，并设计独立验证惠勒-德维特约束的量子线路。

Result: 给出了质量可观测量编码与测量所需量子资源的定量刻画；两量子比特VQE结果与精确有限维谱高度一致；提出了可独立验证惠勒-德维特约束的量子线路方案。

Conclusion: 该工作为黑洞内部量子引力可观测量的量子模拟奠定了基础，建立了量子计算与正则量子引力之间的实质性桥梁。

Abstract: We develop a quantum-computational framework for the mass observable of the Wheeler--DeWitt quantized Schwarzschild black-hole interior. Working in the physical sector spanned by correlated two-mode oscillator states, we construct finite-dimensional representations of the mass operator and map them onto qubit operators through Pauli decomposition. We analyze the resulting representations for increasing truncation sizes and establish analytically the scaling of the number of nonvanishing Pauli terms and independent measurement settings. These results provide a quantitative characterization of the quantum resources required to encode and measure the black-hole mass observable. As a representative benchmark, we implement a two-qubit variational quantum eigensolver for the smallest nontrivial truncation and find excellent agreement with the exact finite-dimensional spectrum. We further describe a quantum-circuit procedure for verifying the Wheeler--DeWitt constraint independently of the mass estimation. Our results provide a direct connection between Wheeler--DeWitt quantum gravity and quantum computation and establish a structured route toward the quantum simulation of gravitational observables.

</details>


### [178] [Thermodynamic, Optical, and Wave Signatures of Charged Letelier AdS Black Holes with PFDM and Quintessence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10942)
*B. Eslam Panah,S. Gurtas Dogan,A. Guvendi,O. Mustafa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个包含弦云、完美流体暗物质（PFDM）和Kiselev型幽灵能量的四维带电Letelier-反德西特（AdS）黑洞度规，系统推导了曲率不变量、视界温度、熵、电势及基于视界的质量函数，并分析了局域热响应与扩展相空间恒等式；计算了矩形热机循环功与效率，给出了光子球、阴影角及标量场径向Helmholtz方程等几何光学与波动性质；同时指出PFDM密度为负、质量函数需进一步边界归一化等关键限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建并分析一种能同时刻画弦云、暗物质与幽灵能量的广义AdS黑洞模型，以探索其热力学、几何光学与标量场动力学行为，并厘清现象学参数与物理可解释性之间的界限。

Method: 基于广义Einstein-Maxwell方程求解含多源物质的Letelier-AdS黑洞度规；解析推导曲率不变量、热力学量（温度、熵、电势、质量函数）、扩展相空间恒等式；构造矩形热机循环计算功与效率；利用测地线方程与有效势分析光子球与阴影；对质量less标量场建立径向Helmholtz方程并分析势垒与束缚区。

Result: 获得完整的热力学函数与相空间关系；得到具体参数下的热机功W=0.095000与效率η≈15.39%；导出光子球条件、临界撞击参数与有限半径阴影角；揭示标量场有效势中存在频率依赖势垒、隧穿区间及可能的束缚区域，且势垒顶尺度趋近光子球捕获尺度；明确指出PFDM密度为负、质量函数非直接对应渐近守恒质量等关键限制。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了对一类多成分现象学AdS黑洞的自洽参数化分析框架，强调代数热力学恒等式与需渐近结构或谱分析才能确立的物理结论（如准正规模、守恒质量）之间的本质区别，为后续更严格的引力源建模与可观测量关联研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We study a four-dimensional charged Letelier--anti-de Sitter (AdS) black-hole metric containing a cloud of strings, a phenomenological perfect-fluid-dark-matter (PFDM) source, and a Kiselev-type quintessence term. We derive the curvature invariants, horizon temperature, entropy, electric potential, and horizon-based mass function, and analyze local thermal response and the associated extended-phase-space identities. A rectangular benchmark cycle gives $W=0.095000$ and $η\simeq15.39\%$ for the explicitly stated geometric-unit parameters; these values are model- and cycle-specific and are not presented as a universal efficiency enhancement. For geometric optics, we derive the photon-sphere condition, critical impact parameter, and finite-radius shadow angle. For a massless, minimally coupled scalar field, we obtain a radial-coordinate Helmholtz equation and identify frequency-dependent barriers, evanescent intervals, and candidate trapping regions; the eikonal barrier-top scale approaches the photon-sphere capture scale. Two qualifications delimit the interpretation. First, with the stated PFDM stress-tensor convention, $ρ_{\rm PFDM}=-β/(8πr^3)$, so positive $β$ describes a negative-density effective source. Second, the mass function follows from the horizon equation, but identifying it with a conserved asymptotic AdS mass requires a separate charge and boundary-normalization analysis. The potential-well analysis likewise does not determine complex quasinormal frequencies. We therefore present the results as a consistent, parameter-specific analysis of the adopted phenomenological geometry, while distinguishing algebraic thermodynamic identities from conclusions requiring asymptotic-charge or spectral calculations.

</details>


### [179] [Horizon-penetrating rotating black-hole configurations in VCDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11775)
*Antonio De Felice*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了VCDM理论中正则黑洞度规与偏好叶状结构的关系，构建了旋转带电的静态解，并分析了其在Kerr-Newman及Kerr-Newman-de Sitter几何下的常平均曲率叶状结构特性，同时通过数值方法验证了解的正则性与渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究VCDM（具有偏好叶状结构的引力理论）中黑洞解是否能保持正则性，特别是其偏好叶状结构是否与标准黑洞几何兼容，并理解其在黑洞视界与宇宙学视界附近的动力学行为。

Method: 构造常平均曲率（CMC）叶状结构下的静态旋转带电黑洞解；采用通量公式刻画内区数据和不同渐近区域（双曲面型与膨胀型）；进行17种构型的数值扫描及固定四维几何下的平均通量扫描；结合连续残差、几何重构与网格比较验证数值解；推导精确的lapse函数边界条件。

Result: 得到穿越黑洞未来视界（及de Sitter情形下宇宙学视界）的非零常平均曲率叶状结构；发现通量变化仅影响偏好叶状结构的剪切而不改变标准测试物质所感知的背景几何；lapse与剪切的局域差异在计算范围内呈现近似平方的有效幂律；获得排除正则静态外区lapse零点的严格界限；区分了奇异有限固有距离端点与渐近‘喇叭形’端点，并给出静态势空类空内极限的条件性约束。

Conclusion: VCDM中的正则黑洞解需满足严格的叶状结构约束；静态构造无法唯一确定旋转黑洞内部终点与动力学选择；局部宇宙学解释需额外匹配假设；该工作为VCDM框架下黑洞与宇宙学统一建模提供了关键数值与几何基础。

Abstract: A regular black-hole metric need not define a regular configuration of VCDM, whose preferred foliation is part of the gravitational solution. We construct stationary rotating, possibly charged configurations on the constant-auxiliary Einstein-Maxwell branch. Their Kerr-Newman and Kerr-Newman-de Sitter geometries carry nonzero constant-mean-curvature foliations that cross the future black-hole horizon and, in the de Sitter case, the future cosmological horizon. A horizon-regular flux formulation specifies the inner data and the distinct hyperboloidal and expanding asymptotic ends. Seventeen survey configurations are supplemented by a mean-flux scan at fixed four-geometry and paired flat/de Sitter comparisons down to $κM = 0.01$, where $K_0 = 3κ$. Varying the flux changes the preferred-foliation shear without changing the background seen by standard test matter. The paired local lapse and shear differences have effective powers close to two over the computed range, with the lapse comparison requiring a fixed time normalization. Continuous residuals, geometric reconstructions, and selected grid comparisons test the numerical solutions. An exact lapse bound excludes zeros in the regular stationary exterior block with $Δ_r > 0$. We distinguish singular finite-proper-distance endpoints from asymptotic trumpet ends and give conditional bounds on a stationary spacelike inner limit. A local cosmological interpretation requires additional matching assumptions; dynamical selection and the rotating interior endpoint are not determined by the stationary construction.

</details>


### [180] [Quasinormal Modes of Black holes in the generalized Schwarzschild-de Sitter solution of $f(R)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11895)
*Dhruba Jyoti Gogoi,Himanshu Chaudhary,Tiberiu Harko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了修正f(R)引力中Schwarzschild型黑洞的标量、矢量和轴向张量扰动，计算了其拟正则模（QNMs），发现特定参数下结果接近广义相对论中的Schwarzschild黑洞，并揭示了模型参数对振荡频率与阻尼率的非线性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索修正f(R)引力理论中静态球对称黑洞解的稳定性及其引力波特征，以检验该理论与广义相对论在强场引力波观测上的可区分性。

Method: 基于含线性增长项和对数修正的f(R)引力Schwarzschild型解，利用高阶WKB近似和解析方法计算标量、矢量及轴向张量扰动的拟正则模，并辅以参数空间等高线图和时域波形分析。

Result: 发现参数A导致振荡频率和阻尼率非线性增大；参数η的影响依赖于A的大小，在小A时类似A效应，大A时效应相反；标量扰动在大A下显著偏离矢量和轴向张量扰动；时域演化验证了这些频域特征。

Conclusion: 该f(R)模型在适当参数下可复现Schwarzschild黑洞的QNMs，但参数变化会引发可观测的偏差，尤其标量扰动具有独特行为，为未来引力波观测甄别修正引力理论提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We analyze scalar, vector, and {axial} tensor perturbations in a Schwarzschild-type static spherically symmetric solution of modified $f(R)$ gravity. This solution differs from the Schwarzschild metric of general relativity through the presence of a linearly growing term and a logarithmic correction, and it depends on four integration constants. Using the higher-order Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin (WKB) approximation and an analytical method, we calculate the quasinormal modes (QNMs) for all three types of perturbations. Our results show that, for appropriately chosen parameter values, the QNMs closely match those of the Schwarzschild black hole. In particular, the model parameter $A$ produces a nonlinear increase in both the oscillation frequency and damping rate of ringdown gravitational waves. A similar effect arises for the parameter $η$ when $A$ is small, while for larger $A$ values the effects are opposite, where the scalar perturbations display a distinct deviation compared to the vector and {axial} tensor cases. Contour plots of QNMs in the parameter space support these findings. Finally, time-domain profiles are obtained to illustrate the dynamical evolution of the gravitational wave signal.

</details>


### [181] [Covariant Hamiltonian Gravity in Spherical Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11099)
*Cong Zhang,Zhoujian Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于球对称协变哈密顿引力理论，构建了描述引力动力学的有效爱因斯坦方程，并证明任意静态球对称度规族均可作为其真空解；应用该框架构造了广义Vaidya解，其能量动量张量严格呈现标准Vaidya形式，表明标准零尘可作为超越广义相对论（包括正则黑洞）的动力学黑洞的物质源；进一步指出，在该有效框架下，ingoing Vaidya解的质量损失必然违反零能量条件，暗示量子引力修正本身可能不足以解释类霍金辐射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超越广义相对论的引力理论中动力学黑洞的物质源机制，并检验量子引力修正是否足以解释霍金辐射类的质量损失现象。

Method: 基于球对称协变哈密顿引力框架，构建能以任意一参数静态球对称度规族为真空解的有效爱因斯坦方程；进而推导并分析广义Vaidya解及其对应的能量动量张量和能量条件。

Result: 成功构造出满足要求的有效爱因斯坦方程及广义Vaidya解；确认其能量动量张量为标准Vaidya形式；发现ingoing Vaidya解的质量损失必须违反零能量条件。

Conclusion: 标准零尘可作为多种修改引力理论（含正则黑洞）中动力学黑洞的合理物质源；但仅靠量子引力修正可能无法自洽产生霍金式蒸发，因其需违背零能量条件。

Abstract: Building upon the covariant Hamiltonian gravity in spherical symmetry, we construct the effective Einstein equation governing gravitational dynamics. For any prescribed one-parameter family of static, spherically symmetric metrics, this equation can be constructed such that the prescribed family constitutes their vacuum solutions. As an application, we construct generalized Vaidya solutions to the effective Einstein equation and show that the corresponding energy-momentum tensor takes precisely the standard Vaidya form. The results demonstrate that standard null dust sources can serve as matter source for dynamical black-hole geometries beyond general relativity, including regular black holes. Furthermore, this work suggests that quantum gravity corrections alone may not account for Hawking-like evaporation, as mass loss in the ingoing Vaidya solution necessarily requires a violation of the null energy condition within our effective framework.

</details>


### [182] [Exceptional Points in Scalar Quasinormal Modes of Rastall String-Fluid Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11366)
*Ming Zhang,Xiao-Mei Wang,Ya-Nan Wang,Miao Wang,Lei Zhang,Xufen Zhang,De-Cheng Zou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Rastall弦流体黑洞标量准正规模中的例外点（EPs），通过在标量势中加入局域Pöschl-Teller扰动，并以隆起位置a和幅度ε作为独立调控参数；采用双区域切比雪夫配置法结合任意精度线性代数求解双曲面问题，并利用复判别式、本征向量融合、Jordan链残差、平方根分裂及闭合回路模交换等多重判据验证缺陷性；在常规A=1、ε_R=-1分支中定位到一个数值稳定的二阶EP，而在对数A=2分支中未确认连续谱意义上的EP存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Rastall弦流体黑洞中准正规模的例外点，理解其非厄米物理特性与时空结构的关系。

Method: 引入局域Pöschl-Teller势扰动，用双域Chebyshev配置法求解超曲面问题，结合任意精度线性代数与多种数学判据（复判别式、本征向量共存、Jordan链残差等）识别例外点。

Result: 在常规A=1、ε_R=-1分支中发现一个分辨率稳定的二阶例外点，并观测到Jordan型响应(C+Dt)e^{iω_EP t}；在对数A=2分支中，有限截断下虽出现缺陷点，但其坐标不收敛，故无法确认其在连续谱中存在。

Conclusion: 例外点可在特定Rastall弦流体黑洞参数分支中稳健实现，为非厄米引力系统提供了明确模型；而另一分支缺乏收敛证据，提示例外点存在具有强参数依赖性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study exceptional points (EPs) in scalar quasinormal modes(QNMs) of Rastall string-fluid black holes by adding a localized Pöschl--Teller perturbation to the scalar potential, with the bump position $a$ and amplitude $\eb$ serving as independent controls. We solve the hyperboloidal problem with two-domain Chebyshev collocation and arbitrary-precision linear algebra, and use the complex discriminant, eigenvector coalescence, Jordan-chain residuals, square-root splitting and closed-loop mode exchange as complementary tests of defectiveness. For the regular $A=1$, $\eR=-1$ branch, the two-parameter search locates a resolution-stable second-order EP from $N=24$ to $N=60$, while constrained semidiscrete evolution gives the Jordan response $(C+Dt)e^{\iiω_{\rm EP}t}$. For the logarithmic $A=2$ branch, defective points occur in each finite truncation but their coordinates do not converge, so we make no continuum claim for this branch.

</details>


### [183] [Negative Dynamical Friction in Dark Matter Spikes around Rotating Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11380)
*Wei Xiong,Peng-Cheng Li,Bin Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了暗物质对极端质量比旋进（EMRIs）的影响，发现旋转大质量黑洞周围的暗物质晕可通过负动力学摩擦向次要黑洞供能，显著改变其轨道相位，并指出环境能量交换与保守度规响应均对高精度EMRI建模至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质可能通过轨道能量交换和保守度规响应影响极端质量比旋进（EMRIs），但其具体机制和建模重要性尚需量化分析。

Method: 理论建模与解析计算，分析旋转大质量黑洞周围暗物质晕对稳定顺行圆轨道上次级黑洞的动力学摩擦效应，并估算其对轨道相位及自作用修正的影响。

Result: 发现暗物质晕可引发负动力学摩擦，使次级黑洞获得轨道能量；该效应在黑洞自旋超过阈值时延伸至最内稳定圆轨道之外；四年内相位差达约100弧度，环境红移修正量级与一阶保守自作用修正相当但符号相反。

Conclusion: 暗物质引起的环境能量交换与保守度规响应均不可忽略，是实现高精度EMRI波形建模的必要物理要素。

Abstract: Dark matter may affect extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) through orbital-energy exchange and a conservative metric response. Here we show that dark matter spikes around rotating massive black holes can drive negative dynamical friction, supplying orbital energy to secondary black holes on stable prograde circular orbits. The energy-gain region extends beyond the innermost stable circular orbit once the black-hole spin exceeds a model-dependent threshold. For an illustrative four-year inspiral with Milky Way--like halo parameters and mass ratio $q=10^{-5}$, the total dynamical-friction contribution produces a phase difference of order $10^2$ radians relative to vacuum. For the same system, the fixed-frequency environmental redshift correction is comparable in magnitude and opposite in sign to the first-order conservative self-force correction. Both environmental energy exchange and the conservative metric response are therefore essential to precision EMRI modeling.

</details>


### [184] [Pushing the accuracy of the quasi-circular baseline for the SEOBNR family of models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11449)
*Cheng Foo,Alessandra Buonanno,Héctor Estellés,Lorenzo Pompili*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了SEOBNRv5iHM，一种改进的准圆、对齐自旋引力波波形模型，通过引入现象学中间区域优化了晚期旋进与并合-衰荡阶段建模，显著提升了与数值相对论波形的匹配精度，尤其在高阶模式和大质量双黑洞系统中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着引力波探测器灵敏度提升，对双黑洞信号进行高精度建模对源参数刻画和天体物理推断愈发关键；现有模型（如SEOBNRv5HM）在晚期旋进与并合-衰荡连接处存在非准圆修正带来的误差，需改进。

Method: 基于有效单体（EOB）框架，在SEOBNRv5HM基础上构建SEOBNRv5iHM：取消非准圆修正，引入可独立设定环降起始时间（含各阶球谐模式峰值时刻）的现象学中间区域；模型使用SXS v3目录中591个准圆对齐自旋数值相对论波形进行校准与验证。

Result: 对于(2,2)模，在总质量10–300 M⊙范围内，最大失配中位数达4.25×10⁻⁵，比SEOBNRv5HM低4.5倍、比其他半解析模型低3–8倍；84%样本低于10⁻⁴；M≥150 M⊙时中位数降至9.7×10⁻⁶，媲美NRHybSur3dq8；含ℓ_max=5多极模式、倾角π/3下的偏振失配中位数为4.2×10⁻⁴，优于SEOBNRv5HM、IMRPhenomTHM及其他半解析模型。

Conclusion: SEOBNRv5iHM显著提升了准圆对齐自旋波形精度，尤其改善了高阶模式建模与大质量系统性能，为其衍生的进动与偏心模型奠定更可靠基础，有助于降低未来引力波数据分析中的波形系统误差。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave observations have opened a new era of astronomy, with accurate modeling of binary black hole signals becoming increasingly important for source characterization and astrophysical inference as detector sensitivities improve. We present SEOBNRv5iHM, an updated quasi-circular, aligned-spin inspiral-merger-ringdown gravitational-waveform model with higher-order modes, constructed within the effective-one-body formalism and building upon SEOBNRv5HM. The improvements mainly concern the late inspiral and the merger-ringdown: we remove the non-quasi-circular corrections used in previous models and instead introduce a phenomenological intermediate region between the inspiral and the ringdown, which allows the ringdown attachment time to be chosen independently for each mode, including at the peak of the mode's amplitude. The model is calibrated and validated against 591 quasi-circular aligned-spin waveforms from the third Simulating eXtreme Spacetimes catalog. For the $(2,2)$ mode, when considering the maximum unfaithfulness against numerical relativity over a total mass range of 10-300$M_\odot$, SEOBNRv5iHM achieves a median of $4.25\times10^{-5}$, a factor of 4.5 below SEOBNRv5HM, and a factor of 3-8 below other semi-analytical models, with $84\%$ of cases below $10^{-4}$. For $M\ge150 M_\odot$, the median drops to $9.7\times10^{-6}$, comparable to the numerical-relativity surrogate NRHybSur3dq8. For polarization mismatches at inclination $π/3$, with modes up to $\ell_\mathrm{max} = 5$, the median maximum unfaithfulness is $4.2\times10^{-4}$, a factor of 2.6 below SEOBNRv5HM, 1.9 below IMRPhenomTHM, and 6-11 below the other semi-analytical models. Since spin-precessing and eccentric models are built on top of the quasi-circular, aligned-spin baseline, these improvements will make future SEOBNR models more accurate, reducing waveform systematics in upcoming analyses.

</details>


### [185] [Smooth Rigidity Near Extremal Horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11495)
*Ye Zhou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究退化Killing视界附近真空爱因斯坦时空的光滑性与唯一性问题，证明在特定几何条件下（如商空间度量存在类空图），具有相同横向Jet的光滑真空爱因斯坦芽在视界邻域内局部等距；并推广至Einstein-Maxwell理论和Kerr-de Sitter/旋转Nariai情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退化Killing视界处的横向Taylor级数虽受爱因斯坦方程约束，但未必唯一确定光滑时空——可能存在所有阶均消失的非零差异；本文旨在消除该模糊性，建立光滑分类与唯一延拓准则。

Method: 结合终端波映射规范（terminal wave-map gauge）与轨道柱上的能量估计，推导加权有限匹配判据；利用Lichnerowicz谱条件简化分类，并构造多扭曲Einstein实现；对四维Einstein-Maxwell及旋转Nariai情形进行推广分析。

Result: 在d≥4维、商空间度量可表为紧致视界截面的类空图时，相同标记横向Jet的光滑真空爱因斯坦芽在双侧邻域局部等距；对正Einstein截面，Lichnerowicz谱条件将光滑分类归约为平行首阶横向数据；所有此类数据均有收敛的多扭曲Einstein实现；得到四维带电Nariai刚性及a²/r₀²≤1/8时的旋转Nariai/Kerr-de Sitter刚性。

Conclusion: 视界处几何结构（特别是商空间度量的类空可表性）是决定爱因斯坦时空局部唯一性的关键；该框架统一处理真空、电磁耦合及旋转情形，提供有限正则性与光滑性两种刚性结果。

Abstract: The Einstein equations constrain the transverse Taylor series at a degenerate Killing horizon, but even complete knowledge of that series need not determine a smooth spacetime: a nonzero difference can vanish to every order at the horizon. We show that this ambiguity disappears when the limiting metric on the space of Killing orbits admits a spacelike graph over a compact horizon section. In dimensions $d\geq4$, smooth vacuum Einstein germs with the same marked transverse jets are then locally isometric on a two-sided neighborhood of the whole section. The proof combines a terminal wave-map gauge with energy estimates on the orbit cylinder and yields a finite weighted matching criterion. For unwarped horizons with positive-Einstein sections, a condition on the full Lichnerowicz spectrum reduces the smooth classification to parallel first transverse data. All such data have convergent multiwarped Einstein realizations, including examples whose sections have negative sectional-curvature directions. We also obtain a finite-regularity version for round sections. The continuation argument extends to four-dimensional Einstein--Maxwell theory and gives smooth charged-Nariai rigidity. Finally, rotating Nariai data admit the required spacelike graph throughout the regular branch; an all-order tensor estimate yields smooth Kerr--de Sitter or rotating-Nariai rigidity for $a^2/r_0^2\leq1/8$, without assuming ambient axial symmetry.

</details>


### [186] [Quasinormal modes of a scale-dependent black hole in 5D spacetime with cosmological constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11584)
*Ángel Rincón,Zdeněk Stuchlík*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维尺度依赖黑洞在正宇宙学常数下的准正规模（QNMs），考虑了标量、电磁和狄拉克扰动；通过尺度依赖引力框架下的精确解析解和一阶近似方法，计算了不同尺度依赖参数ε下的QNMs频率，并验证了所有扰动下解的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究尺度依赖引力理论中五维带正宇宙学常数黑洞的准正规模行为，检验其相对于广义相对论的偏离效应及稳定性。

Method: 基于尺度依赖引力理论推导五维黑洞的精确解析解，采用一阶近似处理视界函数以匹配小ε情形，并利用WKB半解析方法计算标量、电磁和狄拉克扰动下的准正规模频率。

Result: 得到了不同ε值下三类扰动的QNMs频率（列于图表中），且所有情况下频率虚部均为负，表明系统稳定。

Conclusion: 在所考察的尺度依赖参数范围内，五维尺度依赖黑洞对各类扰动均保持稳定，支持该类修正引力模型的物理自洽性。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the quasinormal modes (QNMs) of a scale-dependent black hole in five dimensions with a positive cosmological constant, considering i) scalar, ii) electromagnetic, and iii) Dirac perturbations. After deriving exact analytical solutions within the framework of scale-dependent gravity in five dimensions and selecting a different family of solutions (cf. \cite{Contreras:2019fwu}), we analyze the corresponding effective potentials (given by the lapse function) for each perturbation type. We employ a first-order approximation for the lapse function, as it closely matches the exact case for small $\varepsilon$. Since quantum corrections are expected to be small, our focus is on a relatively low range of the scale-dependent parameter $\varepsilon$. We confirm that the WKB semi-analytical approach can be used to compute the QNMs for these perturbations to the background geometry. We calculate the QNM frequencies for the classical case (for comparison) and for cases where the scale-dependent parameter $\varepsilon$, which quantifies deviations from General Relativity, is non-zero. The results are summarized in a series of tables and figures. Finally, we find that, regardless of the perturbation type, the solutions are stable, as the imaginary part of each QNM frequency is negative.

</details>


### [187] [Nonlinear Scalarization of Reissner-Nordström Black Holes in Scalar-Torsion-Maxwell Teleparallel Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11686)
*Joaquín Robledo,Yerko Vásquez,P. A. González*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量-挠率-麦克斯韦扭平行引力中Reissner-Nordström黑洞的非线性标量化现象，通过引入避免快子不稳定的多项式耦合函数，发现六阶耦合项可驱动标量场演化至非平凡稳态解；数值求解表明存在带标量毛的渐近平直带电黑洞解，并依据标量场节点数分类多分支解，高阶耦合抑制高节点分支，且标量反作用导致度规与挠率标量偏离RN背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在标量-挠率-麦克斯韦扭平行引力框架下，非最小耦合标量场如何通过非线性效应引发黑洞标量化，规避线性不稳定性（如快子模），拓展传统标量化机制的理解边界。

Method: 采用多项式形式的标量-Weitzenböck不变量耦合函数，在退耦极限下分析有限振幅标量扰动的非线性演化；通过构造有效势并分析其局域极小值，结合数值求解全耦合爱因斯坦-标量-麦克斯韦-挠率场方程，构建渐近平直带电黑洞的带毛解。

Result: 发现六阶耦合项可使标量场动态演化至非零稳态；获得一系列渐近平直、带电、带标量毛的黑洞解，按标量场节点数分为多个分支；高阶耦合参数增大时高节点分支被压制；标量场反作用显著改变度规函数和挠率标量分布。

Conclusion: 非线性标量化是扭平行引力中一种可行的黑洞自发对称破缺机制，其解结构丰富且可控，为检验修改引力理论及黑洞无毛定理提供了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate the nonlinear scalarization of Reissner-Nordström black holes in scalar-torsion-Maxwell teleparallel gravity, in which the scalar field is nonminimally coupled to the Weitzenböck invariant. We consider polynomial coupling functions that avoid tachyonic instabilities at the linear level, thereby exploring a scalarization mechanism driven by nonlinear effects. In the decoupling limit, we study the nonlinear evolution of finite-amplitude scalar perturbations and show that the inclusion of a sixth-order coupling term allows the scalar field to dynamically evolve toward nontrivial stationary configurations. This behavior can be understood in terms of the effective potential, which develops nontrivial local minima that provide a mechanism for nonlinear saturation. By numerically solving the fully coupled field equations, we construct asymptotically flat charged black hole solutions endowed with scalar hair. We classify these solutions according to the number of nodes in the scalar field profile, identifying multiple branches whose structure and multiplicity depend on the coupling parameters. We further show that the higher-order coupling progressively suppresses higher-node branches and characterize the scalar backreaction through deviations of the metric functions and the torsion scalar from the Reissner-Nordström background.

</details>


### [188] [Efficiency enhancement in the Unruh quantum Otto engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11917)
*Yongjie Pan,Baocheng Zhang,Qingyu Cai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由两个初始无纠缠的Unruh-DeWitt探测器构成的Unruh量子奥托热机（UQOE）的效率提升机制，发现即使热吸收阶段后系统处于可分态，效率仍可提升，表明纠缠并非效率提升的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Unruh量子热机中效率提升的物理机制，特别是纠缠与效率增强之间的关系。

Method: 构建具有不同加速度的两个UDW探测器组成的UQOE模型，采用对角自由哈密顿量，以其中一个探测器的固有时定义功和热；分析相对固有时因子、循环操作点及探测器间距对效率的影响。

Result: 识别出满足初态被动性、循环闭合与能量守恒的循环工作点，其效率高于传统UQOE；热吸收后系统为可分态；探测器间距增大削弱纠缠但影响非局域相干性及循环闭合条件。

Conclusion: UQOE可在热吸收后保持可分态的同时实现效率增强，证明纠缠生成不是效率提升的必要条件。

Abstract: We investigate the efficiency enhancement of an Unruh quantum Otto engine (UQOE) composed of two initially unentangled Unruh-DeWitt (UDW) detectors. The two detectors have different accelerations and interact with a massless scalar field in the Minkowski vacuum. We choose a diagonal free Hamiltonian for the UQOE and define its work and heat with respect to the proper time of one of the detectors. Consequently, the coherence terms generated through the interaction with the scalar field do not directly contribute to the work and heat of the UQOE. Instead, the efficiency enhancement arises from the relative proper-time factor, which assigns different weights to the population changes of the two detectors. We then identify cyclic operating points that satisfy the conditions of initial-state passivity, cycle closure, and energy conservation. Their efficiency is higher than that of a conventional UQOE, and the state of the UQOE after the heat-absorption stage is separable. Finally, we study the effect of the separation between the two detectors on the engine efficiency. During the interaction of the two detectors with the field, increasing their separation reduces the generated entanglement; however, this separation enters the nonlocal coherence terms, thereby restricting the range of parameter settings compatible with cycle closure and influencing the achievable efficiency enhancement. Our results demonstrate that the UQOE can achieve enhanced efficiency even when its state after the heat-absorption stroke is separable, showing that entanglement generation is not a necessary condition for efficiency enhancement.

</details>


### [189] [Numerical-Relativity Particle-in-Cell Simulations: Self-Gravitating Collisionless Matter in Dynamical Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12831)
*Jiaxi Wu,Elias R. Most*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的数值相对论粒子-网格（NRPIC）框架，用于求解爱因斯坦-维拉斯诺夫系统，以全动力学方式模拟碰撞less相对论物质与动态时空的耦合演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为研究引力坍缩、大尺度分离系统中的黑洞动力学及极值黑洞形成等问题，需要高精度、自适应的非线性数值工具。

Method: 基于高性能可移植代码AthenaK，构建三维NRPIC框架，将粒子动力学与Z4c形式的移动刺破规范下时空演化耦合，并通过守恒应力-能量沉积于自适应加密网格；引入类Boris粒子推进器，利用测地线力的电动力学（洛伦兹力）形式。

Result: 成功验证了球对称尘埃坍缩、椭球坍缩穿越碰撞less奇点形成黑洞、相对论星团的稳定性与非线性迁移；并模拟了双过密区在共同碰撞less包络中的坍缩、黑洞并合及并合后吸积过程。

Conclusion: 该NRPIC框架为广义相对论中引力不稳定性及复杂黑洞动力学的全非线性研究提供了强大而灵活的新工具。

Abstract: Gravitational collapse and instability, black-hole dynamics in systems with large scale separations, and questions surrounding extremal black hole formation require full nonlinear studies using accurate and adapted numerical tools. To this end, we present a new algorithm to study collisionless relativistic systems using a fully kinetic description of matter coupled to the evolving spacetime. In detail, we present a numerical-relativity particle-in-cell (NRPIC) framework for solving the Einstein--Vlasov system in three spatial dimensions. Implemented on top of the performance-portable \texttt{AthenaK} code, the method couples particle dynamics to Z4c spacetime evolution in moving puncture gauge through conservative stress-energy deposition on adaptively refined meshes. Our implementation makes use of advances in the computational modeling of relativistic plasmas using modern PIC models, now made available to general-relativistic gravity via an equivalent electrodynamic formulation. We introduce a Boris-type particle pusher that exploits the electrodynamic (Lorentz force) form of the geodesic force. We validate the coupled evolution against spherical dust collapse, follow prolate collapse through collisionless caustics into black-hole formation, and investigate the stability and nonlinear migration of relativistic clusters. Highlighting its potential to study gravitational instability in NR, we simulate the collapse of two overdensities within a common collisionless envelope, following black-hole formation, binary inspiral and merger, and subsequent mass accretion onto the remnant.

</details>


### [190] [Single and repetitive Penrose processes in genuine uncharged Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11943)
*Ruo-Cen Wu,Hanjun Zou,Meng Xu,Yun-Long Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞中Penrose过程的解析框架，区分了坐标类时Killling能量与热力学质量生成能量，通过多支参数映射关联粒子衰变、径向输运、热力学态与几何重构；给出了赤道面内粒子在能心点衰变的解析解及六项物理约束；数值结果表明外磁场增强会降低能量回报率和旋转能利用效率，但提高热力学消耗率；单次提取存在磁场上限，重复提取终因轨道可及性限制而终止，黑洞最终保留大部分初始转动能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在BR电磁真空背景下的KBR0黑洞中，缺乏统一框架来协调Penrose过程中的粒子动力学、热力学与时空几何；需厘清不同能量定义（如Killling能量 vs 热力学质量生成能）并建立从热力学态到度规参数的映射。

Method: 构建基于Killing矢量与热力学质量生成器的能量分离框架；推导赤道面内中性粒子在径向转向点衰变的解析解；提出六项物理约束以保证完整能量提取事件；实施多支参数映射与数值扫描分析η_EROI、η_EUE、η_thermo随磁场和自旋的变化。

Result: 获得Penrose过程在KBR0时空中的完整解析解与轨道可及性判据；发现外磁场增强导致能量提取效率下降、热力学消耗率上升；确定单次提取的磁场可行域上界由母粒子下落受阻和俘获碎片负能丢失双重机制决定；重复提取必然终止于局部斜率失效或远场入射支闭合；黑洞演化沿与比能无关的公共积分曲线进行。

Conclusion: Penrose过程在强电磁背景（BR真空）的KBR0黑洞中虽仍可行，但其能量提取效率显著受限于外磁场；热力学与几何的耦合必须通过区分能量源本质来刻画；重复提取无法耗尽黑洞转动能，揭示了该类时空内在的稳定性与能量约束机制。

Abstract: We establish an analytical framework for the Penrose process in the genuine uncharged Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson (KBR0) black-hole spacetime within a Bertotti-Robinson (BR) electromagnetic vacuum, consistently linking particle decay, radial transport, thermodynamic states, and geometric reconstruction by distinguishing coordinate Killing energy from thermodynamic mass-generator energy and performing multibranch parameter mapping from thermodynamic states to background metric parameters. Furthermore, we present the analytical solution for the decay of equatorial massive neutral particles at a common radial turning point inside the ergoregion, the orbital accessibility criteria, and the six physical constraints required for a complete extraction event. Numerical calculations demonstrate that for both near-extremal and lower-spin inputs, in both single and repetitive Penrose processes, the net energy return on investment ($η_{\text{EROI}}$) and the rotational energy utilization efficiency ($η_{\text{EUE}}$) decrease with increasing external magnetic field, whereas the rotational energy reservoir consumption rate per unit incident energy ($η_{\text{thermo}}$) increases. For the inputs considered, the feasible domain and the upper bound of the initial magnetic field for single extraction are constrained by two boundary mechanisms: the obstruction of parent-particle infall and the loss of negative energy in the captured fragment. Finally, for repetitive extraction processes within the surveyed regime, all sequences terminate due to orbital accessibility obstructions, explicitly local-slope failure and far-field incident-branch closure, with the final black-hole state retaining most of its initial rotational energy reservoir. Moreover, the black-hole state evolves along a common integral curve independent of the parent particle's specific energy, which solely alters the sequence endpoint.

</details>


### [191] [Deviations from Kerr: Critical curves and photon-ring critical parameters for general observer inclinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12050)
*Hao-Peng Yan,Xiang-Qian Li,Xiao-Jun Yue*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有可分离类光测地线和Kerr角结构的稳态轴对称时空中的临界曲线和光子环临界参数，通过Kerr相对分解方法，得到了观测者在任意倾角下这些可观测量的一阶解析偏离，并验证了其在典型度规和数值计算中的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强引力场中黑洞阴影和光子环的观测特征，检验广义相对论及替代引力理论；需发展适用于一般轴对称、非Kerr时空的解析分析工具。

Method: 采用Kerr参考几何下的径向度规函数相对分解，推导临界曲线法向位移、去放大指数、时延与方位角进动的一阶解析表达式；利用光学度规偏差构造显式组合量，并在观测屏上统一位置角处评估各量；结合内在关系分析光学信息共享与径向不稳定性影响。

Result: 得到临界曲线法向位移因子化形式 $\tilde u_n=\tilde F_{\mathrm K}\mathscr C$；揭示临界曲线、时延与方位角进动间存在精确内在关系，而去放大指数额外依赖径向不稳定性；在多个代表性非Kerr度规中验证了一阶预测与精确有限变形轮廓及独立数值结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 该一阶解析框架稳健可靠，适用于常规、可观测的外部光子壳分支，为未来EHT等观测区分Kerr与非Kerr时空提供了实用且物理清晰的理论工具。

Abstract: We study critical curves and photon-ring critical parameters in stationary, axisymmetric spacetimes with separable null geodesics and Kerr angular structure. A Kerr-relative decomposition of the radial metric functions gives analytic first-order departures of these observables for observers at general inclination. The critical curve's signed normal displacement factorizes as $\tilde u_n=\tilde F_{\mathrm K}\mathscr C$, where $\tilde F_{\mathrm K}$ depends only on the Kerr reference geometry and $\mathscr C$ is an explicit combination of optical metric deviations. The demagnification exponent, time delay, and azimuthal advance are evaluated at a common position angle on the observer screen. Intrinsic relations among the exact observables express the optical information shared by the curve, time delay, and azimuthal advance, while demagnification additionally depends on radial instability. Applications to representative metrics illustrate how different radial deformations produce distinct departures from Kerr around the critical curve. Comparisons with exact finite-deformation profiles and independent numerical calculations verify the first-order predictions on regular, accessible exterior photon-shell branches.

</details>


### [192] [Next-to-next-to-leading gravitational radiation-reaction corrections to quasi-elliptic and quasi-hyperbolic compact binary orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12093)
*Donato Bini,Luc Blanchet,Guillaume Faye,Andrea Geralico,Quentin Henry,Sara Rufrano Aliberti,Emeric Seraille*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文使用拉格朗日常数变分法计算了致密双星系统在4.5PN阶（即辐射反作用的下下领头阶）的辐射反作用修正，涵盖准椭圆与准双曲线轨道，并验证了两种情形下局域效应结果的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确建模致密双星系统的引力波辐射反作用效应，尤其在高阶后牛顿（PN）精度下，对引力波数据解析至关重要；4.5PN阶包含关键的非局域效应（如尾项和反冲），需系统处理。

Method: 采用拉格朗日常数变分法，在调和坐标系下，基于最新得到的4.5PN阶辐射反作用加速度（通用参考系及质心系），分别求解准椭圆与准双曲线轨道的扰动方程，并细致评估4PN尾项与4.5PN引力波反冲引起的非局域效应。

Result: 得到了4.5PN阶辐射反作用对准椭圆与准双曲线轨道的完整修正；通过解析延拓验证了两种轨道下局域效应结果的等价性；明确了非局域效应（尾项与反冲）的贡献形式与大小。

Conclusion: 4.5PN阶辐射反作用的局域部分在准椭圆与准双曲线轨道间具有一致性，表明该阶理论具有内在自洽性；非局域效应虽计算方式不同，但可被系统纳入统一框架，为高精度引力波波形建模提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: We apply the Lagrange method of variation of constants to compute the radiation-reaction (RR) corrections to the quasi-elliptic and quasi-hyperbolic motion of compact binary systems at 4.5PN order, i.e., at next-to-next-to-leading order in the RR. We rely on the recent determination of the 4.5PN RR acceleration, in a general frame and in the center-of-mass frame, in harmonic coordinates. The non-local effects, due to the tails at 4PN order and to the gravitational-wave recoil at 4.5PN order, are carefully evaluated. Although the calculation is implemented differently in the quasi-elliptic and quasi-hyperbolic cases (different perturbation equations and choices of orbital parameters), we verify by analytic continuation that the results for the local effects are equivalent in the two cases.

</details>


### [193] [Direct product geometries in $n$-dimensional linear, quadratic, and conformal gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12483)
*Petr Jizba,Tereza Lehečková*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性引力、纯二次曲率引力及高维共形引力中n维直积时空的精确解，分类了不同引力理论下的解空间，并分析了几何结构对理论参数的依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找广义相对论之外引力理论中的精确解，直积时空提供了一种可处理的途径。

Method: 系统研究了(m1+m2)、(2+m)和(2+…+2)型直积时空在多种引力理论中的解，考虑二维子空间具有非常数曲率的情形，并进行分类与共形关系、Petrov类型及电磁源兼容性分析。

Result: 发现含非常数曲率二维子空间的解由代数条件或微分方程控制；仅含常曲率子空间的解满足纯代数关系；标准静态黑洞几何在n>4的该类共形引力直积解中不存在；给出了四维和六维的具体解析解。

Conclusion: 直积结构下解的存在性与形式强烈依赖于引力理论类型及其耦合参数，共形引力在高维中显著限制了黑洞类解的出现。

Abstract: Direct-product spacetimes offer a tractable route to exact solutions in gravity theories beyond general relativity. We study $n$-dimensional direct-product spacetimes of $(m_1+m_2)$, $(2+m)$, and $(2+\ldots+2)$ type in linear gravity, general pure quadratic-curvature gravity, and one of the recently proposed higher-dimensional extensions of conformal gravity, allowing the two-dimensional subspaces to have nonconstant curvature. We classify the resulting solution spaces and determine how the allowed geometries depend on the gravitational theory and, in quadratic gravity, on the curvature couplings. Metrics containing two-dimensional part(s) of nonconstant curvature are governed either by algebraic conditions on a curvature profile or, in one special class of quadratic gravity, by a differential equation. Relations governing metrics with only constant-curvature $m$-dimensional subspaces are purely algebraic. We further examine conformal relations to linear-gravity solutions, generalized Petrov types, and compatibility with electromagnetic sources. Particularly noteworthy is that standard static black-hole geometries are absent from the direct-product sector of the considered form of conformal gravity for $n>4$. Four- and six-dimensional cases are analyzed in detail, and explicit analytic solutions illustrating the resulting structure are presented whenever possible.

</details>


### [194] [Gravity Without Absolutes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12489)
*Julian Barbour,Maria I. R. Lourenço*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了物理学中还原论与整体论的范式转换，指出牛顿引力理论在绝对时空框架下存在无限任意解的问题，并提出莱布尼茨的充分理由原则（PSR）可消除这种任意性，进而导向一个粒子数无限、相关性取代因果性的整体性宇宙图景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近半个世纪物理学缺乏重大突破，且主流范式存在未解难题，促使作者反思还原论的局限性，探索以整体论为新路径的可行性。

Method: 以牛顿第二定律为核心，在还原论的绝对时空框架下分析N体宇宙解的任意性；引入莱布尼茨的充分理由原则（PSR）加以约束，并进一步追问宇宙粒子数的本体论依据。

Result: PSR消除了N体宇宙模型中的无限任意性，预言所有N体宇宙都具有意外丰富的结构；进一步推导出宇宙粒子数必为无穷；在此基础上，相关性可替代因果性，而表观的动力学演化可能仅源于当下状态的内在自洽。

Conclusion: 还原论在基础物理中面临根本挑战，而基于PSR的整体论进路不仅逻辑更简洁，还为解决当前理论困境提供了新方向，有力支持了整体论作为未来物理学范式的合理性。

Abstract: By formulating his theory of universal gravitation in absolute space and time, Newton demonstrated the power of reductionism; it has been behind every major advance in physics since then. However, the lack of significant advances over the last half-century, together with unresolved problems in the dominant paradigms of physics and cosmology, suggest that holism may need to replace reductionism as the way forward. The ontological simplicity and historical significance of Newtonian gravity makes it the ideal theory for examining this possibility. Taking Newton's second law of motion to be the core of his theory, we show that its use within the reductionist framework of absolute space and time introduces an infinity of arbitrary solutions if used to describe $N$-body universes rather than subsystems within them. We show that Leibniz's principle of sufficient reason (PSR) eliminates the infinite arbitrariness and transforms the predicted nature of $N$-body universes; they are all shown to be unexpectedly interesting. An even greater change is enforced if one asks what principle could determine the number of particles in the universe. The PSR suggests it can only be infinite. We suggest that, in such a universe, correlation could replace causation while still giving the appearance that each state has been created by dynamical evolution from the past, whereas its actual explanation may lie entirely within the present state. While much work remains to develop these ideas into a viable theory, we are confident that it advances the case for holism.

</details>


### [195] [Thermodynamics of Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson black holes from background subtraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12683)
*Chang-Xu Yan,Zili Mou,Yu Tian,Hongbao Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文解决了在Bertotti-Robinson磁场中带电旋转黑洞因非渐近平直性导致的时间生成元和视界电势规范选择模糊性问题，通过新建立的可积性条件与渐近平直黑洞规范作为初值，唯一确定了规范，并利用闵氏时空为参考背景计算了质量与角动量，从而自动满足第一定律、CR关系与Smarr公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非渐近平直的带电旋转黑洞在Bertotti-Robinson磁场中存在时间生成元和视界电势的规范选择模糊性，需明确其物理定义以构建一致的热力学框架。

Method: 采用新建立的可积性条件，结合渐近平直黑洞的规范作为初始条件来唯一固定规范；再用背景减除法（以闵氏时空为参考）计算质量与角动量。

Result: 成功唯一确定了规范；导出了质量与角动量；第一定律、CR关系与Smarr公式均被严格满足。

Conclusion: 非渐近平直背景下黑洞热力学量的定义可通过适当规范固定与背景选择得以自洽，拓展了黑洞热力学在强场环境中的适用性。

Abstract: The non-asymptotic flatness of charged rotating black holes immersed in the Bertotti-Robinson magnetic field causes the ambiguity in the gauge choice for the time generator and the horizon electric potential. By using the newly established form of the integrability condition together with the gauge choice for the asymptotically flat charged rotating black holes as the initial condition, we have found that the gauge can be uniquely fixed. With this fixed gauge, we further obtain the mass and angular momentum by the background subtraction method with the Minkowski spacetime as the reference spacetime. As a result, not only is the first law of black hole thermodynamics automatically satisfied, but also both the CR relation and the Smarr formula are obeyed.

</details>


### [196] [Scalar quasinormal modes of the exact spinning Morris--Thorne wormhole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12838)
*Mark Sukaiti,Davide Batic,Denys Dutykh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在旋转Morris-Thorne虫洞上无质量标量场的准正规模（QNMs），通过二维切比雪夫配置法构造二次特征值问题，发现频率谱具有镜像对称性，并受超辐射窗口约束；所有计算出的模式均为阻尼模式且位于该窗口外，大mj极限下频率趋近窗口上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解旋转虫洞时空中的标量场动力学行为及其稳定性，特别是准正规模谱的结构与超辐射效应的关系。

Method: 基于精确旋转Morris-Thorne虫洞度规，提取轴向行为和两渐近区的出射相位，紧致化径向坐标，利用二维切比雪夫配置法构建二次特征值问题；结合解析背景约束、独立数值积分与Kerr谱验证。

Result: 发现频率\widehat{ω}=r_0ω关于Re\widehat{ω}=3πjm/2镜像对称；各极次族仅依赖mj组合；角向用三个切比雪夫多项式精确表示L=|m|,|m|+1,|m|+2；所有收敛的QNMs均为阻尼模式且落在超辐射窗口外；大mj下频率趋近窗口上界，偏差∝(mj)^{-1/2}。

Conclusion: 当前标量场扰动显示虫洞在所考察参数范围内稳定（无增长模），但完整稳定性需进一步分析未解谱及度规-物质耦合扰动系统。

Abstract: We study the quasinormal modes (QNMs) of a minimally coupled massless scalar field on the exact spinning Morris-Thorne wormhole. The problem is posed on the complete two-ended geometry, whose asymptotic rest frames rotate relative to each other. After extracting the axis behaviour and the outgoing phases at both infinities, we compactify the radial coordinate and construct a quadratic eigenvalue problem by two-dimensional Chebyshev collocation. The analytic background yields exact constraints on the spectrum. At fixed azimuthal number $m$, the frequencies $\widehatω=r_0ω$ are mirror symmetric about $\operatorname{Re}\widehatω=3πjm/2$, where $r_0$ is the throat radius and $j=J/r_0^2$ is the dimensionless angular momentum. Each multipole family depends on $m$ and $j$ only through $mj$ and three angular Chebyshev polynomials represent exactly the multipoles $L=|m|,|m|+1,|m|+2$. A conserved flux confines nonthreshold real quasinormal frequencies to the superradiant window $0<ω/(mΩ_-)<1$, where $Ω_-=3πJ/r_0^3$. For the sampled sectors $m=1,2,3$ and spins up to $j=19$, every converged frequency is damped and lies outside this window. Independent integration verifies the low-lying modes, while Kerr spectra provide a separate benchmark for the two-dimensional solver. A limiting radial equation explains why the computed mode frequencies approach the upper edge of the superradiant window with frequency offsets proportional to $(mj)^{-1/2}$ at large $mj$. A complete stability assessment requires further analysis of the unresolved scalar spectrum and of perturbations of the coupled metric-matter system.

</details>


### [197] [Beyond $Λ$CDM: Probing Structure Growth and Observational Viability of Generalized ${f\,(Q , \mathcal{L}_{m})}$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12839)
*Raj Kumar Das,Sivasish Paul*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了f(Q, Lm)引力理论中一种广义幂律形式的加性最小耦合模型，推导了哈勃函数，并通过观测数据约束参数；发现当ν≠1时物质连续性方程不守恒，可解释为有效粒子产生机制；背景数据部分支持该模型，但加入结构增长数据（fσ₈）后强烈倾向于ν→1，即退化为标准ΛCDM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展f(Q)引力至f(Q, Lm)框架，探究非最小/加性最小耦合对宇宙学演化（尤其是背景与结构增长）的影响，并检验其相对于ΛCDM的合理性。

Method: 构建广义幂律形式f(Q,Lm) = −Q^μ + αLm^ν + β；推导含非守恒项的背景与线性扰动方程（含Euler与连续性方程）；采用OHD、DESI DR2 BAO及三组SNe Ia数据联合拟合参数；利用fσ₈探针分析结构增长；通过贝叶斯证据对比ΛCDM。

Result: 背景数据在部分情况下支持该模型（中等贝叶斯证据），但引入fσ₈后ν被强约束趋近于1，额外自由度被显著排除；非守恒情形（ν≠1）对应有效粒子产生机制。

Conclusion: 仅依赖背景观测可能误导对修改引力模型的支持程度；结构增长数据（如fσ₈）对甄别真实物理自由度至关重要；当前f(Q,Lm)广义幂律模型在综合数据下未显示出超越ΛCDM的优势。

Abstract: $f(Q, \mathcal{L}_m)$ gravity is a well-known extension of $f(Q)$ gravity, obtained through non-minimal or additive minimal coupling in the action's Lagrangian density. Background-level cosmography within this framework, along with comparisons to $Λ$CDM, has been the focus of prior studies. The present work considers a generalized power-law form of the additive minimal coupling, $f(Q,\mathcal{L}_m) = -Q^μ+ α\mathcal{L}_m^{\,ν} + β$, from which the Hubble function $H(z)$ is derived through a novel parametrization. When $ν\neq 1$, the matter continuity equation is rendered non-conserved, a feature that can be interpreted as an effective particle-creation mechanism. Constraints on the model parameters are obtained from Observational Hubble Data, DESI DR2 BAO measurements, and three SNe Ia compilations (Pantheon+, Union3, DES-SN5YR), allowing their cosmographic effects to be examined. Beyond the background level, the perturbed field, Euler, and continuity equations are derived under non-conservation, and their impact on structure growth is studied through a joint analysis incorporating the $fσ_8$ probe. Bayesian evidence, computed relative to $Λ$CDM, shows that the model is favoured by background data in several cases, with moderate support for one realisation, whereas the inclusion of $fσ_8$ drives $ν\to1$ and decisively disfavours the additional freedom, highlighting the importance of growth data in testing such extensions.

</details>


### [198] [Mapping of Spacetimes with Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12857)
*Estuti Shukla,David Radice,Rossella Gamba,Sebastiano Bernuzzi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经网络的数据驱动方法，用于从数值相对论模拟的度规数据中学习到目标规范（如Eddington-Finkelstein或Kerr）的坐标映射，无需预设变换形式；在Schwarzschild/Kerr解析解上验证有效，并成功应用于移动穿刺法数值黑洞演化，实现了对非自转/自转黑洞的近似背景识别与曲率扰动建模，所得波形与非线性模拟高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值相对论中规范自由导致坐标解释困难、不同模拟间难以比较，亟需一种不依赖先验函数形式、能自动学习坐标映射的方法，以桥接数值解与解析背景。

Method: 构建神经网络直接从数值度规数据学习到目标规范（如ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein或Kerr）的坐标变换；训练目标为使变换后的源度规逼近目标度规；未假设变换的具体函数形式；在解析解上预验证，再应用于移动穿刺法数值模拟。

Result: 在Schwarzschild/Kerr解析解上成功恢复已知坐标变换（至目标时空等距自由度）；对非自转数值黑洞，学到的映射将时变度规映射至静态Schwarzschild（EF坐标）；对自转穿刺，实现对Kerr背景的近似识别，并基于此构造曲率扰动、用Teukolsky方程演化，所得波形与全非线性模拟高度吻合。

Conclusion: 所提神经网络方法可有效学习数值时空到解析背景的坐标映射，为跨代码验证、黑洞微扰理论及波形建模提供了新工具，证实了数据驱动规范校准的可行性与实用性。

Abstract: Gauge freedom in numerical relativity complicates the interpretation of simulation coordinates and the comparison of different descriptions of the same spacetime. We present a data-driven neural network method for learning coordinate maps from numerical metric data to a prescribed target gauge. The network is trained by matching the transformed source metric to the target metric, without assuming a functional form for the coordinate transformation. We first validate the method on analytical Schwarzschild and Kerr geometries, recovering known transformations up to the isometries of the target spacetime. We then apply it to numerical black hole evolutions in moving-puncture coordinates. For a non-spinning black hole, the learned map relates the time-dependent numerical metric to a stationary Schwarzschild metric in ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein coordinates. For a spinning puncture, the method provides an approximate identification with a Kerr background despite departures from Kerr in the numerical spacetime. We use this identification to construct curvature perturbations and evolve them with the Teukolsky equation, obtaining waveforms that closely match those extracted from the full nonlinear simulation. These results demonstrate that learned coordinate maps can relate numerical spacetimes to analytical backgrounds, with potential applications to cross-code validation and black hole perturbation theory.
